/*
 * ================================================================
 *  SURGE PROTECTOR v1.1.0
 *  AI Dungeon Power & Spell Management Engine
 * ================================================================
 *  SCRIPT: LIBRARY (complete replacement)
 *  STATUS: v1.0 initial release; Mode 2 natural-language guidance is advisory
 *  RELEASE: v1.0 playable baseline; tier references and guarded migration
 *
 *  COMMANDS:
 *    #GAME MODE 1       Explicit #ability activation (default)
 *    #GAME MODE 2       AI-led natural-language guidance (advisory)
 *    #GAME MODE STATUS  Show active mode
 *
 *  QUICK NAVIGATION (Ctrl+F):
 *    INPUT SYNTAX        spMode1ParseInput / spCommandParse
 *    REGISTRATION        spExecCommand / spPowerFromPreset
 *    AUTHORIZATION       spMode1Authorize / spEffectLedger
 *    CONTEXT HINTS       spM2FindCandidates / spM2BuildContextHints
 *    STORYTELLER GUIDE   spM2BuildCardGuide
 *    PLAYER DOCS         spSyncReferenceCards (zero triggers)
 *
 *  This is one of FOUR matching v1.1.0 script tabs.
 *  Character-registered powers and limits are authoritative.
 *  Reference compendium cards cannot grant additional powers.
 *  Mode 2 candidate hints are advisory, not guaranteed enforcement.
 *  Existing adventure power registrations are preserved.
 * ================================================================
 */

const SP_VERSION = '1.1.0';
// Internal saved-state schema; this is NOT a separate product release version.
const SP_STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION = '0.8.4';

/** Emit one diagnostic line in the existing [SP][SECTION] format. No state changes. */
function spLog(part, message) {
  console.log('[SP][' + part + '] ' + message);
}

/** Create/migrate state.surgeProtector; preserve registered powers on upgrades. Side effects: modifies state. */
function spInit() {
  var sp = state.surgeProtector;
  if (!sp || typeof sp !== 'object' || Array.isArray(sp)) {
    sp = {
      version: SP_STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
      turns: 0, inputRuns: 0, contextRuns: 0, outputRuns: 0,
      generationTurns: 0, lastInput: null,
      pendingAssessment: null, lastAssessment: null, pendingProbe: null,
      pendingClass: null, lastClassification: null,
      classResults: { valid: 0, recovered: 0, missing: 0, malformed: 0, duplicate: 0 },
      probeResults: { first: 0, misplaced: 0, missing: 0 },
      assessments: { valid: 0, missing: 0, malformed: 0, duplicate: 0, stray: 0 }
    };
    state.surgeProtector = sp;
    spLog('INIT', 'Created state.surgeProtector schema ' + SP_STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION);
  } else if (sp.version !== SP_STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
    var old = sp.version || 'unknown';
    sp.turns = typeof sp.turns === 'number' ? sp.turns : 0;
    sp.inputRuns = typeof sp.inputRuns === 'number' ? sp.inputRuns : sp.turns;
    sp.contextRuns = typeof sp.contextRuns === 'number' ? sp.contextRuns : 0;
    sp.outputRuns = typeof sp.outputRuns === 'number' ? sp.outputRuns : 0;
    sp.generationTurns = sp.contextRuns;
    sp.pendingAssessment = null;
    sp.pendingProbe = null;
    sp.pendingClass = null;
    sp.classResults = sp.classResults && typeof sp.classResults === 'object'
      ? sp.classResults : { valid: 0, recovered: 0, missing: 0, malformed: 0, duplicate: 0 };
    if (typeof sp.classResults.recovered !== 'number') sp.classResults.recovered = 0;
    sp.probeResults = sp.probeResults && typeof sp.probeResults === 'object'
      ? sp.probeResults : { first: 0, misplaced: 0, missing: 0 };
    // lastAssessment is historical only in probe mode; do not overwrite it.

    // Preserve v0.2 assessment totals across a patch upgrade for test history.
    if (!sp.assessments || typeof sp.assessments !== 'object') {
      sp.assessments = { valid: 0, missing: 0, malformed: 0, duplicate: 0, stray: 0 };
    }
    sp.version = SP_STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION;
    spLog('MIGRATE', 'state.surgeProtector schema ' + old + ' -> ' + SP_STATE_SCHEMA_VERSION +
      ' kept inputTurns=' + sp.inputRuns + ' generationTurns=' + sp.generationTurns);
  }
  return sp;
}

// UX-01A: two player-facing documentation Story Cards. These have no trigger keys,
// remain unpinned, and must NEVER be injected by Surge Protector into model context.
// AI Dungeon supports names/notes as optional addStoryCard/updateStoryCard args
// (Aug 2025 patch). Older versions may reject a second card with identical keys;
// create using a transient unique key and immediately update to empty in that case.
var SP_DOC_TYPE = 'SurgeProtector';
var SP_DOC_MARKERS = {
  commands: '[SurgeProtector managed reference:commands]',
  instructions: '[SurgeProtector managed reference:instructions]'
};

function spDocCardContent(kind) {
  if (kind === 'commands') return [
    'SURGE PROTECTOR COMMANDS  |  ' + SP_VERSION,
    'PLAYER REFERENCE ONLY — this card has no triggers and is not pinned.',
    '',
    'GENERAL',
    '#POWER HELP — quick command help.',
    '#POWER LIST — registered powers.   #SPELL LIST — registered spells.',
    '#POWER DETAILS <name/trigger> — current effect, limits and exclusions.',
    '#POWER CATALOG — names of built-in power presets (25); reference profiles are separate.',
    '#POWER LEVELS Telepathy — each level shows what it can do, caps, and exclusions.',
    'Power tier examples reflect the bound ability: different power families scale differently.',
    '#SPELL CATALOG [0-9] — spell index; optional D&D spell rank.',
    'Spell entries show concise effects; all 100 indexed SRD 2014 spells have playable rules-guidance profiles.',
    '#SPELL DETAILS <name> — effect, usage examples, cast-rank scaling, and exclusions.',
    '',
    'POWERS',
    '#POWER ADD <preset name> <level 1-10> — register/update a built-in power.',
    '#POWER CUSTOM <name> <level 1-10> | <description> — other powers, including Minor Healing.',
    '#POWER REMOVE <name/trigger> — deregister a power.',
    '#POWER LIMIT <trigger> RANGE <number>ft — change range cap.',
    '#POWER LIMIT <trigger> MASS <number>lb — change mass cap.',
    'Examples: #POWER ADD Cryokinesis 2',
    '          #POWER CUSTOM Shadow Step 5 | Teleport through visible shadows within 30 ft',
    '          #POWER LIMIT tk RANGE 30ft',
    '',
    'SPELLS — 100 SRD spell rules-guidance profiles (limitations are advisory in Mode 2).',
    '#SPELL ADD <name> [cast rank] — register a indexed D&D 2014 spell.',
    '#SPELL REMOVE <name/trigger> — deregister a spell.',
    'Example: #SPELL ADD Fireball 5',
    '',
    'GAMEPLAY MODES',
    '#GAME MODE 1 — Manual Activation; use #<power-trigger> <action>.',
    '#GAME MODE 2 — Natural Language; describe powers normally (advisory).',
    '#GAME MODE STATUS — show mode.   #POWER CACHE ON|OFF|STATUS — precedent hints.',
    'Alternate formats: #power_list, #list powers, #list_powers, #powers, #spell_list.',
    'Mode 1 spell upcasting: #<spell-trigger>:<cast rank> <action>.',
    '',
    'All commands are entered as player actions, one at a time.',
    'This reference is generated by Surge Protector; the registry controls permissions.'
  ].join('\n');
  return [
    'SURGE PROTECTOR INSTRUCTIONS  |  ' + SP_VERSION,
    'PLAYER REFERENCE ONLY — this card has no triggers and is not pinned.',
    '',
    'QUICK START',
    '#GAME DOCS — create/repair the two player-reference Story Cards.',
    '1. Install matching Library, Input, Context and Output script tabs.',
    '2. Take one turn: the two SurgeProtector reference cards are created automatically.',
    '3. Type #POWER CATALOG to browse built-in powers (reference cards are not presets).',
    '4. Type #POWER ADD Cryokinesis 2 (example).',
    '5. Preview levels first: #POWER LEVELS Cryokinesis, then inspect #POWER LIST and #POWER DETAILS cryokinesis.',
    '6. Choose your mode with #GAME MODE 1 or #GAME MODE 2.',
    '',
    'MODE 1 — MANUAL ACTIVATION (default)',
    'Type the registered trigger before an action:',
    '  #cryokinesis You coat the scales with frost.',
    'An ordinary action without #trigger does not activate innate powers.',
    'Scripted authorization is stronger in this mode, within implemented mechanics.',
    '',
    'MODE 2 — NATURAL LANGUAGE',
    'Describe an action normally: You coat the scales with frost.',
    'The script suggests registered powers and their limits to the AI narrator.',
    'This is advisory; the narrator can still make mistakes.',
    '',
    'IMPORTANT RULES',
    'Only registered abilities are authorized. Story history and reference cards',
    'do not grant powers. A registered power cannot borrow unrelated effects.',
    'Power levels are 1 to 10; D&D spell ranks are a separate 0 to 9 scale.',
    'Telepathy includes mental speech from level 1; unwilling probing is a separate higher-tier use.',
    'For custom powers, write clear effects AND limits in the description.',
    'No numeric limit shown does not imply unlimited ability.',
    'Equipment and ordinary actions are not automatically supernatural powers.',
    '',
    'WHEN SOMETHING GOES WRONG',
    'Check #POWER LIST, #POWER DETAILS <trigger> and #GAME MODE STATUS.',
    'Confirm all four script tabs are from the same version.',
    'Read the SurgeProtector Commands card for every supported command.',
    'Documentation cards contain no triggers and should remain unpinned.',
    'They are for the PLAYER; they do not activate in storyteller context.'
  ].join('\n');
}

/** Find only our clearly marked cards; never touch unrelated player-created cards. */
function spFindManagedDocCard(kind) {
  if (typeof storyCards === 'undefined' || !Array.isArray(storyCards)) return -1;
  var marker = SP_DOC_MARKERS[kind];
  var title = kind === 'commands' ? 'SurgeProtector Commands' : 'Instructions';
  for (var i=0;i<storyCards.length;i++) {
    var c=storyCards[i];
    if (!c || c.type !== SP_DOC_TYPE || c.title !== title) continue;
    if (typeof c.description === 'string' && c.description.indexOf(marker) !== -1 ||
        typeof c.entry === 'string' && c.entry.indexOf('SURGE PROTECTOR '+kind.toUpperCase()) === 0) return i;
  }
  return -1;
}

/** Idempotent Story Card bootstrap for fresh AND existing adventures.
 * AI Dungeon may return a new index before its storyCards snapshot is refreshed.
 * Use that returned index. Never silently treat a successful add as failed.
 * Force flag enables explicit #GAME DOCS diagnostics/retry. */
function spSyncReferenceCards(sp,force) {
  var functionsReady=typeof addStoryCard==='function' && typeof updateStoryCard==='function';
  var listReady=typeof storyCards!=='undefined' && Array.isArray(storyCards);
  var summary={created:0,updated:0,failed:0,pending:0,available:functionsReady&&listReady};
  if(!summary.available) {
    summary.failed=2;
    summary.summary='Story Card functions or storyCards list unavailable in this hook';
    if(force || !sp.docsSync || sp.docsSync.version!==SP_VERSION || sp.docsSync.available!==false)
      spLog('DOCS','UNAVAILABLE add='+typeof addStoryCard+' update='+typeof updateStoryCard+
        ' storyCards='+(listReady?'array':typeof storyCards)+'; try #GAME DOCS on the next turn; check Adventure Script installation/compatibility');
    sp.docsSync={version:SP_VERSION,available:false,failed:2};
    return summary;
  }
  var kinds=['commands','instructions'];
  for(var ki=0;ki<kinds.length;ki++) {
    var kind=kinds[ki],title=kind==='commands'?'SurgeProtector Commands':'Instructions';
    var marker=SP_DOC_MARKERS[kind],entry=spDocCardContent(kind);
    var index=spFindManagedDocCard(kind),existing=index>=0;
    try {
      if(!existing){
        // Don't overwrite another script's or player's card of same title.
        if(storyCards.some(function(c){return c&&c.type===SP_DOC_TYPE&&c.title===title;})){
          summary.failed++;spLog('DOCS','COLLISION title='+title);continue;
        }
        // Empty-key creation can fail on API versions with key uniqueness;
        // fall back to a setup-only temporary trigger, removed in this hook.
        var added=addStoryCard('',entry,SP_DOC_TYPE,title,marker);
        if(typeof added==='number' && added>=0)index=added;
        else index=spFindManagedDocCard(kind);
        if(added===false || index<0){
          var temp='__SP_BOOTSTRAP_'+kind+'_'+SP_VERSION.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/ig,'_')+'__';
          added=addStoryCard(temp,entry,SP_DOC_TYPE,title,marker);
          if(typeof added==='number' && added>=0)index=added;
          else index=spFindManagedDocCard(kind);
        }
        if(index<0){summary.failed++;spLog('DOCS','CREATE_FAILED kind='+kind+' returned='+String(added));continue;}
        summary.created++;
      }
      var card=storyCards[index];
      if(!card || card.keys!=='' || card.entry!==entry || card.type!==SP_DOC_TYPE ||
         card.title!==title || card.description!==marker || card.isPinned===true) {
        // Update even if storyCards snapshot has not been hydrated yet.
        updateStoryCard(index,'',entry,SP_DOC_TYPE,title,marker);
        summary.updated++;
      }
      // Some scripting adapters ignore optional name/notes on update.
      card=storyCards[index];
      if(card){
        card.keys='';card.entry=entry;card.type=SP_DOC_TYPE;
        card.title=title;card.description=marker;card.isPinned=false;
      } else summary.pending++;
    }catch(err){
      summary.failed++;
      // Strictly avoid retaining a temporary trigger after an exception.
      if(index>=0 && storyCards[index] &&
          (storyCards[index].description===marker || storyCards[index].entry===entry)){
        storyCards[index].keys='';storyCards[index].isPinned=false;
      }
      spLog('DOCS','kind='+kind+' error='+spBrief(String(err&&err.message||err),140));
    }
  }
  var count=storyCards.filter(function(c){return c&&c.type===SP_DOC_TYPE&&c.keys==='';}).length;
  summary.summary='created='+summary.created+' updated='+summary.updated+
    ' failed='+summary.failed+' pending='+summary.pending+' visible='+count;
  var previous=sp.docsSync||{};
  if(force || summary.created || summary.updated || summary.failed ||
     previous.version!==SP_VERSION || previous.available!==true)
    spLog('DOCS','version='+SP_VERSION+' '+summary.summary);
  sp.docsSync={version:SP_VERSION,available:true,
    created:(previous.created||0)+summary.created,updated:(previous.updated||0)+summary.updated,
    failed:(previous.failed||0)+summary.failed,visible:count};
  return summary;
}

/** Read the latest history entry and its action type; Context is source of truth for Do/Say/Story. */
function spActionInfo() {
  var entries = typeof history !== 'undefined' && Array.isArray(history) ? history : [];
  var last = entries.length ? entries[entries.length - 1] : null;
  return {
    type: last && typeof last.type === 'string' ? last.type.toLowerCase() : 'unknown',
    historyLength: entries.length,
    lastHistoryText: last && typeof last.text === 'string' ? last.text : ''
  };
}

/** Only authored Do/Say/Story actions require ability inspection; Continue bypasses activation. */
function spShouldAssess(type) {
  return type === 'do' || type === 'say' || type === 'story';
}

/** Shorten a string for bounded log/context output, preserving the original elsewhere. */
function spBrief(text, limit) {
  var s = typeof text === 'string' ? text.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() : '';
  return s.length > limit ? s.slice(0, limit) + '...' : s;
}

/** Legacy classification prompt retained for compatibility with earlier semantic experiments. */
function spAssessmentPrompt(type, actionText) {
  // Model-based semantic classifier: no registry, no suppression, no enforcement.
  // Do not interpret the quoted player text as instructions about this metadata task.
  var actionSample = spBrief(actionText, 600);
  return [
    '[SURGE PROTECTOR / INTERNAL DIAGNOSTIC]',
    'Classify ONLY the most recent player-authored action; do not assess an NPC or old history.',
    'ACTION_TYPE=' + type.toUpperCase(),
    'PLAYER_ACTION=' + JSON.stringify(actionSample),
    'Baseline NORMAL: actions, speech, and attempts within ordinary human physical, sensory, mental, and learned capability. Ordinary attempts may fail; trying to lift a huge boulder is not itself a superpower.',
    'NON_NORMAL: player ATTEMPTS, ACTIVATES, or ASSERTS personally producing magic/spells, teleportation/spatial or time warping, telepathy/telekinesis, transformation, superhuman strength/speed, mutation-derived powers, regeneration, supernatural sensing, elemental control, summoning, resurrection, or similar extraordinary effects.',
    'Use NO_ACTION for discussion or speculation about powers, requests that OTHER people use powers, NPC-only magic, or environmental descriptions with no player ability used.',
    'Use UNCERTAIN only when the attempted effect cannot be determined from the text. A spoken incantation actively casting a spell is NON_NORMAL; merely quoting a spell is NO_ACTION.',
    'Equipment usage alone is outside this classifier: handling a wand or tool does not prove an innate player power.',
    'This is observation ONLY. Do NOT change the story outcome, block powers, impose penalties, or reveal this assessment in narration.',
    'Your FIRST line of generated output MUST be one assessment tag, BEFORE any narrative:',
    '[SP_ASSESS|CLASS|CATEGORY|brief reason]',
    'CLASS must be NORMAL, NON_NORMAL, UNCERTAIN, or NO_ACTION.',
    'CATEGORY must be uppercase with underscores (e.g. NONE, TELEPORTATION, TELEPATHY, MAGIC, SUPER_STRENGTH, UNKNOWN). Reason: plain text, at most 100 characters, no pipes or brackets.',
    'On the NEXT line, continue normal story narration. Do not include extra tags, prefaces, or code fences.',
    '[/SURGE PROTECTOR DIAGNOSTIC]',
    'REMINDER: FIRST line = [SP_ASSESS|CLASS|CATEGORY|brief reason]. Story begins on line 2.'
  ].join('\n');
}

/** Append script instructions under a maxChars budget, preserving memory and newest history. */
function spAddDiagnosticContext(original, instructions) {
  var suffix = '\n\n' + instructions;
  var limit = typeof info !== 'undefined' && info &&
    typeof info.maxChars === 'number' && info.maxChars > 0 ? info.maxChars : null;
  if (limit === null || original.length + suffix.length <= limit) {
    return { text: original + suffix, trimmed: 0, skipped: false };
  }
  if (suffix.length >= limit) {
    return { text: original, trimmed: 0, skipped: true };
  }
  // Preserve the beginning (Plot Essentials/memory) and the end (current action),
  // discarding older history in the middle if needed to fit the context budget.
  var memoryLen = typeof info.memoryLength === 'number' ? info.memoryLength : 0;
  memoryLen = Math.max(0, Math.min(memoryLen, original.length));
  var budget = limit - suffix.length;
  var prefixLen = Math.min(memoryLen, Math.floor(budget / 2));
  var tailLen = budget - prefixLen;
  var beginning = original.slice(0, prefixLen);
  var ending = original.slice(-tailLen);
  var result = beginning + ending + suffix;
  return { text: result, trimmed: original.length - prefixLen - tailLen, skipped: false };
}

/** Legacy diagnostic metadata parser; not the Mode 1 authorization decision. */
function spExtractAssessment(text) {
  var lines = text.split('\n');
  var clean = [];
  var valid = [];
  var bad = 0;
  var match;
  for (var i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    var line = lines[i];
    if (/^\s*\[SP_ASSESS\|/i.test(line)) {
      match = /^\s*\[SP_ASSESS\|([A-Z_]+)\|([A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*)\|([^\]|\r\n]{1,100})\]\s*$/i.exec(line);
      if (match) {
        var cls = match[1].toUpperCase();
        var category = match[2].toUpperCase();
        if (cls === 'NORMAL' || cls === 'NON_NORMAL' || cls === 'UNCERTAIN' || cls === 'NO_ACTION') {
          valid.push({ classification: cls, category: category, reason: match[3].trim() });
        } else { bad++; }
      } else { bad++; }
    } else {
      clean.push(line);
    }
  }
  var hadTag = valid.length + bad > 0;
  // Do not return empty Output text: AI Dungeon considers this a script failure.
  var rendered = hadTag ? clean.join('\n').replace(/\s+$/, '') : text;
  if (!rendered.trim()) rendered = text;
  return { text: rendered, valid: valid, malformed: bad, hadTag: hadTag };
}

// v0.2.6: FIRST variable-classification experiment after the fixed marker passed.
// No registry, power authorization, or narrative suppression in this build.
// v0.2.6: Brief effect-based semantic classifier; preserve context budget.
// A player-CAUSED effect can be extraordinary even if the player never names a power.
/** Mode 2 observation-only natural-language classifier prompt; no direct suppressor enforcement. */
function spClassPrompt(type, actionText) {
  // Actor-first: WHO gains/uses the capability, then WHAT that capability does.
  // A Story action can describe an NPC or environment; the writer is not
  // automatically the actor. This remains diagnostic-only.
  return [
    '[SURGE PROTECTOR CLASSIFIER / DIAGNOSTIC ONLY]',
    'Assess the latest PLAYER action for PLAYER-CHARACTER capability use or acquisition.',
    'Type: ' + String(type).toUpperCase(),
    'Player action: ' + JSON.stringify(spBrief(actionText, 430)),
    'DECISION ORDER (follow these in order):',
    '1. WHO? Identify the acting character. I/you or the established player-character name (e.g. Alex Mercer) means PLAYER. Named NPCs (e.g. Master Greldorn) and environment are not the player. A player-authored STORY about an NPC is still NPC-only.',
    '2. NO_ACTION if only an NPC or the environment acts, or the player merely discusses, asks about, or requests a power without personally invoking it. Say dialogue that actually casts a spell IS a player power use.',
    '3. NON_NORMAL if the player attempts, asserts success with, or claims to ACQUIRE an extraordinary ability. Include magic, teleportation, moving objects remotely by will, transformation, superhuman feats, psionics, and similar. An unnamed effect counts: causing a distant iron bar to float without touching it is NON_NORMAL. Story assertion that the protagonist discovers transformation is NON_NORMAL.',
    '4. NORMAL if the player performs an ordinary human action or physical attempt, even if it fails, such as straining with both hands to lift a giant boulder. Rope/pulley/tool use alone is not a personal power.',
    '5. UNCERTAIN only when the actor or intended effect cannot be determined.',
    'Begin output with EXACTLY ONE of: SPCLASS=NORMAL / SPCLASS=NON_NORMAL / SPCLASS=NO_ACTION / SPCLASS=UNCERTAIN',
    'Then continue natural narration. DIAGNOSTIC ONLY: do not block, weaken, grant or modify powers; do not explain the tag.',
    '[/SURGE PROTECTOR CLASSIFIER]'
  ].join('\n');
}

// A single-character typo can be recovered only if exactly one known label
// is one insertion, deletion, or substitution away. No general fuzzy guessing.
/** Check whether two short classification labels differ by a single edit. */
function spClassOneEditApart(actual, expected) {
  if (actual === expected || Math.abs(actual.length - expected.length) > 1) return false;
  var a = 0, b = 0, edits = 0;
  while (a < actual.length && b < expected.length) {
    if (actual[a] === expected[b]) { a++; b++; continue; }
    edits++;
    if (edits > 1) return false;
    if (actual.length > expected.length) a++;
    else if (actual.length < expected.length) b++;
    else { a++; b++; }
  }
  edits += (actual.length - a) + (expected.length - b);
  return edits === 1;
}

/** Recover unambiguous misspelled classifier labels; never turn missing into NORMAL. */
function spNormalizeClass(raw) {
  var label = String(raw || '').toUpperCase();
  var allowed = ['NORMAL', 'NON_NORMAL', 'NO_ACTION', 'UNCERTAIN'];
  if (allowed.indexOf(label) !== -1) return { status: 'VALID', classification: label };
  var candidates = allowed.filter(function (item) { return spClassOneEditApart(label, item); });
  return candidates.length === 1
    ? { status: 'RECOVERED', classification: candidates[0] }
    : { status: 'MALFORMED', classification: null };
}

// Output sanitation and assessment are separate responsibilities.
// A malformed SPCLASS token is ALWAYS removed from player-visible narration,
// even when its intended class is unknown. However it is never silently VALID.
/** Strip classifier tags from visible output and report classification/metadata status. */
function spExtractClass(text) {
  var source = typeof text === 'string' ? text : String(text);
  var tokenRe = /\bSPCLASS[ \t]*[:=][ \t]*([A-Z0-9_-]*)/gi;
  var tokens = [], match;
  while ((match = tokenRe.exec(source)) !== null) {
    tokens.push({ index: match.index, whole: match[0], rawLabel: match[1] });
  }
  if (tokens.length === 0) return {
    text: source, status: 'MISSING', classification: null, placement: 'NONE',
    validTags: 0, tokenCount: 0, hadMarker: false, recoveredFrom: null
  };

  // Strip ALL reserved metadata tokens, regardless of validity or placement.
  var cleaned = source.replace(tokenRe, '');
  if (/^\s*$/.test(source.slice(0, tokens[0].index))) cleaned = cleaned.replace(/^\s+/, '');
  if (!cleaned.trim()) cleaned = '...'; // Avoid an empty AI Dungeon output.

  var placement = /^\s*$/.test(source.slice(0, tokens[0].index)) ? 'PREFIX' : 'MISPLACED';
  var parsed = tokens.length === 1 ? spNormalizeClass(tokens[0].rawLabel) : null;
  var status = tokens.length > 1 ? 'DUPLICATE' : parsed.status;
  var classification = tokens.length === 1 ? parsed.classification : null;
  var recoveredFrom = status === 'RECOVERED' ? tokens[0].rawLabel : null;

  // A classification deeply embedded in narration cannot be trusted as
  // the response header, although the reserved marker is still sanitized.
  if (tokens.length === 1 && tokens[0].index > 240) {
    status = 'MALFORMED'; classification = null; recoveredFrom = null;
  }
  return {
    text: cleaned, status: status, classification: classification,
    placement: placement, validTags: tokens.filter(function (t) {
      return spNormalizeClass(t.rawLabel).status === 'VALID';
    }).length,
    tokenCount: tokens.length, hadMarker: true, recoveredFrom: recoveredFrom,
    rawLabel: tokens.length === 1 ? tokens[0].rawLabel : null
  };
}

// ---- MODE 1 REGISTRY: AUTHORITY LIVES IN STATE, NOT STORY TEXT ----
// Fresh installs start empty; existing registered powers migrate in place.
// No inventory tracking. Only personally held abilities belong here.
/** Ensure Mode 1 registry/settings exist; normalize legacy Telekinesis fixture without deleting powers. */
function spMode1Init(sp) {
  if (!sp.settings || typeof sp.settings !== 'object') sp.settings = {};
  if (sp.settings.gameMode !== 1 && sp.settings.gameMode !== 2) sp.settings.gameMode = 1;
  if (!sp.settings.fallback) sp.settings.fallback = sp.settings.gameMode === 1 ? 'DENY' : 'PERMIT'; // Mode 1 never waives triggers.
  // Earlier tag requests failed in five live attempts. The new outcome auditor
  // requires no model-generated metadata; keep tags ONLY as opt-in diagnostics.
  if (typeof sp.settings.semanticTagsEnabled !== 'boolean') sp.settings.semanticTagsEnabled = false;
  // A/B switch affects *only* whether examples are injected into Context.
  // Authorization, capability gates, registry, and read-only diagnostics are identical.
  // Default ON preserves existing adventure behavior; #POWER CACHE OFF disables hints.
  if (typeof sp.settings.precedentHintsEnabled !== 'boolean') sp.settings.precedentHintsEnabled = true;
  if (!sp.mode1 || typeof sp.mode1 !== 'object') {
    sp.mode1 = {
      registry: {},
      pendingInput: null,
      pendingGeneration: null,
      pendingCommand: null,
      lastActivation: null,
      inputStats: { none: 0, registered: 0, unknown: 0 },
      generations: 0
    };
    spLog('MODE1 INIT', 'Fresh registry is empty; mode=1. Register with #POWER ADD Telekinesis 4');
  }
  // Older/incomplete installs can have a saved registry without newer counters.
  // Repair bookkeeping ONLY; do not reset the actual player's registered powers.
  if (!sp.mode1.registry || typeof sp.mode1.registry !== 'object' || Array.isArray(sp.mode1.registry)) {
    sp.mode1.registry = {};
  }
  if (!sp.mode1.inputStats || typeof sp.mode1.inputStats !== 'object') {
    sp.mode1.inputStats = { none: 0, registered: 0, unknown: 0 };
  }
  ['none', 'registered', 'unknown'].forEach(function(key) {
    if (typeof sp.mode1.inputStats[key] !== 'number') sp.mode1.inputStats[key] = 0;
  });
  if (typeof sp.mode1.generations !== 'number') sp.mode1.generations = 0;
  // v0.7.1: optional diagnostic statistics; no changes to any registered power.
  if (!sp.mode1.semanticStats || typeof sp.mode1.semanticStats !== 'object') {
    sp.mode1.semanticStats = { valid:0, missing:0, malformed:0, duplicate:0, misplaced:0, skipped:0, conflict:0 };
  }
  ['valid','missing','malformed','duplicate','misplaced','skipped','conflict'].forEach(function(k) {
    if (typeof sp.mode1.semanticStats[k] !== 'number') sp.mode1.semanticStats[k] = 0;
  });
  // v0.8.1 bounded decision/observation memory. Only registry/JS rules grant powers.
  // A corrupt or missing map is repaired without touching the registered powers.
  if (!sp.mode1.precedents || typeof sp.mode1.precedents !== 'object' || Array.isArray(sp.mode1.precedents)) {
    sp.mode1.precedents = {};
  }
  // Never store or trust an indefinitely pending auditor decision on upgrade.
  // Output only audits the current generation's pendingGeneration snapshot.
  // Upgrade the legacy test power in-place when an earlier saved adventure
  // retains the original power definition. Never reset the player's registry.
  var testPower = sp.mode1.registry && sp.mode1.registry.tk;
  if (testPower && testPower.id === 'tk' && testPower.name === 'Telekinesis') {
    if (!testPower.kind) testPower.kind = 'TELEKINESIS';
    if (!testPower.catalogType) { testPower.catalogType = 'power'; testPower.catalogId = 'telekinesis'; testPower.catalogVersion = 'legacy-v0.5'; }
    if (typeof testPower.maxWeightLb !== 'number') testPower.maxWeightLb = 100;
    if (typeof testPower.maxRangeFt !== 'number') testPower.maxRangeFt = 50;
  }
  var refreshed=spRefreshCatalogDescriptions(sp.mode1.registry);
  if(refreshed)spLog('TIER DOCS','refreshed='+refreshed+' preservesCaps=true preservesCustom=true');
  return sp.mode1;
}

// ---- INPUT SYNTAX: SHORT #TRIGGER ACTIVATION ----
// Example player input: `#tk I lift the ball` or `#fireball:5 I cast at rank 5`.
// AI Dungeon may prefix Do text with `> ` or `> You `; preserve that wrapper.
// A deprecated [tk] form remains accepted for existing adventures, but all
// new docs and confirmation messages use #tk. A hashtag in the middle of a
// sentence is NOT an activation. Management commands are parsed FIRST in Input.
/**
 * Parse and optionally strip ONE leading ability invocation.
 * @param {string} input - Raw AI Dungeon action from the Input hook.
 * @param {Object} registry - Persistent state-owned powers, keyed by trigger.
 * @returns {{status:string, trigger:?string, power:?Object, text:string,
 *   stripped:boolean, requestedCastLevel:?number, castLevel:?number}}
 *   REGISTERED / UNKNOWN / NONE / LEVEL_DENIED are distinct and logged.
 *   Does not mutate the registry; never authorizes a power by itself.
 */
function spMode1ParseInput(input, registry) {
  var source = typeof input === 'string' ? input : String(input);
  var wrapper = '(\\s*(?:>\\s*(?:You\\s+)?)?)';
  var token = '(#([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,23})(?::([0-9]))?|\\[\\s*([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,23})(?::([0-9]))?\\s*\\])';
  var re = new RegExp('^' + wrapper + token + '(?=\\s|$)', 'i');
  var match = re.exec(source);
  if (!match) return { status: 'NONE', trigger: null, text: source, stripped: false };
  var id = (match[3] || match[5]).toLowerCase();
  var rawCastLevel = match[4] != null ? match[4] : match[6];
  var requestedCastLevel = rawCastLevel != null ? Number(rawCastLevel) : null;
  var power = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(registry, id) ? registry[id] : null;
  var levelDenied = !!power && requestedCastLevel != null &&
    (power.catalogType !== 'spell' || requestedCastLevel < power.baseLevel || requestedCastLevel > power.maxCastLevel);
  // Strip the trigger but retain AI Dungeon's optional Do wrapper.
  // The platform may BOTH prepend `> You ` AND turn `I lift` into
  // `you lift` before Input runs. Without this normalization it becomes
  // `> You you lift`, which is an actual defect seen in live testing.
  // Only remove a repeated subject when `> You` is already present;
  // actions lacking that wrapper keep their original grammatical subject.
  var remainder = source.slice(match[0].length).replace(/^\s+/, '');
  if (/^\s*>\s*you\s+$/i.test(match[1])) {
    remainder = remainder.replace(/^(?:you|i)\b\s*/i, '');
  }
  var stripped = match[1].replace(/\s+/g, ' ') + remainder;
  if (!match[1]) stripped = stripped.trim();
  if (!stripped.trim()) stripped = match[1] ? match[1].trimEnd() + ' attempt to use a power.' : 'I attempt to use a power.';
  return {
    status: levelDenied ? 'LEVEL_DENIED' : power ? 'REGISTERED' : 'UNKNOWN',
    trigger: id, power: power || null, requestedCastLevel: requestedCastLevel,
    castLevel: power && power.catalogType === 'spell'
      ? (requestedCastLevel == null ? power.baseLevel : requestedCastLevel) : null,
    text: stripped, stripped: true
  };
}

// ---- CONSERVATIVE MODE 1 AUTHORIZATION CHECKER (v0.4.0) ----
// Not an AI semantic interpreter. Only explicit mechanical violations are
// decisive. CANDIDATE means "no recognized hard violation", not ALLOW.
// No trigger never prohibits an ordinary physical/social action.
/** Detect a few explicit supernatural action patterns; result is advisory and conservative. */
function spMode1IntentCategory(raw) {
  var s = String(raw || '').replace(/\"[^\"\n]*\"/g,' ').toLowerCase();
  // Narrow active-verb patterns limit false positives from mentioning a word.
  if (/\b(?:read|scan|probe|enter|access|search|invade|reach into)\b[^.!?;]{0,100}\b(?:mind|minds|thoughts|memories|memory|consciousness)\b/.test(s) ||
      /\b(?:use|invoke|attempt|practice)\s+(?:my\s+)?telepath(?:y|ic\s+powers)\b/.test(s)) {
    return 'TELEPATHY';
  }
  if (/\b(?:i|you)\s+(?:(?:try|attempt|begin|prepare|want)\s+to\s+)?(?:teleport|translocate)\b/.test(s) ||
      /\b(?:use|cast|invoke)\s+(?:a\s+)?(?:teleportation|translocation|teleport\s+spell)\b/.test(s)) {
    return 'TELEPORTATION';
  }
  if (/\b(?:lift|levitate|float|raise|hoist|move)\s+(?:myself|yourself|me|my\s+body|your\s+body)\b/.test(s) ||
      /\b(?:i|you)\s+(?:(?:try|attempt|start|begin)\s+to\s+)?(?:fly|take flight)\b/.test(s)) {
    return 'SELF_FLIGHT';
  }
  if (/\b(?:i|you)\s+(?:transform|turn|change|morph)\s+(?:myself|yourself|into)\b/.test(s)) {
    return 'TRANSFORMATION';
  }
  if (/\b(?:i|you)\s+(?:summon|conjure|produce|shoot|create)\s+(?:a\s+)?(?:flames?|fireballs?)\b/.test(s)) {
    return 'ELEMENTAL_MAGIC';
  }
  if (/\b(?:i|you)\s+(?:heal|resurrect|raise the dead)\b/.test(s)) {
    return 'HEALING_MAGIC';
  }
  if (/\b(?:twist|crush|stop|tear|rupture|squeeze|pull)\b[^.!?;]{0,40}\b(?:heart|brain|artery|arteries|lungs?)\b/.test(s)) {
    return 'INTERNAL_TISSUE';
  }
  return null;
}

/** Extract explicit attempted weight and return violation only if greater than registered cap. */
function spMode1WeightOverLimit(raw, maxLb) {
  var s = String(raw || '').toLowerCase();
  var re = /\b(\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:-\s*|\s+)?(pounds?|lbs?|kilograms?|kgs?|tons?|tonnes?)\b/g;
  var m;
  while ((m = re.exec(s)) !== null) {
    // Require a lifting/manipulation verb in the same clause before the weight.
    // A historical or quoted number elsewhere is not sufficient evidence.
    var before = s.slice(Math.max(0, m.index - 140), m.index);
    if (!/\b(?:lift|levitate|raise|move|push|pull|throw|carry|shift|hoist|suspend|hurl|lifting|raising|moving)\b[^.!?;]{0,140}$/.test(before)) continue;
    var amount = Number(m[1].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (!isFinite(amount)) continue;
    var unit = m[2];
    var lb = /^(?:kilograms?|kgs?)$/.test(unit) ? amount * 2.20462262185
      : /^tonnes?$/.test(unit) ? amount * (unit === 'tonne' || unit === 'tonnes' ? 2204.62262185 : 2000)
      : amount;
    if (lb > maxLb + 0.000001) return { actual: Math.round(lb * 100) / 100, cap: maxLb, unit: 'lb' };
  }
  return null;
}

/** Extract explicit attempted range and compare against registered hard range ceiling. */
function spMode1RangeOverLimit(raw, maxFt) {
  var s = String(raw || '').toLowerCase();
  var re = /\b(?:at\s+(?:a\s+)?distance\s+of\s+|from\s+)?(\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(feet|foot|ft|meters?|metres?|m)\s+away\b|\b(?:at\s+(?:a\s+)?distance\s+of|from)\s+(\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(feet|foot|ft|meters?|metres?|m)\b/g;
  var m;
  while ((m = re.exec(s)) !== null) {
    var amount = Number((m[1] || m[3]).replace(/,/g, ''));
    var unit = m[2] || m[4];
    if (!isFinite(amount)) continue;
    var feet = /^(?:meters?|metres?|m)$/.test(unit) ? amount * 3.280839895 : amount;
    if (feet > maxFt + 0.000001) return { actual: Math.round(feet * 100) / 100, cap: maxFt, unit: 'ft' };
  }
  return null;
}

/** Compare an explicit action duration against an authorized sustained duration. */
function spMode1DurationOverLimit(raw, maxSeconds) {
  var s=String(raw || '').toLowerCase();
  // Only sustained acts with an explicit requested duration: no standalone
  // time references in narration or quoted examples.
  var re=/\b(?:hold|maintain|sustain|keep|remain|stay|last|continue|hover|burn)\b[^.!?;]{0,85}?\bfor\s+(\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(seconds?|secs?|minutes?|mins?|hours?|hrs?)\b/g;
  var m;
  while((m=re.exec(s)) !== null) {
    var value=Number(m[1].replace(/,/g,''));
    if (/^(?:minutes?|mins?)$/.test(m[2])) value *= 60;
    if (/^(?:hours?|hrs?)$/.test(m[2])) value *= 3600;
    if (value > maxSeconds + .000001) return {actual:value,cap:maxSeconds,unit:'sec'};
  }
  return null;
}

/** Translate activation status and power intent into an AUTH result (NO_TRIGGER, BLOCK, REVIEW, CANDIDATE). */
function spMode1Authorize(actionType, actionText, decision, semantic) {
  var status = decision && decision.status || 'NONE';
  if (status === 'NONE') return { result: 'NO_TRIGGER', reason: 'NO_ACTIVATION' };
  if (status === 'UNKNOWN') return { result: 'UNKNOWN_TRIGGER', reason: 'UNREGISTERED' };
  if (status === 'LEVEL_DENIED') return { result: 'BLOCK_LIMIT', reason: 'CAST_LEVEL', observed: decision.requestedCastLevel, cap: decision.power.maxCastLevel, unit: 'rank' };
  if (status !== 'REGISTERED' || !decision.power) return { result: 'REVIEW', reason: 'INVALID_ACTIVATION' };
  return spAuthorizePowerIntent(decision.power, actionType, actionText, semantic);
}

// Generic ability authorizer: deterministic when explicit and known, semantic REVIEW otherwise.
// CANDIDATE is not proof of success. Never promote an unknown effect to a new power.
/** Inspect recognized category/quantity while leaving unfamiliar intent for semantic review. */
function spAuthorizePowerIntent(power, actionType, actionText, semantic) {
  if (actionType === 'story') return { result: 'REVIEW', reason: 'STORY_ACTOR_AMBIGUOUS' };
  if (!power) return { result: 'REVIEW', reason: 'MISSING_POWER' };
  var s = String(actionText || '').toLowerCase();
  var cat = spMode1IntentCategory(actionText);
  var compatible = {
    TELEKINESIS: ['TELEKINESIS'], TELEPATHY: ['TELEPATHY'], PYROKINESIS: ['ELEMENTAL_MAGIC'],
    HEALING: ['HEALING_MAGIC'], TELEPORTATION: ['TELEPORTATION'],
    FIREBALL: ['ELEMENTAL_MAGIC'], MAGE_HAND: ['TELEKINESIS'], CURE_WOUNDS: ['HEALING_MAGIC'], MISTY_STEP: ['TELEPORTATION']
  };
  if (cat && power.kind !== 'CUSTOM' && (compatible[power.kind] || []).indexOf(cat) === -1) {
    return { result: 'BLOCK_CATEGORY', reason: cat };
  }
  var mass = power.maxWeightLb;
  if (typeof mass === 'number') {
    var weight = spMode1WeightOverLimit(actionText, mass);
    if (weight) return { result: 'BLOCK_LIMIT', reason: 'WEIGHT', observed: weight.actual, cap: weight.cap, unit: weight.unit };
  }
  if (typeof power.maxRangeFt === 'number') {
    var range = spMode1RangeOverLimit(actionText, power.maxRangeFt);
    if (range) return { result: 'BLOCK_LIMIT', reason: 'RANGE', observed: range.actual, cap: range.cap, unit: range.unit };
  }
  if (power.caps && typeof power.caps.durationSeconds === 'number') {
    var duration = spMode1DurationOverLimit(actionText, power.caps.durationSeconds);
    if (duration) return { result: 'BLOCK_LIMIT', reason: 'DURATION', observed: duration.actual, cap: duration.cap, unit: duration.unit };
  }
  if (power.caps && typeof power.caps.maxTargets === 'number') {
    var targets = spMode1TargetsOverLimit(actionText, power.caps.maxTargets);
    if (targets) return { result: 'BLOCK_LIMIT', reason: 'TARGETS', observed: targets.actual, cap: targets.cap, unit: targets.unit };
  }
  // Category and numeric checks above take priority; semantic JS then vetoes only
  // high-confidence contradictions, not a creative but uncertain ability use.
  var sem=semantic || spSemanticPrecheck(power,actionType,actionText);
  if (sem.result === 'MISMATCH') return {result:sem.blockType,reason:sem.reason};
  if (sem.reason === 'UNCLASSIFIED_EFFECT') return {result:'REVIEW',reason:'UNCLASSIFIED_EFFECT'};
  if (sem.result === 'MATCH' && power.kind === 'CUSTOM') {
    return {result:'CANDIDATE',reason:'POSSIBLE_CUSTOM_SEMANTIC'};
  }
  // Explicitly known profile semantics. Other powers get conservative context-based review,
  // not an invented deterministic ALLOW decision.
  var matchers = {
    TELEKINESIS: /\b(?:lift|lifting|levitate|levitating|raise|raising|move|moving|push|pull|throw|carrying|carry|shift|hoist|suspend|float|hurl)\b/,
    TELEPATHY: /\b(?:mind|thoughts|telepath|memory|memories|consciousness)\b/,
    PYROKINESIS: /\b(?:fire|flame|ignite|heat|burn|pyrokinesis)\b/,
    HEALING: /\b(?:heal|wound|injury|mend|regenerat)\b/,
    TELEPORTATION: /\b(?:teleport|blink|translocat)\b/,
    FIREBALL: /\b(?:fireball|sphere of fire|explosion of flame|explode)\b/,
    MAGE_HAND: /\b(?:hand|manipulate|move|lift|open|pour|stow|retrieve)\b/,
    CURE_WOUNDS: /\b(?:heal|cure|wound|injur|restore)\b/,
    MISTY_STEP: /\b(?:teleport|misty|blink|translocate)\b/
  };
  var matcher = matchers[power.kind];
  if (matcher && matcher.test(s)) return { result: 'CANDIDATE', reason: 'POSSIBLE_' + power.kind };
  return { result: 'REVIEW', reason: 'SEMANTIC_INTENT_REVIEW' };
}

/** Enforce an explicitly registered simultaneous-target cap ONLY for a clear
 * player request involving a stated numeric count. Non-numeric scope such as
 * "nearby" or "weak" never becomes an invented number. */
function spMode1TargetsOverLimit(raw,maxTargets) {
  var text=String(raw||'').replace(/"[^"\n]*"/g,' ').toLowerCase();
  var clauses=text.split(/[.!?;]+/);
  var re=/\b(?:contact|message|reach|connect(?:\s+with)?|communicate(?:\s+with)?|address|read|affect|influence|control|broadcast(?:\s+to)?|heal|summon)\b[^.!?;]{0,65}?\b(\d[\d,]*)\s+(?:minds?|people|persons?|targets?|individuals?)\b/g;
  for(var i=0;i<clauses.length;i++) {
    if (/\b(?:ask|discuss|wonder|question|imagine|speculate|hypothesize)\b/.test(clauses[i]))continue;
    re.lastIndex=0;
    var match;
    while((match=re.exec(clauses[i]))!==null) {
      var prefix=clauses[i].slice(Math.max(0,match.index-18),match.index);
      if (/\b(?:not|never|don't|do not|won't|would not|could not)\s*$/.test(prefix))continue;
      var count=Number(match[1].replace(/,/g,''));
      if(Number.isFinite(count)&&count>maxTargets)return {actual:count,cap:maxTargets,unit:' targets'};
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/** Generic, ADVISORY scope pressure, grounded only in what the player actually
 * wrote for this power. No per-power phrase lists, arbitrary numeric caps,
 * automatic damage/costs, or invented restrictions. A very large attempted
 * scale against an explicitly weak/nearby/single-target description merits
 * heightened narrative resistance rather than blanket blocking creative uses.
 */
function spNarrativeScopePressure(power,raw) {
  if(!power)return null;
  var scope=(String(power.name||'')+' '+String(power.description||'')).toLowerCase();
  var anchors=[];
  if (/\b(?:weak|feeble|faint|minor|rudimentary|untrained|novice|limited|small.scale)\b/.test(scope))anchors.push('WEAK_OR_LIMITED');
  if (/\b(?:nearby|close.range|short.range|within (?:sight|earshot)|local (?:area|targets?|minds?))\b/.test(scope))anchors.push('NEARBY_ONLY');
  if (/\b(?:one|a single|only one)\s+(?:person|mind|individual|target)\b/.test(scope))anchors.push('INDIVIDUAL_SCOPE');
  if (!anchors.length)return null;
  var clauses=spEffectClauses(raw);
  for (var i=0;i<clauses.length;i++) {
    var text=clauses[i].toLowerCase();
    // Discussion, hypothetical propositions and negation are not power uses.
    if (/\b(?:ask|discuss|wonder|question|imagine|speculate|hypothesize|mention|say|tell)\b/.test(text) ||
      /\b(?:i|you)\s+(?:do not|don't|never|will not|won't|would not|wouldn't)\s+/.test(text))continue;
    if(!/\b(?:i|you)\s+/.test(text))continue;
    if(!/\b(?:contact|reach|send|broadcast|connect|speak|communicate|read|control|affect|influence|summon|ignite|burn|engulf|heat|lift|push|pull|teleport|extend|project|cast|use)\b/.test(text))continue;
    var global=/\b(?:across|throughout|around|over|to|engulf)\s+(?:(?:the|an|a|entire|whole|all of the)\s+){0,3}(?:planet|world|continent|globe|country|nation)\b|\b(?:planet.wide|worldwide|global|(?:the )?(?:entire|whole) (?:planet|world|continent|population))\b/.test(text);
    var match=/\b(\d[\d,]*)\s+(?:minds?|people|persons?|targets?|individuals?)\b/.exec(text);
    var many=match&&Number(match[1].replace(/,/g,''))>=100 ||
      /\b(?:thousands?|millions?|hundreds?)\s+of\s+(?:minds?|people|persons?|targets?|individuals?)\b/.test(text);
    if (!global&&!many)continue;
    return {severity:'EXTREME',anchors:anchors,scale:global?'PLANETARY_OR_REGIONAL':'MASS_TARGETS',
      evidence:spBrief(text,110)};
  }
  return null;
}

// ---- v0.7.1 SEMANTIC PRECHECK + STORYTELLER ASSESSMENT ----
// Two intentionally distinct mechanisms:
//   (1) Pre-narration, conservative JS can veto an EXPLICIT incompatible category
//       or a directly contradicted stated condition. Never guess broad semantics.
//   (2) The AI writes diagnostic [SP_SEM|...] metadata WITH its narration.
//       This can measure reliability but CANNOT authorize or retroactively veto it.
// Result MATCH indicates a plausible requested effect, NOT story-world success.
/** Infer only explicit attempted extraordinary categories, never from casual nouns or negations. */
function spSemanticRequestedCategories(raw) {
  var s=String(raw || '').toLowerCase();
  var categories=[];
  function add(category,re) {
    var m;
    while((m=re.exec(s))!==null) {
      // 'I do not become invisible' is not a requested invisible result.
      var prev=s.slice(Math.max(0,m.index-17),m.index);
      if (/\b(?:not|never|without|don't|do not|cannot|can't|won't)\s*$/.test(prev) ||
        /\b(?:say|mention|quote|write|read|utter|spell)\s+(?:the\s+(?:word|phrase)\s+)?['\"]?\s*$/.test(prev)) continue;
      categories.push(category); break;
    }
  }
  add('INVISIBILITY', /\b(?:become|turn|go|remain|stay|make\s+(?:myself|yourself|my\s+(?:body|form|presence)|your\s+(?:body|form|presence))|render\s+(?:myself|yourself|my\s+(?:body|form)|your\s+(?:body|form)))\s+(?:(?:completely|totally|fully|utterly|entirely)\s+)?invisible\b|\b(?:cast|use|activate|gain)\s+invisibility\b|\bvanish\s+from\s+(?:sight|view)\b/g);
  add('TELEPORTATION', /\b(?:teleport|translocate|blink\s+(?:to|into|from))\b/g);
  add('PHASING', /\b(?:phase|walk|pass)\s+through\s+(?:a\s+)?(?:solid\s+)?(?:wall|stone|matter)\b/g);
  add('TELEPATHY', /\b(?:read|scan|probe|enter|reach\s+into|overhear)\s+(?:(?:someone's|a person's|the|his|her|their)\s+)?(?:mind|thoughts|memories)\b/g);
  add('TRANSFORMATION', /\b(?:i|you)\s+(?:turn|transform|morph)\s+(?:myself|yourself|[a-z]+)\s+into\b/g);
  add('HEALING_MAGIC', /\b(?:magically\s+heal|heal\s+(?:my|his|her|their|the)\s+(?:wounds?|injuries)|cast\s+(?:healing|cure\s+wounds))\b/g);
  add('ELEMENTAL_MAGIC', /\b(?:ignite|conjure|summon|create|shoot|project)\b[^.!?;,]{0,45}\b(?:flames?|fireballs?|fire)\b/g);
  return categories.filter(function(c,i,a){return a.indexOf(c)===i;});
}
/** Extract clauses only to detect *additional supernatural requests*, not to understand
 * ordinary language. Dialogue/quoted examples and NPC-led clauses are excluded;
 * the AI still interprets anything we cannot confidently classify.
 */
function spEffectClauses(raw) {
  var text=String(raw||'').replace(/"[^"\n]*"/g,' ').replace(/'[^'\n]+'/g,' ');
  text=text.replace(/\b(?:upon arrival|after arriving|afterward|afterwards)\s*,?/gi,' | ');
  text=text.replace(/\band\s+then\s+/gi,' | ');
  text=text.replace(/\b(?:and\s+then|and\s+also|and|then)\s+(?=(?:i|you)\b|(?:cast|conjure|invoke|perform|summon|cloak|shroud|enchant|alter|make)\b)/gi,' | ');
  return text.split(/[.!?;|]+|,\s+(?=(?:and\s+)?(?:i|you)\s|(?:and\s+)?(?:cast|conjure|perform)\b)/i)
    .map(function(x){return x.trim();}).filter(Boolean);
}
/** Conservative marker that a separate clause seeks an unrecognized magical
 * RESULT (not just mentions magic, poses a question, or describes an NPC).
 * This is a prompt for AI semantic review, NEVER grounds a mechanical veto.
 */
function spUnresolvedMagicClause(text) {
  var s=String(text||'').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (/\b(?:do not|don't|never|without|avoid|refuse to)\s+(?:cast|invoke|perform|conjure|summon|use)\b/.test(s))return false;
  if (/\b(?:ask|tell|question|wonder|discuss|talk|say|mention|describe|quote|read about)\b/.test(s) &&
      !/\b(?:cast|invoke|perform|conjure|summon)\b/.test(s))return false;
  if (/^(?:(?:while|as)\s+)?(?:greldorn|he|she|they|the\s+\w+|an?\s+\w+)\s+/.test(s))return false;
  // Avoid reviewing harmless motion such as waving or walking.
  return /\b(?:cast|invoke|perform|conjure|summon)\s+(?:(?:a|an|the|my|your)\s+)?(?:(?:strange|unknown|mysterious|magical|arcane|other|new)\s+){0,3}(?:spell|enchantment|ritual|incantation|magic|power|hex|charm|trick)\b/.test(s) ||
    /(?:\b(?:i|you)\s+(?:also\s+)?|^)(?:cloak|shroud|enchant|alter|rewrite)\s+(?:myself|yourself|my\s+(?:body|form|presence)|your\s+(?:body|form|presence)|everyone|the\s+\w+)\b/.test(s) ||
    /\b(?:i|you)\s+(?:also\s+)?(?:perform|create|attempt)\s+(?:an?\s+)?(?:other|additional|second|unfamiliar|strange)\s+(?:supernatural|magical|arcane)\s+(?:effect|feat|action)\b/.test(s) ||
    /\b(?:i|you)\s+(?:also\s+)?(?:make|cause|force)\s+(?:everyone|anyone|others?|people|the\s+crowd)\b[^.!?;]{0,65}\b(?:see|perceive|notice|sight|eyes|ignore|recognize)\b/.test(s);
}
/** The exception-driven 'yin-yang' intake: preserve independently recognized
 * effects, and report unclassified EXTRA effects instead of stamping the entire
 * action eligible. No scores: recognized / unclassified / authoritative veto.
 */
function spEffectLedger(power,actionType,raw) {
  var granted=spSemanticGrantedCategories(power), clauses=spEffectClauses(raw);
  var effects=[],unresolved=[];
  if(actionType!=='do'&&actionType!=='say')return {coverage:'UNKNOWN',effects:[],unresolved:[],granted:granted};
  clauses.forEach(function(clause){
    // The requested PC action can contain an NPC's actions; never attribute
    // those as PLAYER power requests simply because the noun matches.
    var subject=clause.toLowerCase();
    if(/^(?:master\s+)?greldorn\s+(?:tells?|asks?|orders?|commands?|suggests?|warns?)\b/.test(subject))return;
    var npc= /\b(?:while|as)\s+(?:master\s+)?greldorn\b|\b(?:greldorn|he|she|they)\s+(?:makes?|becomes?|casts?|turns?)\b/.test(subject);
    if(npc && !/\b(?:i|you)\s+(?:also\s+)?(?:teleport|become|make|render|cast)\b/.test(subject))return;
    var cats=spSemanticRequestedCategories(clause);
    cats.forEach(function(category){
      if(!effects.some(function(e){return e.category===category;}))
        effects.push({category:category,verdict:granted.indexOf(category)>=0?'ELIGIBLE':granted.length?'DENY':'UNCERTAIN',evidence:spBrief(clause,90)});
    });
    if(!cats.length && spUnresolvedMagicClause(clause))unresolved.push(spBrief(clause,110));
  });
  return {coverage:unresolved.length?'PARTIAL':effects.length?'COMPLETE':'UNKNOWN',
    effects:effects,unresolved:unresolved.slice(0,3),granted:granted};
}

/** Infer category grants from a registered power's definition; unknown is NOT a denial. */
function spSemanticGrantedCategories(power) {
  var grants=[];
  var known={TELEKINESIS:['TELEKINESIS'], MAGE_HAND:['TELEKINESIS'],
    TELEPATHY:['TELEPATHY'], PYROKINESIS:['ELEMENTAL_MAGIC'], FIREBALL:['ELEMENTAL_MAGIC'],
    TELEPORTATION:['TELEPORTATION'], MISTY_STEP:['TELEPORTATION'],
    HEALING:['HEALING_MAGIC'], CURE_WOUNDS:['HEALING_MAGIC']};
  if (power && known[power.kind]) grants=known[power.kind].slice();
  var d=String(power && power.description || '').toLowerCase();
  var checks={
    TELEPORTATION:/\b(?:teleport|translocat|blink\s+(?:to|into|from))\b/,
    INVISIBILITY:/\b(?:invisible|invisibility|become unseen)\b/,
    PHASING:/\b(?:phas(?:e|ing)|pass through solid)\b/,
    TELEPATHY:/\b(?:telepath|read minds?|read thoughts?)\b/,
    HEALING_MAGIC:/\b(?:heal|healing|cure wounds|regenerat)\b/,
    ELEMENTAL_MAGIC:/\b(?:fire|flame|pyrokinesis|ignite)\b/
  };
  Object.keys(checks).forEach(function(k) {
    var m=checks[k].exec(d);
    if(!m || grants.indexOf(k)>=0) return;
    // Do not turn a PROHIBITION in a prose definition into a new power.
    // E.g. 'Teleport ...; cannot become invisible' grants teleportation ONLY.
    var clauseStart=Math.max(d.lastIndexOf(';',m.index),d.lastIndexOf('.',m.index),d.lastIndexOf('!',m.index));
    var prev=d.slice(clauseStart+1,m.index);
    if (/\b(?:cannot|can't|does not|doesn't|do not|don't|no|never|without|forbidden|prohibited|exclude[ds]?)\b[^.;!?]*$/.test(prev)) return;
    grants.push(k);
  });
  // A description with NO recognized grants must be reviewed, not falsely vetoed.
  return grants;
}
/** Require visibility only when the registered description explicitly requires a visible destination. */
function spSemanticVisibilityViolation(power,raw) {
  var d=String(power && power.description || '').toLowerCase();
  var s=String(raw || '').toLowerCase();
  if (!/(?:visible\s+(?:destination\s+)?(?:shadows?|locations?|points?|targets?)|line\s+of\s+sight)/.test(d)) return false;
  return /\b(?:cannot|can't|can not|couldn't|do not|don't|unable to)\s+see\s+(?:(?:the|my|that|a)\s+)?(?:destination|target|arrival|far|other)\s+(?:shadow|location|point|place)\b/.test(s) ||
    /\b(?:destination|target|arrival|other)\s+(?:shadow|location|point|place)\s+(?:is\s+)?(?:not\s+visible|unseen|out\s+of\s+sight)\b/.test(s) ||
    /\b(?:an?\s+)?(?:unseen|invisible|not\s+visible)\s+(?:destination\s+)?shadow\b/.test(s) ||
    /\b(?:shadow|destination|location|point)\s+that\s+(?:i|you)\s+(?:cannot|can't|do not|don't|couldn't)\s+see\b/.test(s);
}
/** Fast, conservative deterministic semantic hints. Never interprets a novel power by guessing. */
function spSemanticPrecheck(power, actionType, actionText, ledger) {
  if (!power || actionType !== 'do' && actionType !== 'say') return {result:'SKIP',reason:'NOT_ELIGIBLE'};
  var evidence=ledger||spEffectLedger(power,actionType,actionText);
  var requested=evidence.effects.map(function(e){return e.category;});
  var granted=evidence.granted;
  if (granted.length) {
    for(var i=0;i<requested.length;i++) if(granted.indexOf(requested[i])<0) {
      return {result:'MISMATCH',reason:requested[i],blockType:'BLOCK_CATEGORY',requested:requested,granted:granted};
    }
  }
  if (spSemanticVisibilityViolation(power,actionText)) {
    return {result:'MISMATCH',reason:'VISIBLE_DESTINATION',blockType:'BLOCK_CONDITION',requested:requested,granted:granted};
  }
  // A description requiring a visible destination doesn't establish that
  // condition merely because the player says 'I teleport'. Do not veto it,
  // but do not claim an established MATCH either until visibility is stated.
  var d=String(power.description || '').toLowerCase();
  if (requested.indexOf('TELEPORTATION')!==-1 &&
      /\bvisible\s+(?:destination\s+)?(?:shadows?|locations?|points?|targets?)\b|line\s+of\s+sight/.test(d) &&
      !/\b(?:visible|in sight|line of sight|I can see|you can see)\b/.test(String(actionText || '').toLowerCase())) {
    return {result:'UNCERTAIN',reason:'VISIBILITY_NOT_ESTABLISHED',requested:requested,granted:granted};
  }
  if (evidence.unresolved.length) return {result:'UNCERTAIN',reason:'UNCLASSIFIED_EFFECT',requested:requested,granted:granted};
  if (requested.length && requested.every(function(c){return granted.indexOf(c)!==-1;})) {
    // A positive MATCH is only a plausibility signal; requirements and circumstances
    // beyond explicitly checked facts remain the storyteller's responsibility.
    return {result:'MATCH',reason:'RECOGNIZED_IN_SCOPE_INTENT',requested:requested,granted:granted};
  }
  return {result:'UNCERTAIN',reason:'SEMANTIC_INTENT_REVIEW',requested:requested,granted:granted};
}
/**
 * Ask for ONE semantic observation at the FIRST line of generated output.
 * Rationale: v0.7.0 live output omitted the final-line tag on 4/4 tests;
 * the older (Mode 2) first-line classifier was more reliable in practice.
 * This request remains DIAGNOSTIC. The pre-generation JS verdict wins.
 */
function spSemanticPrompt() {
  return [
    '[SURGE PROTECTOR SEMANTIC ASSESSMENT / INTERNAL]',
    'Privately compare THIS activated PLAYER power attempt to ACTIVE_POWER SCOPE, HARD_LIMITS, EXCLUSIONS and prerequisites.',
    'MATCH = requested supernatural effects fit the power and explicit conditions; it does NOT promise success.',
    'MISMATCH = any requested supernatural effect or explicitly violated prerequisite is outside the power.',
    'UNCERTAIN = cannot tell confidently; do not infer a new power.',
    'IMPORTANT: Your FIRST line of generated output MUST be EXACTLY ONE metadata tag:',
    '[SP_SEM|MATCH|brief reason] or [SP_SEM|MISMATCH|brief reason] or [SP_SEM|UNCERTAIN|brief reason]',
    'Choose ONE classification; plain reason max 100 characters, without brackets or pipes.',
    'Story begins on line 2. Write natural narration normally; Output removes the first line.',
    'Metadata never overrides AUTH_RESULT, SUPPRESSION or a hard JS BLOCK. Narration must obey those first.',
    'No markdown, code fences, prefacing, additional tags or references to this mechanism in the story.',
    '[/SURGE PROTECTOR SEMANTIC ASSESSMENT]',
    'REMINDER: FIRST line = [SP_SEM|CLASS|brief reason] with CLASS MATCH, MISMATCH or UNCERTAIN; story begins on line 2.'
  ].join('\n');
}
/**
 * Strip isolated model metadata without removing in-world quoted mentions.
 * Tolerate a tag as FIRST line (new contract) or LAST line (legacy), plus
 * occasional single-line markdown fencing. Duplicate or malformed results
 * remain untrusted; this function NEVER makes an authorization decision.
 */
function spExtractSemantic(text) {
  var raw=String(text || '');
  var lines=raw.split('\n'), keep=[], tags=[], malformed=0, indices=[];
  var tagPattern=/^\s*\[SP_SEM\|(MATCH|MISMATCH|UNCERTAIN)\|([^\[\]|\r\n]{1,100})\]\s*$/i;
  var standalonePrefix=/^\s*\[SP_SEM\|/i;
  // Identify marker-only fences: do not remove fences that surround story/code.
  var removableFence={};
  for (var n=0;n<lines.length;n++) {
    if (standalonePrefix.test(lines[n])) {
      if (n>0 && /^\s*```(?:text)?\s*$/i.test(lines[n-1]) && n+1<lines.length && /^\s*```\s*$/.test(lines[n+1])) {
        removableFence[n-1]=true;removableFence[n+1]=true;
      }
    }
  }
  for(var i=0;i<lines.length;i++) {
    if(removableFence[i]) continue;
    var line=lines[i];
    if (standalonePrefix.test(line)) {
      indices.push(i);
      var match=tagPattern.exec(line);
      if(match && match[2].trim()) tags.push({classification:match[1].toUpperCase(),reason:match[2].trim()});
      else malformed++;
    } else keep.push(line);
  }
  var count=tags.length+malformed;
  var status=count===0?'MISSING':count>1?'DUPLICATE':malformed?'MALFORMED':'VALID';
  var narration=count?keep.join('\n').replace(/^\s*\n+/, '').replace(/\s+$/,''):raw;
  if (!narration.trim()) narration='The moment remains unresolved.';
  var classification=status==='VALID'?tags[0].classification:null;
  // New first-line tag and older last-line tag both meet placement expectations.
  // Interior tags are stripped, but their placement is counted as suspect.
  var first=indices.length===1 && lines.slice(0,indices[0]).every(function(l){return !l.trim() || /^\s*```(?:text)?\s*$/i.test(l);});
  var last=indices.length===1 && lines.slice(indices[0]+1).every(function(l){return !l.trim() || /^\s*```\s*$/.test(l);});
  var misplaced=count===1 && !first && !last;
  return {text:narration,status:status,classification:classification,
    reason:classification?tags[0].reason:null,rawTags:count,misplaced:misplaced,
    markerSeen:/\[SP_SEM\|/i.test(raw)};
}

// ---- MODE 1 ASYMMETRIC DUAL AUDIT AND AUTHORIZATION PRECEDENTS (v0.8.1) ----
// Three distinct kinds of evidence: (1) REGISTRY is authoritative,
// (2) this turn's JS authorization is the expected outcome, and
// (3) storyteller output is OBSERVED, never permission to acquire powers.
// Store only bounded, per-power, definition-matched cases in persistent state.
// Full narration and untrusted text are NEVER replayed into storyteller context.

/** Fingerprint the parts of a registered definition that affect permission.
 * Hash is for cache invalidation, NOT security or authorization.
 */
function spPowerSignature(power) {
  if (!power) return '';
  var snap=JSON.stringify([
    power.id,power.kind,power.catalogType,power.level,power.baseLevel,power.maxCastLevel,
    power.description,power.exclusions,power.mechanics,power.maxRangeFt,power.maxWeightLb,
    power.caps,power.limits
  ]);
  var n=2166136261;
  for(var i=0;i<snap.length;i++) {n^=snap.charCodeAt(i);n=Math.imul(n,16777619);}
  return (n>>>0).toString(16);
}

/** Map mechanical authorization to three trust states. ELIGIBLE is only an
 * in-scope candidate, NOT a guarantee of in-fiction success. The storyteller
 * may narrate a failure for ordinary dramatic/circumstantial reasons.
 * All hard vetoes remain authoritative even if the AI narrates otherwise.
 */
function spJsAuthorizationVerdict(authorization) {
  var r=authorization&&authorization.result;
  if(r==='BLOCK_CATEGORY'||r==='BLOCK_CONDITION'||r==='BLOCK_LIMIT'||r==='UNKNOWN_TRIGGER')return 'DENY';
  if(r==='CANDIDATE')return 'ELIGIBLE';
  if(r==='NO_TRIGGER')return 'NO_POWER';
  return 'UNCERTAIN';
}

/** Save what JS conclusively decided BEFORE the narrator writes a word.
 * This is an AUTHORIZATION precedent, not a claim that an effect happened.
 * Unknown narrated outcomes do not invalidate a known mechanical denial.
 * Retries replace cases for the same action slot; definition changes retire
 * old cases. An uncertain verdict is retained diagnostically, never prompted.
 */
function spRecordAuthorizationPrecedent(mode1,pending) {
  if(!mode1||!pending||pending.status!=='REGISTERED'||!pending.powerSignature||!pending.trigger)return null;
  if(!mode1.precedents||typeof mode1.precedents!=='object')mode1.precedents={};
  var id=pending.trigger, arr=Array.isArray(mode1.precedents[id])?mode1.precedents[id]:[];
  var verdict=pending.jsVerdict||spJsAuthorizationVerdict(pending.authorization);
  var ledger=pending.effectLedger||{coverage:'UNKNOWN',effects:[],unresolved:[]};
  var explicitDenied=ledger.effects.filter(function(e){return e.verdict==='DENY';}).map(function(e){return e.category;});
  var explicitEligible=ledger.effects.filter(function(e){return e.verdict==='ELIGIBLE';}).map(function(e){return e.category;});
  var hard=pending.authorization&&/^BLOCK_/.test(pending.authorization.result);
  // A numeric or condition veto is about its own specific rule, NOT proof the
  // ability category is globally denied. Record the reason as well as scope.
  var deniedCats=hard&&pending.authorization.result!=='BLOCK_CATEGORY'?
    (pending.requestedCategories||[]).slice():explicitDenied;
  // A verdict alone does not prove that JS understood the requested effect.
  // In particular, Pyrokinesis can be a valid CANDIDATE while this ledger sees
  // no category at all; retain that turn for diagnostics, NOT as a reusable grant.
  // Mechanical limits remain binding regardless of whether we cache an example.
  var scoped=ledger.coverage==='COMPLETE' && ledger.unresolved.length===0;
  var verified=scoped && (
    (verdict==='ELIGIBLE' && explicitEligible.length>0 && explicitDenied.length===0) ||
    (verdict==='DENY' && hard && deniedCats.length>0)
  );
  var r={generation:pending.generation,slot:pending.actionSlot||null,
    signature:pending.powerSignature,categories:(pending.requestedCategories||[]).slice(0,4),
    eligibleCategories:explicitEligible,deniedCategories:deniedCats,
    coverage:ledger.coverage,unresolvedCount:ledger.unresolved.length,
    gatePolicy:pending.suppression&&pending.suppression.result==='GATE'?'WITHHOLD_UNCLASSIFIED':null,
    authorization:pending.authorization?pending.authorization.result:'REVIEW',
    authorizationVerdict:verdict,verifiedAuthorization:verified,
    source:'JS_EFFECT_LEDGER_V2',reason:pending.authorization?pending.authorization.reason:'UNKNOWN',
    suppression:pending.suppression?pending.suppression.result:'REVIEW',
    outcome:'UNKNOWN',execution:'UNKNOWN',confidence:'LOW',resolution:'PENDING_NARRATION'};
  arr=arr.filter(function(c){
    if(!c||c.signature!==r.signature)return false;
    if(r.slot&&c.slot===r.slot)return false;
    // Keep contrasting effect categories and reasons; bounded case memory.
    return !(c.authorization===r.authorization&&c.reason===r.reason&&
      (c.categories||[]).join(',')===r.categories.join(',')&&
      (c.authorizationVerdict||'')===verdict);
  });
  arr.push(r);
  mode1.precedents[id]=arr.slice(-6);
  return r;
}

/** Combine independent TYPES of evidence, not two equal Yes/No votes.
 * JS answers "may this effect be attempted?"; observed narration answers
 * "what appears to have happened?" A legal attempt can fail. A narrator
 * success cannot override a JS veto or grant any permanent ability.
 * AI prose is NOT a formal signed classifier verdict.
 */
function spResolveDualAudit(pending,audit) {
  var verdict=pending&&pending.jsVerdict||spJsAuthorizationVerdict(pending&&pending.authorization);
  var observed=audit||{result:'UNKNOWN',execution:'UNKNOWN',confidence:'LOW'};
  var status='SEMANTIC_UNRESOLVED',conflict=false;
  if(verdict==='DENY') {
    if(observed.result==='VIOLATION'&&observed.confidence==='HIGH') {
      status='HARD_VETO_CONFLICT';conflict=true;
    } else if(observed.result==='COMPLIANT'&&observed.execution==='FAILURE') {
      status='HARD_VETO_CONFIRMED';
    } else status='HARD_VETO_UNOBSERVED';
  } else if(verdict==='ELIGIBLE') {
    if(observed.result==='COMPLIANT'&&observed.execution==='SUCCESS')status='ELIGIBLE_SUCCESS';
    else if(observed.result==='COMPLIANT'&&observed.execution==='FAILURE')status='ELIGIBLE_FAILED';
    else status='ELIGIBLE_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN';
  }
  return {status:status,jsVerdict:verdict,observed:observed.result||'UNKNOWN',
    execution:observed.execution||'UNKNOWN',conflict:conflict,rewrite:false};
}

/** Drop dialogue, quoted hypotheticals, and metadata before attributing effects.
 * Conservatively detect only DIRECT second-person player achievements.
 * NPCs, hypotheticals, metaphors, quoted dialogue => NO success evidence.
 */
/** Extract narrator prose while excluding the player's echoed command and
 * obvious character dialogue. This is a heuristic, not a natural-language parser.
 * We never ask the AI to emit a mandatory tag or formulaic sentence.
 */
function spOutcomeNarratorProse(raw) {
  return String(raw || '')
    .replace(/“[^”]*”/g, ' ')
    .replace(/"[^"\n]*"/g, ' ')
    .replace(/^[ \t]*>[^\n]*(?:\n|$)/gm, ' ')
    .replace(/\[SP_SEM\|[^\]\n]*\]/gi, ' ');
}

/** Return explicit narrator clauses about the player, not arbitrary nearby words.
 * The model may join a decisive outcome to scenery with an em dash, semicolon,
 * or comma (e.g. "The room is unchanged—your body is still visible").
 * Preserve the whole sentence as well: splitting is evidence discovery, not an
 * instruction to the storyteller or a general grammatical parser.
 */
function spOutcomeClauses(sentence) {
  var s=String(sentence||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  var clauses=s?[s]:[];
  // A comma matters only if it introduces an actual player/observer clause;
  // ordinary descriptive commas should not manufacture extra subjects.
  var parts=s.split(/[—;]|,\s*(?=(?:(?:but|yet|and|then)\s+)?(?:you|your|everyone|anyone)\b)/i);
  for(var i=1;i<parts.length;i++) {
    var part=parts[i].trim().replace(/^(?:(?:but|yet|and|then)\s+)+/,'').trim();
    if(part)clauses.push(part);
  }
  return clauses;
}

/** Effect-specific proof that the PLAYER remained visible. This is not a
 * global keyword search: generic unchanged scenery, NPC visibility, quotation,
 * speculation, and indirect thoughts do not prove that invisibility failed.
 * Output auditing is observational and must favor UNKNOWN when uncertain.
 */
function spPlayerStillVisible(raw) {
  var narrative=spOutcomeNarratorProse(raw).toLowerCase();
  var sentences=narrative.split(/[.!?\n]+/);
  for(var i=0;i<sentences.length;i++) {
    var clauses=spOutcomeClauses(sentences[i]);
    for(var j=0;j<clauses.length;j++) {
      var q=clauses[j];
      // Direct physical observation of the player, with variable adjectives.
      if(/^your\s+(?:body|form|figure|outline|silhouette)\s+(?:is|was|remains?|stays?|continues? to be)\s+[^,;.!?]{0,85}\b(?:visible|plainly seen|in (?:plain )?(?:sight|view))\b/.test(q))return true;
      if(/^you\s+(?:are|were|remain|stay|continue to be)\s+[^,;.!?]{0,50}\b(?:visible|in (?:plain )?(?:sight|view))\b/.test(q))return true;
      // A clear witness can still see the PC, not a generic NPC discussion.
      if(/^(?:everyone|anyone|the (?:onlookers|watchers|guards))\s+(?:can|could)\s+still\s+see\s+you\b/.test(q))return true;
      if(/^your\s+(?:body|form|figure|outline|silhouette)\s+(?:never|does not|doesn't|fails? to)\s+(?:fades?|vanish(?:es)?|disappear(?:s)?)(?:\s+from\s+(?:sight|view))?\b/.test(q))return true;
      if(/^you\s+(?:do not|don't|never|cannot|can't|fail to)\s+(?:become|turn|grow)\s+invisible\b/.test(q))return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/** Small conversion helper for measurements directly attached to a player
 * displacement sentence; do NOT infer range from nearby destination scenery.
 */
function spOutcomeDistanceFt(phrase) {
  var q=String(phrase||'').toLowerCase();
  var m=q.match(/\b(\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?|one hundred|a hundred|hundred|ten|fifteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|five|six|seven|eight|nine|twelve|twenty-five|one|two|three|four)\s*(feet|foot|ft|meters?|metres?)\b/);
  if(!m)return null;
  var v=Number(m[1].replace(/,/g,''));
  if(!isFinite(v)) {
    var words={'one hundred':100,'a hundred':100,'hundred':100,'ten':10,'fifteen':15,'twenty':20,'thirty':30,'forty':40,'fifty':50,'sixty':60,'seventy':70,'eighty':80,'ninety':90,'five':5,'six':6,'seven':7,'eight':8,'nine':9,'twelve':12,'twenty-five':25,'one':1,'two':2,'three':3,'four':4};
    v=words[m[1]];
  }
  return isFinite(v)?v*(/^met/.test(m[2])?3.28084:1):null;
}

/** A coherent narrated displacement is better evidence than a magic keyword.
 * The explicit new player location must be paired with a narrated magical
 * transition. A physical walk, NPC teleport, memory, or mere imagined journey
 * must not count as a teleportation success.
 */
function spPlayerOutcomeEvidence(raw) {
  var narration=spOutcomeNarratorProse(raw);
  var segments=narration.split(/[.!?\n]+/).map(function(p){return p.trim().toLowerCase();});
  var positives={INVISIBILITY:false,TELEPORTATION:false,TELEKINESIS:false,ELEMENTAL_MAGIC:false};
  var failed=false, failedCategories={INVISIBILITY:false,TELEPORTATION:false};
  var teleportFt=null;
  var strongTransition=/\b(?:dissolv\w*|unravel\w*|dissolution|between[- ](?:state|worlds)|transit\w*|transition|materializ\w*|teleport\w*|aetheric\s+passage|aether\s+unfolds|shadow[- ]walk\w*)\b/i.test(narration);
  var shadowContext=/\bshadow\b/i.test(narration);
  var subject=/^(?:and\s+|then\s+|in (?:a|the) (?:blink|heartbeat|instant),?\s+|suddenly,?\s+|as a result,?\s+|finally,?\s+)*you\s+/;
  var possessive=/^(?:and\s+|then\s+)?your\s+(?:body|form|figure|outline)\s+/;
  var priorPlayerAttempt=false;
  for(var i=0;i<segments.length;i++) {
    var c=segments[i];if(!c)continue;
    // A generic 'nothing changes' is evidence only when preceded by a player
    // attempt in the nearby narration, not NPC machinery or scene description.
    var directFailure=/^(?:you\s+)?(?:remain\w*\s+(?:fully\s+|completely\s+|clearly\s+)?(?:visible|where you (?:stand|stood)|in (?:the same )?place)|you\s+(?:are|remain|stay)\s+(?:still\s+|fully\s+|completely\s+|clearly\s+)?visible|your\s+(?:body|form|figure|outline)\s+(?:remains|stays|is)\s+[^.!?]*\bvisible|you\s+(?:do not|don't|cannot|can't|fail to)\s+(?:teleport|vanish|disappear|become invisible))\b/.test(c);
    var explicitlyUnchanged=/\b(?:nothing happens|nothing changes|no effect|attempt (?:fails|collapses)|attempt never forms|effort fails|fails? to (?:teleport|vanish|become)|never (?:turn|become) invisible|still visible|remains? (?:fully |completely |clearly )?visible)\b/.test(c);
    // A visible body proves an invisibility attempt failed, but says nothing
    // about whether a separate teleport or telekinesis attempt succeeded.
    var directVisibilityFailure=directFailure && /\bvisible\b/.test(c);
    var genericFailure=directFailure && !directVisibilityFailure;
    if(explicitlyUnchanged && !/\b(?:still|remains?)\s+(?:fully\s+|clearly\s+|completely\s+)?visible\b/.test(c) &&
       (priorPlayerAttempt || /\byou\s+(?:try|attempt|concentrate|focus|will|strain)\b/.test(c)))genericFailure=true;
    // A natural coordinated clause need not repeat the subject: "You try to
    // teleport, but remain where you stood" is a clear movement failure.
    if(/^you\s+(?:try|attempt)\s+to\s+(?:teleport|blink|shadow.walk)\b[^.!?]{0,100}\b(?:but|yet)\s+(?:you\s+)?(?:remain|stay)\s+(?:exactly\s+)?(?:where you (?:stood|stand)|in (?:the same )?place)\b/.test(c))genericFailure=true;
    if(genericFailure)failed=true;
    if(directVisibilityFailure || /^(?:you\s+(?:are|remain|stay)\s+(?:still\s+|fully\s+|completely\s+|clearly\s+)?visible|your\s+(?:body|form|figure|outline)\s+(?:remains|stays|is)\s+[^.!?]*\bvisible)\b/.test(c)) {
      failedCategories.INVISIBILITY=true;
    }
    if(/\byou\s+(?:concentrate|focus|attempt|try|will|strain|prepare)\b/.test(c) &&
       /\b(?:invisib|vanish|disappear|fade|teleport|shadow|aether|magic|power|spell|form)\b/.test(c))priorPlayerAttempt=true;
    else if(i>0 && /\b(?:nothing changes|nothing happens)\b/.test(c))priorPlayerAttempt=false;
    // Keep direct second-person achievements only. Never infer player magic
    // from NPC speech, impersonal descriptions, or an echoed Do command.
    // Look inside natural comma/em-dash clauses while maintaining explicit
    // player attribution; this also catches "..., then you become invisible".
    var clauses=spOutcomeClauses(c);
    if(c.indexOf(', you ')>=0)clauses.push(c.slice(c.indexOf(', you ')+2));
    for(var j=0;j<clauses.length;j++) {
      var q=clauses[j], y=subject.test(q), body=possessive.test(q);
      if(!y&&!body)continue;
      var physicallyOccluded=/\b(?:behind (?:a|the)|around the corner|under the|beneath the|in the crowd|invisible to)\b/.test(q);
      // Do not reify conditional, hypothetical, or imagined effects.
      var imaginedOrConditional=/\b(?:only if|if you|in (?:your|the) imagination|in your mind|in theory|hypothetically|if only)\b/.test(q);
      if(/^(?:you\s+)?(?:try|attempt|wish|hope|intend|imagine|picture|plan|consider|feel|believe|pretend|struggle|fail|cannot|can't|do not|don't|never|might|could|would)\b/.test(q) ||
        /\b(?:do not|don't|cannot|can't|never|fail to|try to|attempt to)\b/.test(q.slice(0,50)))continue;
      if(!physicallyOccluded && !imaginedOrConditional && ((y&&/^you\s+(?:(?:suddenly|actually|now)\s+)?(?:become|turn|are rendered)\s+(?:completely\s+|fully\s+)?invisible\b/.test(q)) ||
         (y&&/^you\s+(?:vanish|disappear)\s+(?:completely\s+)?(?:from sight|from view|before their eyes)\b/.test(q)) ||
         (body&&/^your\s+(?:body|form|figure)\s+(?:vanishes|disappears|becomes invisible|fades from (?:sight|view))\b/.test(q)) ||
         // A state description is as valid as a transition verb. The narrator
         // need not say "become"; "you are now invisible" is still an outcome.
         (y&&/^you\s+(?:are|remain|have become)\s+(?:(?:now|fully|completely|entirely|totally|actually)\s+)*invisible\b/.test(q)) ||
         (body&&/^your\s+(?:body|form|figure)\s+(?:is|becomes|turns)\s+(?:(?:now|fully|completely|entirely|totally)\s+)*invisible\b/.test(q)) ||
         (y&&/^you\s+fade\s+(?:(?:completely|entirely|fully)\s+)?(?:from\s+sight|out\s+of\s+view)\b/.test(q))))positives.INVISIBILITY=true;
      if(y&&/^you\s+(?:(?:suddenly|instantly|successfully)\s+)?(?:teleport|blink|reappear|materialize)\b/.test(q)) {
        positives.TELEPORTATION=true;
      }
      if(y&&/^you\s+arrive\s+instantly\b/.test(q) && strongTransition && shadowContext)positives.TELEPORTATION=true;
      // Natural-language paraphrase: magical dissolution followed by the
      // character explicitly occupying a new location relative to the start.
      // 'You stand fifteen feet...' alone is not magical transportation.
      var relocated=y && /^you\s+(?:stand|find yourself|are now|emerge|arrive|appear|end up)\b/.test(q) &&
        /\bfrom\s+(?:where you (?:began|stood|started)|the (?:place|spot) you stood|your (?:starting|original) (?:position|location))\b/.test(q);
      if(relocated && strongTransition && shadowContext) {
        var distance=spOutcomeDistanceFt(q);
        if(distance!==null){positives.TELEPORTATION=true;teleportFt=distance;}
      }
      if(y&&/^you\s+(?:telekinetically\s+)?(?:levitate|psychokinetically lift)\b/.test(q))positives.TELEKINESIS=true;
      if(y&&/^you\s+(?:conjure|summon|ignite|create)\s+(?:a\s+)?(?:fireball|flame|fire)\b/.test(q))positives.ELEMENTAL_MAGIC=true;
    }
  }
  // Do not promote visibility evidence to a failure of unrelated powers.
  if(spPlayerStillVisible(narration))failedCategories.INVISIBILITY=true;
  return {success:positives,failed:failed,failedCategories:failedCategories,teleportFt:teleportFt};
}

/** Require a narrated player displacement above the limit, rather than
 * mistaking an attempted target distance for a completed journey.
 */
function spNarratedExcessRange(text, capFt, evidence) {
  if(typeof capFt!=='number')return false;
  var ev=evidence||spPlayerOutcomeEvidence(text);
  if(ev.teleportFt!==null && ev.teleportFt>capFt && ev.success.TELEPORTATION)return true;
  var s=spOutcomeNarratorProse(text).toLowerCase();
  var re=/\byou\s+(?:teleport|blink|reappear|materialize)\s+(?:(?:a distance of|for|across|over)\s+)?(\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?|one hundred|a hundred)\s*(feet|foot|ft|meters?|metres?)\b/g, m;
  while((m=re.exec(s))) {
    var dist=spOutcomeDistanceFt(m[1]+' '+m[2]);
    if(dist!==null && dist>capFt)return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/** Observational audit, never an authorization source. Require concrete
 * narrative evidence and preserve UNKNOWN when the storyteller is ambiguous.
 */
function spAuditOutcome(pending, narration) {
  if(!pending||pending.status!=='REGISTERED'||!pending.powerSignature) {
    return {result:'SKIPPED',confidence:'NONE',execution:'UNKNOWN',reason:'NO_CURRENT_REGISTERED_ATTEMPT'};
  }
  var a=pending.authorization||{},ev=spPlayerOutcomeEvidence(narration);
  var category=String(a.reason||'');
