  var blocked=a.result==='BLOCK_CATEGORY'||a.result==='BLOCK_CONDITION'||a.result==='BLOCK_LIMIT';
  var requested=pending.requestedCategories||[];
  var relevantFailure=ev.failed ||
    (ev.failedCategories.INVISIBILITY && (requested.indexOf('INVISIBILITY')!==-1 || a.reason==='INVISIBILITY'));
  var successTypes=Object.keys(ev.success).filter(function(k){return ev.success[k];});
  var forbidden=[];
  if(a.result==='BLOCK_CATEGORY' && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(ev.success,category))forbidden=[category];
  else if(a.reason==='RANGE' && requested.indexOf('TELEPORTATION')!==-1 &&
          spNarratedExcessRange(narration,a.cap,ev))forbidden=['TELEPORTATION'];
  else if(a.reason==='VISIBLE_DESTINATION' && requested.indexOf('TELEPORTATION')!==-1 &&
          /\b(?:unseen|cannot see|could not see|through the wall|other side of (?:the|a) wall|windowless wall)\b/i.test(narration))forbidden=['TELEPORTATION'];
  else if(a.reason==='WEIGHT' && requested.indexOf('TELEKINESIS')!==-1)forbidden=['TELEKINESIS'];
  else if(blocked && a.reason==='DURATION')forbidden=requested.slice();
  var violated=forbidden.some(function(k){return !!ev.success[k];});
  if(violated)return {result:'VIOLATION',confidence:'HIGH',execution:'SUCCESS',reason:'DENIED_PLAYER_EFFECT_NARRATED',effect:forbidden.filter(function(k){return ev.success[k];})[0]};
  if(blocked && relevantFailure)return {result:'COMPLIANT',confidence:'HIGH',execution:successTypes.length?'PARTIAL_SUCCESS':'FAILURE',reason:'DENIED_EFFECT_FAILED'};
  if(!blocked && successTypes.some(function(k){return requested.indexOf(k)!==-1;}))return {result:'COMPLIANT',confidence:'HIGH',execution:'SUCCESS',reason:'AUTHORIZED_PLAYER_EFFECT_NARRATED'};
  if(!blocked && relevantFailure)return {result:'COMPLIANT',confidence:'MEDIUM',execution:'FAILURE',reason:'ALLOWED_ATTEMPT_FAILED'};
  return {result:'UNKNOWN',confidence:'LOW',execution:'UNKNOWN',reason:'NO_UNAMBIGUOUS_OUTCOME'};
}

/** Select at most two definition-matched examples as ADVISORY few-shot hints.
 * Only normalized labels are injected; never raw prior narrative or commands.
 * Precedents cannot change authorization, even after repeated violations.
 */
function spPrecedentHints(mode1,power,actionText,authorization) {
  var cases=mode1&&mode1.precedents&&power&&mode1.precedents[power.id];
  if(!Array.isArray(cases)||!cases.length)return {text:'',used:0};
  var sig=spPowerSignature(power),cats=spEffectLedger(power,'do',actionText).effects.map(function(e){return e.category;});
  var desired=spJsAuthorizationVerdict(authorization);
  // Without a matched current effect, no prior authorization example is relevant.
  if(!cats.length)return {text:'',used:0};
  // A v0.8.0 'JS_PRECHECK' record could mistakenly declare a whole mixed
  // request ELIGIBLE. Keep the old data for diagnostics, QUARANTINE it from
  // semantic guidance until re-evaluated under this version's effect ledger.
  var pool=cases.filter(function(c){
    if(!c||c.signature!==sig||c.source!=='JS_EFFECT_LEDGER_V2' ||
       c.verifiedAuthorization!==true || c.authorizationVerdict!==desired)return false;
    if(desired==='DENY'){
      // Do not apply a compound INVISIBILITY veto to a standalone teleport.
      // Category/condition/limit denials need the same particular rule.
      if(c.reason!==authorization.reason)return false;
      if(!cats.some(function(k){return (c.deniedCategories||[]).indexOf(k)>=0;}))return false;
      return true;
    }
    if(desired==='ELIGIBLE'){
      // NEVER use an entire mixed DENY as a generic positive authorization.
      if(c.authorizationVerdict!=='ELIGIBLE')return false;
      if(!cats.some(function(k){return (c.eligibleCategories||[]).indexOf(k)>=0;}))return false;
      // Unclassified effects in an action cannot become an ALL-ELIGIBLE hint.
      return c.unresolvedCount===0;
    }
    return false;
  });
  pool=pool.map(function(c){
    var score=0;
    var scope=desired==='DENY'?(c.deniedCategories||[]):(c.eligibleCategories||[]);
    for(var j=0;j<cats.length;j++)if(scope.indexOf(cats[j])!==-1)score+=6;
    if(c.reason===authorization.reason)score+=4;
    if(c.authorization===authorization.result)score+=2;
    return {c:c,score:score};
  }).filter(function(o){return o.score>0;}).sort(function(a,b){
    return b.score-a.score||b.c.generation-a.c.generation;
  });
  var selected=[];
  for(var i=0;i<pool.length&&selected.length<2;i++){
    var c=pool[i].c;
    if(!selected.length||c.authorization!==selected[0].authorization||c.reason!==selected[0].reason)selected.push(c);
  }
  if(!selected.length)return {text:'',used:0};
  var out=['PRECEDENTS (ADVISORY: current registry and hard JS limits always win; prior stories NEVER grant abilities):'];
  selected.forEach(function(c,i){
    var scope=desired==='DENY'?(c.deniedCategories||[]):(c.eligibleCategories||[]);
    out.push('PRECEDENT_CASE_'+(i+1)+'=effect '+(scope.join('+')||'REGISTERED_EFFECT')+'; '+
      (desired==='DENY'?'DENIED by registered scope/rule':'ELIGIBLE candidate (success never guaranteed)')+
      '; decision '+c.authorization+' ('+c.reason+').');
  });
  return {text:out.join('\n'),used:selected.length};
}

/** Attach an optional NARRATIVE observation to this turn's saved JS decision.
 * Never recompute or alter authorization from an AI-generated paragraph.
 * If Output replays (retry), replace the same slot rather than duplicate it.
 */
function spSavePrecedent(mode1,pending,audit,resolution) {
  if(!mode1||!pending||pending.status!=='REGISTERED'||!pending.trigger||!pending.powerSignature)return null;
  if(!mode1.precedents||typeof mode1.precedents!=='object')mode1.precedents={};
  var id=pending.trigger,arr=Array.isArray(mode1.precedents[id])?mode1.precedents[id]:[];
  var idx=-1;
  for(var i=0;i<arr.length;i++)if(arr[i]&&arr[i].signature===pending.powerSignature&&
      arr[i].slot===pending.actionSlot&&arr[i].generation===pending.generation){idx=i;break;}
  var r=idx>=0?arr[idx]:spRecordAuthorizationPrecedent(mode1,pending);
  if(!r)return null;
  r.outcome=audit&&audit.result||'UNKNOWN';
  r.execution=audit&&audit.execution||'UNKNOWN';
  r.confidence=audit&&audit.confidence||'LOW';
  r.resolution=resolution&&resolution.status||'UNASSESSED';
  return r;
}

// ---- MODE 1 SURGE SUPPRESSOR (v0.5.0) ----
// Decides allowed OUTCOME SCOPE, not whether a character can try something.
// A strict BLOCK means the prohibited supernatural effect has ZERO expression,
// including residual target movement, warmth or a psychic connection.
// GUARD handles no-trigger turns: normal actions proceed, supernatural effects do not.
// REVIEW stays genuinely uncertain. DAMPEN requires independently grounded,
// separable legal effect; never scale down an out-of-range target or heavy object.
/** True only when a nearby target can legally travel partway toward an out-of-range destination. */
function spMode1SafeRangeDampen(actionText, auth) {
  if (!auth || auth.reason !== 'RANGE') return false;
  var s = String(actionText || '').toLowerCase();
  var startsNear = /\b(?:beside|next to|near) (?:me|you)\b/.test(s) ||
    /\b(?:in (?:my|your) hand|at (?:my|your) feet)\b/.test(s);
  if (!startsNear) return false;
  // Separate current proximity from requested distant ENDPOINT.
  // A target already at 200 ft does not qualify for this exemption.
  if (/\b(?:located|sitting|resting|positioned|standing|already)\b[^.!?;]{0,35}\b\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?\s*(?:feet|foot|ft|meters?|metres?)\s+away/.test(s)) return false;
  if (!/\b(?:move|push|guide|send|slide|carry)\b/.test(s)) return false;
  var endpoint = /\b(?:to|toward|until|up to)\s+(?:a\s+(?:point|spot|position)\s+)?(\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:feet|foot|ft|meters?|metres?)\s+away\b/.exec(s);
  return !!(endpoint && Number(endpoint[1].replace(/,/g, '')) > 0);
}

/** Convert authorization into scoped ALLOW / BLOCK / DAMPEN / GUARD / GATE / REVIEW.
 * GATE does NOT mean the whole request is illegal: it withholds *only* newly
 * requested unclassified supernatural manifestations. Independently proven
 * capability effects and ordinary actions remain usable. This is an instruction
 * boundary for the storyteller, not a guaranteed post-generation interceptor.
 */
function spMode1Suppress(actionType, actionText, decision, authorization, effectLedger) {
  var a = authorization || { result: 'REVIEW', reason: 'NO_AUTHORIZATION' };
  if (a.result === 'NO_TRIGGER') {
    return { result: 'GUARD', reason: 'NO_TRIGGER', scope: 'ORDINARY_ONLY', hard: true };
  }
  if (a.result === 'UNKNOWN_TRIGGER') {
    return { result: 'BLOCK', reason: 'UNREGISTERED_POWER', scope: 'EXTRAORDINARY_ONLY', hard: true };
  }
  if (a.result === 'BLOCK_CATEGORY' || a.result === 'BLOCK_CONDITION') {
    return { result: 'BLOCK', reason: a.reason, scope: a.result === 'BLOCK_CONDITION' ? 'VIOLATED_CONDITION' : 'FORBIDDEN_CATEGORY', hard: true };
  }
  if (a.result === 'BLOCK_LIMIT') {
    // A simultaneous target ceiling permits smaller-scale manifestations,
    // but never the requested above-cap group. Preserve creative partial play.
    if (a.reason === 'TARGETS' && typeof a.cap==='number' && a.cap>=1) {
      return {result:'DAMPEN',reason:'TARGET_COUNT',scope:'REGISTERED_TARGETS_ONLY',
        maxTargets:a.cap,requestedTargets:a.observed,hard:true};
    }
    if (decision && decision.power && decision.power.kind === 'TELEKINESIS' && spMode1SafeRangeDampen(actionText, a)) {
      return { result: 'DAMPEN', reason: 'DESTINATION_RANGE', scope: 'IN_RANGE_SEGMENT_ONLY',
        maxRangeFt: a.cap, requestedRangeFt: a.observed, hard: true };
    }
    return { result: 'BLOCK', reason: a.reason, scope: 'VIOLATING_EFFECT', hard: true };
  }
  // Explicitly detected but unclassified *additional* magic has no license to
  // manifest. This is not a category veto: its category is not known. Keep the
  // registered effect(s) eligible while quarantining the missing capability.
  // Perform this check before CANDIDATE too: a known first effect must not hide
  // a second, unresolved act even if an upstream checker changes its verdict.
  if ((effectLedger && effectLedger.unresolved && effectLedger.unresolved.length) ||
      a.reason === 'UNCLASSIFIED_EFFECT') {
    return { result: 'GATE', reason: 'UNCLASSIFIED_EFFECT',
      scope: 'UNCLASSIFIED_EXTRA_ONLY', hard: true };
  }
  if (a.result === 'CANDIDATE') {
    return { result: 'ALLOW', reason: 'CANDIDATE_WITH_LIMITS', scope: 'REGISTERED_POWER', hard: false };
  }
  return { result: 'REVIEW', reason: a.reason || 'UNCERTAIN', scope: 'KNOWN_POWERS_ONLY', hard: false };
}

/** Build current-turn AI instructions with the chosen power, limits and suppression decision. */
function spMode1ContextPrompt(actionType, actionText, decision, sp, authorization, suppression, includeSemantic, precedentNotes, effectLedger) {
  var lines = [
    '[SURGE PROTECTOR / MODE 1 / INTERNAL]',
    'Current PLAYER power authority comes ONLY from registered state abilities and THIS turn valid #trigger. Story history does NOT grant or upgrade PLAYER powers.',
    'The player may freely attempt ordinary physical/social/learned actions without triggers. NPC and environmental supernatural effects are not automatically forbidden.',
    'Player can ATTEMPT anything; deny only unauthorized supernatural RESULTS. Do not rewrite the action as if it were never attempted.',
    'ACTION_TYPE=' + actionType.toUpperCase(),
    'ACTION=' + JSON.stringify(spBrief(actionText, 300)),
    'TRIGGER_STATUS=' + decision.status,
    'AUTH_RESULT=' + authorization.result,
    'AUTH_REASON=' + authorization.reason,
    'SUPPRESSION=' + suppression.result,
    'SUPPRESSION_SCOPE=' + suppression.scope
  ];
  if ((decision.status === 'REGISTERED' || decision.status === 'LEVEL_DENIED') && decision.power) {
    var p = decision.power;
    lines.push('ACTIVE_POWER=' + p.name + (p.catalogType === 'spell' ? '; BASE_SPELL_LEVEL=' + p.baseLevel + '; MAX_CAST_LEVEL=' + p.maxCastLevel : '; LEVEL=' + p.level + '/10') + '; TRIGGER=#' + p.id + '; power persists through failed attempts.');
    lines.push('SCOPE=' + p.description);
    if (p.catalogType === 'spell') lines.push('CAST_LEVEL=' + decision.castLevel + '; BASE_SPELL_LEVEL=' + p.baseLevel + '; MAX_REGISTERED_CAST_LEVEL=' + p.maxCastLevel + '; NO spell-slot accounting.');
    if (p.kind === 'FIREBALL' && decision.castLevel != null && decision.status === 'REGISTERED') lines.push('FIREBALL_DAMAGE=' + (8 + decision.castLevel - 3) + 'd6 fire (Dexterity saving throw; half on successful save).');
    if (p.kind === 'CURE_WOUNDS' && decision.castLevel != null && decision.status === 'REGISTERED') lines.push('CURE_WOUNDS_HEAL=' + decision.castLevel + 'd8 + spellcasting ability modifier; requires physical touch.');
    lines.push('HARD_LIMITS=' + (Array.isArray(p.limits) ? p.limits.join('; ') : 'No specific numerical caps defined; no unlimited effects implied.'));
    if (p.exclusions) lines.push('EXCLUSIONS=' + p.exclusions);
    if (p.mechanics) lines.push('SPELL_MECHANICS=' + p.mechanics);
    lines.push('Activation never authorizes other categories or exceeds these limits. No automatic success.');
    // Player-authored qualitative limits are meaningful but are NOT invented
    // numerical ceilings. Challenge ambitious overreach in fiction, not in UI.
    if (typeof info==='undefined' || !info || typeof info.maxChars!=='number' || info.maxChars>3000)
      lines.push('PLAY_STYLE=Respect the player-authored power scope in spirit and explicit limits in letter. Routine in-scope uses need no invented struggle, cost, or failure; welcome creative and clever physical applications. Ambitious uses may encounter proportionate resistance; do not erase obstacles by granting more power than described. Convey limitations through natural story, never rules talk.');
    var pressure=spNarrativeScopePressure(p,actionText);
    if(pressure && (typeof info==='undefined' || !info || typeof info.maxChars!=='number' || info.maxChars>3000)){
      lines.push('SCOPE_PRESSURE='+pressure.severity+' anchors='+pressure.anchors.join('+')+' scale='+pressure.scale+'. The requested magnitude greatly exceeds the player-described scope. Do not automatically grant the full massive result; portray believable strain, fragmentary/local effects, failed reach or an interesting obstacle as appropriate. No invented exact hard cap or mandatory penalty.');
    }

  } else if (decision.status === 'UNKNOWN') {
    lines.push('UNREGISTERED_TRIGGER=#' + decision.trigger + '; grants NO power.');
  } else {
    lines.push('NO_VALID_POWER_TRIGGER: no extraordinary PLAYER power is authorized this turn.');
  }
  if (authorization.result === 'BLOCK_CONDITION') {
    lines.push('DETERMINISTIC CONDITION VETO: ' + authorization.reason + '. Explicit mandatory condition is violated.');
  } else if (authorization.result === 'BLOCK_CATEGORY') {
    lines.push('DETERMINISTIC CATEGORY VETO: ' + authorization.reason + ' not covered by power.');
  } else if (authorization.result === 'BLOCK_LIMIT') {
    lines.push('DETERMINISTIC LIMIT VETO: ' + authorization.reason + ' requested=' + authorization.observed + authorization.unit + ' cap=' + authorization.cap + authorization.unit + '.');
  }
  if (suppression.result === 'BLOCK') {
    lines.push('HARD BLOCK: NO manifestation of forbidden supernatural effect, even a partial one: no target trembling/motion, magic sparks/heat, psychic sensations or leaked knowledge CAUSALLY due to attempted power. The ordinary physical attempt may still occur; independent environmental effects are fine.');
    lines.push('Do not suggest the prohibited power briefly answered, or that old unauthorized events prove it exists. Never fabricate PLAYER power acquisition.');
  } else if (suppression.result === 'DAMPEN') {
    if (suppression.scope === 'REGISTERED_TARGETS_ONLY') {
      lines.push('BOUNDED TARGETS: the player may influence at most '+suppression.maxTargets+' mind/target(s) simultaneously, not the requested '+suppression.requestedTargets+'. The requested mass effect cannot manifest. Natural failure, a brief fragmentary connection with a permissible subset, or an obstacle are possible; do not guarantee even partial success or invent extra abilities.');
    } else {
      lines.push('BOUNDED DAMPEN: target begins near player; permit telekinetic movement ONLY to a maximum of ' + suppression.maxRangeFt + ' feet FROM PLAYER. Do not affect anything beyond that hard radius or imply continued remote influence. Physical obstacles and other limits still apply; partial success is not guaranteed.');
    }
  } else if (suppression.result === 'GUARD') {
    lines.push('NO-TRIGGER GUARD: allow normal actions unaffected; any attempted PLAYER extraordinary outcome must have ZERO supernatural manifestation. Do not invent powers.');
  } else if (suppression.result === 'ALLOW') {
    lines.push('ALLOW candidate effect ONLY within ALL registered limits, prerequisites, and story circumstances. No new powers inferred.');
  } else if (suppression.result === 'GATE') {
    lines.push('CAPABILITY GATE ACTIVE: recognized eligible effects may proceed within registered rules. The separate UNCLASSIFIED supernatural effect has NO permission to manifest. The player may attempt it; narrate effort, frustration, or mundane consequences, but withhold any new extraordinary result.');
  } else {
    lines.push('REVIEW: do not infer powers from ambiguous descriptions. Permit mundane attempts; only clearly in-scope registered supernatural effects can succeed.');
  }
  // Escalate guidance only for mixed or incomplete coverage. Do not burden
  // the narrator with an assessment protocol on simple recognized requests.
  if(effectLedger && (effectLedger.effects.length>1 || effectLedger.unresolved.length)) {
    lines.push('EFFECT_LEDGER=' + effectLedger.effects.map(function(e){return e.category+':'+e.verdict;}).join('; ') +
      (effectLedger.unresolved.length?'; UNCLASSIFIED_ADDITIONAL_EFFECT':'') + '.');
    lines.push('EFFECT POLICY: permit each independently eligible effect within its rules; deny each explicitly forbidden effect. Do not discard a separable legal part merely because another part is denied.');
    if(effectLedger.unresolved.length) {
      lines.push('REGISTERED_CAPABILITIES='+(effectLedger.granted.length?effectLedger.granted.join(','):'NONE_IDENTIFIED')+'.');
      lines.push('GATED_UNCLASSIFIED_CLAUSES='+JSON.stringify(effectLedger.unresolved)+'.');
      lines.push('CAPABILITY GATE — UNCLASSIFIED effect is NOT AUTHORIZED to manifest, even if its phrasing sounds imaginative or a prior story suggests it worked. Do not turn ritual, veiling, altered attention, imperceptibility, or any unrelated extra power into a success without a separately registered capability. Preserve all independent legal effects and ordinary actions.');
    }
  }
  if (precedentNotes) lines.push(precedentNotes);
  lines.push('Apply suppression silently. Do not print the above markers or decisions as story text.');
  lines.push('[/SURGE PROTECTOR]');
  // Keep the FIRST-LINE tag reminder as the LAST instruction in Context.
  // This improves salience without changing the already working hard vetoes.
  if (includeSemantic !== false && decision.status === 'REGISTERED') lines.push(spSemanticPrompt());
  return lines.join('\n');
}

SP_SPELL_PROFILE_EXPANSION.forEach(function(profile){
  for(var i=0;i<SP_SPELL_INDEX.length;i++) {
    var item=SP_SPELL_INDEX[i];
    if(item.id!==profile.id || item.status==='PILOT_RULES_PROFILE')continue;
    item.status=profile.status;
    item.kind=profile.kind;
    item.limits=profile.limits;
    item.description=profile.description;
    item.exclusions=profile.exclusions;
    item.mechanics=profile.mechanics;
    break;
  }
});

not with spell rank."],"ray-of-frost":["Hinder a fleeing enemy with a cold ray; slow a charging creature on a hit.","Cantrip damage rises with character level 5/11/17: 2d8/3d8/4d8; speed reduction stays 10 ft."],"light":["Illuminate a sword or pebble; light a dark hallway by touching an object.","No higher-rank scaling; 20-ft bright and 20-ft dim radius, up to one hour."],"detect-magic":["Search a chamber for nearby magical effects; inspect a visible enchanted object.","No higher-rank scaling; sensing requires concentration and can be blocked by material barriers."],"magic-missile":["Send all darts at one visible enemy; split darts among several visible enemies.","Three darts at rank 1 plus one dart per higher rank (rank 3: five darts, rank 5: seven)."],"shield":["React to an incoming sword strike; stop Magic Missile when targeted.","No higher-rank scaling; +5 AC until next turn and Magic Missile immunity during effect."],"invisibility":["Hide an ally while scouting; conceal additional companions when upcast.","One target at rank 2 plus one extra target per rank above 2 (rank 4: three targets)."],"fly":["Give an ally aerial mobility; let multiple allies fly when using a higher rank.","One touched willing creature at rank 3; +1 creature per rank above 3 (rank 5: three)."],"counterspell":["Interrupt an enemy mage casting; counter a higher-ranked spell with a check.","Rank 3 automatically counters spells of rank 3 or lower; higher cast ranks raise automatic threshold."],"minor-illusion":["Make a static illusory crate; imitate a brief sound like a knock.","No higher-rank scaling; the spell permits sound OR a static image, never both at once."],"message":["Whisper instructions unseen to a companion; three noninstantaneous effects may coexist at most."],"feather-fall":["Slow a falling ally; protect up to five people dropping from a ledge.","No higher-rank scaling; up to five falling creatures within 60 ft."],"silent-image":["Project a moving decoy in a 15-ft cube; mimic an object’s appearance.","No higher-rank scaling; visual only and concentration required."],"healing-word":["Heal an ally at range during combat; restore consciousness by healing hit points.","Rank 1 heals 1d4 + modifier; each higher rank adds 1d4 (rank 4: 4d4 + modifier)."]};
SP_SPELL_INDEX.forEach(function(item) {
  if(item.status!=='PILOT_RULES_PROFILE')return;
  var g=SP_SPELL_USAGE_GUIDES[item.id];
  if(g){item.usageExamples=g[0];item.scalingNote=g[1];}
});

  var item=SP_SPELL_INDEX.filter(function(p){return p.id===profile.id;})[0];
  if(!item || item.status==='PILOT_RULES_PROFILE')return;
  ['status','kind','description','exclusions','mechanics','limits','usageExamples','scalingNote']
   .forEach(function(k){item[k]=profile[k];});

var SP_CATALOG_VERSION = '1.1.0';

/** Normalize display names to compact case-insensitive registry/lookup keys. */
function spKey(s) { return String(s || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''); }
/** Lookup by name, id, trigger or alias without mutating the catalog. */
function spFindPreset(catalog, raw) {
  var key = spKey(raw);
  for (var i=0;i<catalog.length;i++) {
    var p=catalog[i];
    if (spKey(p.id)===key || spKey(p.name)===key || spKey(p.trigger)===key ||
      (p.aliases || []).some(function(a){return spKey(a)===key;})) return p;
  }
  return null;
}
// Power presets may interpolate intermediate levels. Quantities like 6.71 ft
// are false precision; present and store game-friendly numbers instead.
// Authored anchors (including the v0.6 100lb/50ft TK fixture) are untouched.
/** Round interpolated estimates to gameplay-friendly values (only when creating new registrations). */
function spFriendlyNumber(number, key) {
  if (typeof number !== 'number' || !isFinite(number) || number <= 0) return number;
  if (/^(maxTargets|maxForms|severityRank)$/.test(key)) return Math.max(1, Math.round(number));
  var step = number < 5 ? 0.5 : number < 100 ? 5 : number < 1000 ? 25 : number < 10000 ? 100 : Math.pow(10, Math.floor(Math.log10(number)) - 1);
  return Math.max(step, Math.round(number / step) * step);
}
/** Apply rounding to interpolated tier caps; authored anchor caps remain untouched. */
function spFriendlyCaps(caps, interpolated) {
  if (!interpolated) return caps;
  Object.keys(caps).forEach(function(key){ caps[key] = spFriendlyNumber(caps[key], key); });
  return caps;
}
/** Render a short one-line list of recognized numerical caps for confirmations. */
function spCompactCaps(caps) {
  var labels = {massLb:'mass',rangeFt:'range',areaRadiusFt:'radius',durationSeconds:'duration',maxTargets:'targets',speedMph:'speed',altitudeFt:'altitude'};
  var units = {massLb:' lb',rangeFt:' ft',areaRadiusFt:' ft',durationSeconds:' sec',maxTargets:'',speedMph:' mph',altitudeFt:' ft'};
  return Object.keys(caps || {}).map(function(k){return (labels[k] || k) + ' ' + caps[k] + (units[k] || '');}).join('; ');
}
// Match ONLY explicit maximum statements. An example, observed distance, or
// isolated number is not a hard cap. Ambiguous/contradictory maxima remain prose.
/** Parse explicit maxima in custom prose; ambiguous/contradictory values are not guessed. */
function spExtractCustomCaps(description) {
  var text = String(description || '').toLowerCase();
  var found = {rangeFt:[], massLb:[], durationSeconds:[], maxTargets:[]};
  var warnings = [];
  var quantity = '(\\d[\\d,]*(?:\\.\\d+)?)\\s*';
  var patterns = {
    rangeFt: [
      '(?:\\bwithin\\s+|\\bup to\\s+|\\bat most\\s+|\\bno more than\\s+|\\bnot beyond\\s+|\\bcannot (?:reach|go|extend|operate|work|move|travel|teleport) (?:more than |beyond |farther than )|\\bmax(?:imum)?\\s+(?:range|distance|reach)\\s*(?::|of|is)?\\s*)' + quantity + '(feet|foot|ft|metres?|meters?|yards?|yd|miles?|mi)\\b',
      '(?:\\brange\\s*(?:of|is|:|up to)\\s*|\\bdistance limit\\s*(?:of|is|:)\\s*)' + quantity + '(feet|foot|ft|metres?|meters?|yards?|yd|miles?|mi)\\b'
    ],
    massLb: [
      '(?:\\bup to\\s+|\\bat most\\s+|\\bno more than\\s+|\\bnot over\\s+|\\bmaximum\\s+(?:weight|mass|load)\\s*(?::|of|is)?\\s*|\\b(?:weight|mass|load) limit\\s*(?::|of|is)?\\s*)' + quantity + '(pounds?|lbs?|kilograms?|kgs?)\\b',
      '(?:\\bweighing\\s+(?:at most|no more than|up to)\\s+)' + quantity + '(pounds?|lbs?|kilograms?|kgs?)\\b'
    ],
    durationSeconds: [
      '(?:\\bfor up to\\s+|\\bfor at most\\s+|\\bfor no more than\\s+|\\bno longer than\\s+|\\bmaximum\\s+duration\\s*(?::|of|is)?\\s*|\\bduration limit\\s*(?::|of|is)?\\s*)' + quantity + '(seconds?|secs?|minutes?|mins?|hours?|hrs?)\\b'
    ]
  };
  Object.keys(patterns).forEach(function(kind){
    patterns[kind].forEach(function(pattern){
      var re = new RegExp(pattern, 'gi'), match;
      while ((match = re.exec(text)) !== null) {
        // E.g. "cannot function within 30 ft" describes a minimum, not max.
        if (/\b(?:cannot|can't|must not|never|does not|doesn't|will not|won't)\b[^.!?;]{0,45}$/.test(text.slice(Math.max(0,match.index-65),match.index))) continue;
        var amount = Number(match[1].replace(/,/g, ''));
        if (!isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) continue;
        var unit = match[2].toLowerCase(), value = amount;
        if (kind === 'rangeFt') {
          if (/^(?:metres?|meters?)$/.test(unit)) value *= 3.280839895;
          if (/^(?:yards?|yd)$/.test(unit)) value *= 3;
          if (/^(?:miles?|mi)$/.test(unit)) value *= 5280;
        } else if (kind === 'massLb') {
          if (/^(?:kilograms?|kgs?)$/.test(unit)) value *= 2.20462262185;
        } else {
          if (/^(?:minutes?|mins?)$/.test(unit)) value *= 60;
          if (/^(?:hours?|hrs?)$/.test(unit)) value *= 3600;
        }
        if (value > 1e18) continue;
        value = Math.round(value * 100) / 100;
        found[kind].push(value);
      }
    });
  });
  // Explicit count ceilings only, including "one mind at a time".
  // An example involving one person without "at a time" is NOT a hard maximum.
  var countRe=/\b(?:up to|at most|no more than|maximum of|only)\s+(\d[\d,]*)\s+(?:minds?|people|persons?|targets?|individuals?)\b/gi, cm;
  while((cm=countRe.exec(text))!==null){
    var targetCount=Number(cm[1].replace(/,/g,''));
    if(Number.isSafeInteger(targetCount)&&targetCount>0)found.maxTargets.push(targetCount);
  }
  if(/\b(?:one|a single)\s+(?:mind|person|individual|target)\s+at\s+a\s+time\b/.test(text))found.maxTargets.push(1);
  var caps={};
  Object.keys(found).forEach(function(key){
    var values=found[key].filter(function(n,i,a){return a.indexOf(n) === i;});
    if(values.length === 1) caps[key]=values[0];
    else if(values.length > 1) warnings.push('Conflicting '+key+' limits were not auto-applied');
  });
  return {caps:caps, warnings:warnings};
}
/** Convert numerical cap keys into human-readable label/unit sentences. */
function spCapStrings(caps) {
  var labels = {massLb:'Maximum mass',rangeFt:'Maximum range',areaRadiusFt:'Maximum area radius',
    volumeGallons:'Maximum volume',durationSeconds:'Maximum duration',maxTargets:'Maximum targets',
    speedMph:'Maximum speed',altitudeFt:'Maximum altitude',maxForms:'Maximum forms',
    lookaheadSeconds:'Maximum lookahead',thicknessFt:'Maximum thickness',severityRank:'Severity ceiling'};
  var units = {massLb:' lb',rangeFt:' ft',areaRadiusFt:' ft',volumeGallons:' gallons',durationSeconds:' seconds',
    maxTargets:'',speedMph:' mph',altitudeFt:' ft',maxForms:'',lookaheadSeconds:' seconds',thicknessFt:' ft',severityRank:''};
  return Object.keys(caps).map(function(k){return (labels[k] || k) + ' ' + caps[k] + (units[k] || '');});
}
// UX-01C: Core *functions* are distinct from milestone examples.
// Every level describes uses AND its qualitative ceiling; numbers still come
// only from catalog caps. These phrases do not independently authorize anything.
var SP_POWER_CORE_FUNCTIONS = {
  "telekinesis": "Lift, push, pull, carry, or reposition visible external objects by mental force",
  "telepathy": "Send, receive, or exchange thoughts; probe unshared thoughts only if permitted by tier and resistance",
  "pyrokinesis": "Ignite, heat, warm, or shape bounded ordinary fire within this tier’s effect scale",
  "cryokinesis": "Cool objects, form frost, freeze available moisture, or shape ordinary ice within this tier’s scale",
  "electrokinesis": "Generate or redirect electricity through sparks, currents, or discharges within this tier’s scale",
  "hydrokinesis": "Move, lift, direct, or reshape available liquid water within this tier’s volume and range",
  "aerokinesis": "Create, redirect, or intensify air movement within the wind scale of this tier",
  "geokinesis": "Shift, lift, compact, or shape ordinary loose earth and stone within this tier’s mass and range",
  "flight": "Hover, ascend, descend, turn, or travel through the air within this tier’s speed and altitude",
  "teleportation": "Instantly relocate yourself to an allowed destination within this tier’s distance and destination rules",
  "super_strength": "Lift, push, pull, carry, or strike through enhanced bodily strength within this tier’s mass",
  "super_speed": "Run or maneuver through enhanced bodily speed up to this tier’s movement scale",
  "regeneration": "Automatically heal only your own wounds and, at high tiers, regrow limbs at the recovery speed this tier allows",
  "healing": "Soothe or repair another living target’s injuries within the severity and target scope of this tier",
  "invulnerability": "Resist physical injuries and impacts only up to the kind of threat demonstrated at this tier",
  "invisibility": "Conceal your visible appearance to the degree this tier allows, without masking other senses",
  "illusion": "Create nonphysical images and sounds from Level 1, with additional simulated senses unlocked at the specified tier",
  "shapeshifting": "Transform your body into allowed forms, with the natural capabilities of the form; Level 10 alone permits finite innate magical creature traits",
  "phasing": "Make permitted parts of your own body intangible to pass through ordinary matter up to this tier’s thickness",
  "precognition": "Perceive conditional possibilities or danger within the time horizon this tier allows",
  "mind_control": "Attempt influence only within this tier’s command scope, with target resistance and safeguards remaining applicable",
  "force_fields": "Create and sustain protective force barriers against the category of impact this tier allows",
  "energy_projection": "Emit focused bursts or beams of the registered energy type at this tier’s permitted strength",
  "summoning": "Conjure size-dependent numbers of temporary allies, from Tiny helpers to finite magical creatures within this tier’s categories",
  "time_manipulation": "Alter the local or subjective rate of time only to the degree, area, and duration this tier allows"
};

/** One complete level sentence: functional verbs + the known tier benchmark.
 * Earlier functional uses persist when advancing a tier; absent effects never
 * appear just because a numerical limit is high. */
function spPowerTierDescription(preset, level) {
  var tier=preset && preset.tiers && preset.tiers[String(level)];
  if (!tier) return '';
  // Telepathy is explicitly authored at each tier: sending thoughts is BASIC,
  // receiving willing thoughts is BASIC, involuntary probing starts at level 5.
  if(['telepathy','regeneration','illusion','shapeshifting','summoning'].indexOf(preset.id)>=0) return tier.benchmark.replace(/[.;]+$/,'')+'.';
  var core=SP_POWER_CORE_FUNCTIONS[preset.id] || preset.effect.replace(/[.;]+$/,'');
  var at=tier.benchmark.replace(/\s*\(conservative interim tier; numeric limits interpolated where possible\)/gi,'').replace(/[.;]+$/,'');
  if(tier.interpolated && tier.scopeAnchor) {
    // Intermediate examples remain within their earlier category; numeric caps
    // are separate authority. Do not invent capabilities from interpolation.
    return core+'; example at this level: '+at.charAt(0).toLowerCase()+at.slice(1)+'.';
  }
  return core+'; demonstrated tier benchmark: '+at.charAt(0).toLowerCase()+at.slice(1)+'.';
}

/** Read-only comparison; the catalog is NOT a registration or an authority grant. */
function spPowerLevelsText(preset) {
  var lines=['POWER LEVELS — '+preset.name, 'Effect: '+preset.effect,
    'Core uses persist as levels increase; numeric caps and listed exclusions remain binding.',
    'Select a tier with #POWER ADD '+preset.name+' <1-10>. Preview only; no registration. Interim tiers remain proposed calibrations.'];
  for(var i=1;i<=10;i++) {
    var tier=preset.tiers[String(i)];
    var cap=spCapStrings(spFriendlyCaps(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tier.caps||{})),!!tier.interpolated));
    lines.push('Level '+i+': '+spPowerTierDescription(preset,i)+' '+
      (cap.length?cap.join(', '):'Qualitative scope only')+'.');
  }
  lines.push('Exclusions: '+preset.exclusions);
  return lines.join('\n');
}

/** COPY one calibrated catalog tier into a new independent registered power object. */
function spPowerFromPreset(preset, level) {
  var tier = preset.tiers[String(level)];
  if (!tier) return null;
  var caps = spFriendlyCaps(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tier.caps || {})), !!tier.interpolated);
  var p = {
    id:preset.trigger, name:preset.name, kind:preset.id.toUpperCase().replace(/-/g,'_'),
    level:level, catalogType:'power', catalogId:preset.id, catalogVersion:SP_CATALOG_VERSION,
    status:'PROPOSED_CALIBRATION', trigger:preset.trigger,
    description:preset.effect + ' Level ' + level + ': ' + (['telepathy','regeneration','illusion','shapeshifting','summoning'].indexOf(preset.id)>=0 ? spPowerTierDescription(preset,level) : tier.benchmark.replace(/\s*\(conservative interim tier; numeric limits interpolated where possible\)/gi,'')),
    exclusions:preset.exclusions,
    limits:spCapStrings(caps).concat(['No unregistered effects; absent caps are NOT unlimited.']),
    caps:caps
  };
  if(typeof caps.massLb === 'number') p.maxWeightLb=caps.massLb;
  if(typeof caps.rangeFt === 'number') p.maxRangeFt=caps.rangeFt;
  return p;
}
/** COPY one of the pilot spell profiles into a registered spell object. */
function spSpellFromPreset(preset, level) {
  var p = {
    id:preset.trigger, name:preset.name, kind:preset.kind,
    level:level, baseLevel:preset.baseLevel, maxCastLevel:level,
    catalogType:'spell', catalogId:preset.id, catalogVersion:SP_CATALOG_VERSION,
    status:preset.status, trigger:preset.trigger,
    description:preset.description + (preset.scalingNote ? ' Scaling: '+preset.scalingNote : ''),
    usageExamples:preset.usageExamples || '',scalingNote:preset.scalingNote || '',
    exclusions:preset.exclusions, mechanics:preset.mechanics,
    limits:spCapStrings(preset.limits),caps:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(preset.limits))
  };
  if (typeof p.caps.massLb === 'number') p.maxWeightLb=p.caps.massLb;
  if (typeof p.caps.rangeFt === 'number') p.maxRangeFt=p.caps.rangeFt;
  if (preset.kind === 'FIREBALL') p.limits.push('20-ft radius; 8d6 base fire; +1d6 per spell slot above 3; Dexterity saving throw');
  if (preset.kind === 'MAGE_HAND') p.limits.push('Spectral hand cannot attack, activate magic items, or exceed 10 pounds');
  return p;
}
/** Present a short registration list. No changes to the saved registry. */
function spRegistryList(registry, catalogType) {
  return Object.keys(registry).sort().filter(function(id) {
    return !catalogType || registry[id].catalogType === catalogType;
  }).map(function(id) {
    var p = registry[id];
    var kind = p.catalogType === 'spell'
      ? ('D&D rank ' + p.baseLevel + ', maximum cast rank ' + p.maxCastLevel)
      : ('level ' + p.level + '/10');
    return '#' + id + ' — ' + p.name + ' (' + kind + ')';
  });
}

/**
 * Recognize ONLY administrative verbs, never ordinary ability invocations.
 * `#POWER ADD ...` is a command; `#pyrokinesis I conjure flame` is not.
 * Called before spMode1ParseInput in the Input hook.
 */
function spCommandParse(raw) {
  var s = String(raw || '').trim().replace(/^>\s*(?:you say\s*["“]?|you\s+)?/i, '').trim();
  // AI Dungeon may add speech quotation marks or final punctuation.
  s = s.replace(/["”]\s*$/, '').replace(/\.\s*$/, '').trim();
  // Canonical aliases; only recognized administrative commands are rewritten.
  // v1.1 canonical mode command. Legacy #POWER MODE and #mode still work.
  var gameMode=/^#game(?:[ _-]+)mode(?:[ _-]+(1|2|status))?$/i.exec(s);
  if(gameMode)return {group:'GAME',operation:'MODE',args:gameMode[1]||'STATUS'};
  var docs=/^#(?:game|power|surgeprotector|sp)(?:[ _-]+)(?:docs|documentation|repair(?:[ _-]+docs)?)$/i.exec(s);
  if(docs)return {group:'GAME',operation:'DOCS',args:'SYNC'};
  // Never rewrite arbitrary #ability triggers or natural-language actions.
  var a=/^#(power|spell)[_ -](add|custom|remove|list|details|levels|catalog|help|limit|cache|mode)\b[_ ]*(.*)$/i.exec(s);
  if(!a) {
    var b=/^#(list|add|remove|details|catalog|levels)[_ -](powers?|spells?)\b[_ ]*(.*)$/i.exec(s);
    if(b) a=[null,b[2].replace(/s$/i,'').toUpperCase(),b[1].toUpperCase(),b[3]];
    else {
      var short=/^#(powers|spells|help|mode)[_ ]*(.*)$/i.exec(s);
      if(short && ((short[1].toLowerCase()==='mode' && /^[12]|status$/i.test(short[2])) ||
        (short[1].toLowerCase()!=='mode' && !short[2]))) {
        a=[null,short[1].toLowerCase()==='spells'?'SPELL':'POWER',
          short[1].toLowerCase()==='powers'||short[1].toLowerCase()==='spells'?'LIST':
          short[1].toUpperCase(),short[2]];
      }
    }
  }
  var m=a || /^#(POWER|SPELL)\s+(ADD|CUSTOM|REMOVE|LIST|DETAILS|LEVELS|CATALOG|HELP|LIMIT|CACHE|MODE)\b\s*(.*)$/i.exec(s);
  if (!m) return null;
  return { group: m[1].toUpperCase(), operation: m[2].toUpperCase(), args: m[3].trim() };
}

// -------- SPELL CATALOG DISPLAY HELPERS (metadata only, NO new permissions) --------
/** Readable rank label for D&D spell ranks, which are NOT generic power tiers. */
function spSpellRankLabel(rank) { return rank === 0 ? 'Cantrips (rank 0)' : 'Rank ' + rank; }

/**
 * Display spell index organized by rank. Optional requested rank 0..9.
 * Marks registrable pilot profiles; other spells are references only.
 */
function spSpellCatalogText(rank) {
  var available = rank === null ? SP_SPELL_INDEX : SP_SPELL_INDEX.filter(function(p) {return p.baseLevel === rank;});
  var groups = [];
  for (var lv = 0; lv <= 9; lv++) {
    var spells = available.filter(function(p) {return p.baseLevel === lv;});
    if (!spells.length) continue;
    groups.push(spSpellRankLabel(lv) + ' (' + spells.length + ')');
    groups.push(spells.map(function(p) {
      return '  ' + p.name + (p.status === 'PILOT_RULES_PROFILE' ? ' *' : '');
    }).join('\n'));
  }
  return 'SPELL CATALOG — 100 D&D 2014 SRD spells (all registrable)\n' + groups.join('\n') +
    '\n* = Rules-guidance profile available. Spell slots, save rolls and concentration state are not auto-tracked.\n' +
    'Details: #SPELL DETAILS Haste | Filter: #SPELL CATALOG 3';
}

/**
 * Render a spell profile with distinct sections and one fact per line.
 * Only read SP_SPELL_INDEX metadata: inspecting a spell must never grant it.
 * Semicolon-delimited mechanics/exclusions become list lines so the output is
 * navigable on both desktop and mobile. These are descriptive rules, NOT a
 * claim that all spell resources or qualitative restrictions are JS-enforced.
 */
function spSpellDetailsText(p, registered) {
  var pilot = p.status === 'PILOT_RULES_PROFILE';
  var lines = [
    'SPELL — ' + p.name,
    'Base rank: ' + p.baseLevel + (p.baseLevel === 0 ? ' (cantrip)' : ' (D&D spell rank)'),
    'Activation: #' + p.trigger + (registered ? ' (registered)' : ' (after #SPELL ADD ' + p.name + ')'),
    'Status: ' + (pilot ? 'Registrable SRD rules-guidance profile (narrative enforcement; not a full spellcasting engine)' : 'Profile unavailable')
  ];
  if (registered) lines.push('Registered maximum: ' + registered.maxCastLevel);

  // Keep spell effect, mechanics, numeric limits, and narrative restrictions
  // visually separate rather than combining them into a long paragraph.
  lines.push('', 'EFFECT', '  ' + (p.description || p.effect || 'Not specified'));
  if (p.mechanics) {
    lines.push('', 'MECHANICS');
    String(p.mechanics).split(/;\s*/).filter(Boolean).forEach(function(part) {
      lines.push('  - ' + part.trim());
    });
  }
  var caps = p.limits ? spCapStrings(p.limits) : [];
  if (caps.length) {
    lines.push('', 'NUMERIC LIMITS');
    caps.forEach(function(limit) { lines.push('  - ' + limit); });
  }
  if (p.exclusions) {
    lines.push('', 'SCOPE / REQUIREMENTS');
    String(p.exclusions).split(/;\s*/).filter(Boolean).forEach(function(part) {
      lines.push('  - ' + part.trim());
    });
  }
  if (p.usageExamples) lines.push('', 'COMMON USES', '  '+p.usageExamples);
  if (p.scalingNote) lines.push('', 'CAST-RANK SCALING', '  '+p.scalingNote);
  lines.push('', 'TRACKING NOTE: Spell slots, class restrictions and spellcasting resources are not tracked.');
  return lines.join('\n');
}

/** Resolve a registered name/trigger/id; returns null on no match. */
function spRegisteredId(registry, raw) {
  var key=spKey(raw);
  return Object.keys(registry).filter(function(id){return spKey(id)===key || spKey(registry[id].name)===key || spKey(registry[id].catalogId)===key;})[0] || null;
}

/** Refresh unchanged built-in prose in existing saves, never numeric caps or custom entries.
 * A power whose description was modified by a user, script, or another tool
 * is intentionally left untouched. This is solely a documentation migration. */
function spRefreshCatalogDescriptions(registry) {
  if(!registry || typeof registry!=='object') return 0;
  var updates=0;
  Object.keys(registry).forEach(function(key){
    var p=registry[key];
    if(!p || p.catalogType!=='power' || !p.catalogId ||
       !Number.isInteger(p.level) || p.level<1 || p.level>10) return;
    var preset=spFindPreset(SP_POWER_PRESETS,p.catalogId);
    if(!preset || preset.trigger!==key || p.name!==preset.name) return;
    var tier=preset.tiers[String(p.level)];
    var oldDescription=preset.effect+' Level '+p.level+': '+tier.benchmark.replace(/\s*\(conservative interim tier; numeric limits interpolated where possible\)/gi,'');
    // Telepathy's old benchmarks differ from the revised catalog; use the exact
    // pre-alpha.19 descriptions for this pre-existing built-in only.
    if(preset.id==='telepathy') {
      var legacy={
        1:'Sense a willing nearby person’s surface thought',
        2:'Sense a willing nearby person’s surface thought',
        3:'Read one consenting mind clearly',
        4:'Read one consenting mind clearly',
        5:'Probe an unwilling mind if resistance fails',
        6:'Probe an unwilling mind if resistance fails',
        7:'Contact several minds across a city',
        8:'Contact several minds across a city',
        9:'Contact several minds across a city',
        10:'Link many willing minds across a region'
      };
      oldDescription=preset.effect+' Level '+p.level+': '+legacy[p.level];
    }
    if(p.description!==oldDescription) return;
    if(preset.id!=='telepathy') return; // retain compact existing registered definitions for other powers
    p.description=preset.effect+' Level '+p.level+': '+spPowerTierDescription(preset,p.level);
    updates++;
  });
  return updates;
}

/** Execute one management command and return OOC response. Only this mutates the registry via ADD/CUSTOM/LIMIT/REMOVE. */
function spExecCommand(sp, command) {
  var registry=sp.mode1.registry, args=command.args;
  function fail(msg){return 'SURGE PROTECTOR: '+msg;}
  if (command.operation==='HELP') return fail([
    'QUICK START',
    '#POWER CATALOG — built-in preset names; #POWER ADD Telekinesis 4 — register one.',
    '#POWER CUSTOM Minor Healing 2 | Touch heals scratches; cannot mend bones.',
    '  Use CUSTOM for abilities not listed in #POWER CATALOG.',
    '#POWER LIST — inspect registry; #POWER DETAILS tk — inspect limits.',
    '#POWER LEVELS Telepathy — compare every tier’s uses, caps and exclusions BEFORE registration.',
    '',
    'GAMEPLAY MODES',
    '#GAME MODE 1 — manual: use #tk <action> (default).',
    '#GAME MODE 2 — natural language; AI guidance is advisory, not guaranteed.',
    '#GAME MODE STATUS — show current mode.',
    '',
    'MANAGE',
    '#POWER REMOVE tk — remove power; #POWER LIMIT tk RANGE 30ft — change cap.',
    '#POWER CACHE ON|OFF|STATUS — precedent hints.',
    '#SPELL CATALOG [0-9] — available spell index; #SPELL DETAILS Fireball.',
    '#SPELL ADD Fireball 5 — 100 supported SRD spells; #SPELL LIST.',
    'Reference cards and index entries are NOT automatically registrable powers.',
    'For full instructions open the triggerless SurgeProtector Commands Story Card.'
  ].join('\n'));
  if (command.operation==='DOCS' && command.group==='GAME') {
    var docResult=spSyncReferenceCards(sp,true);
    return fail('Documentation sync: '+docResult.summary+'. Open Story Cards and confirm two cards of Type SurgeProtector. If no cards appear, check the [SP][DOCS] console log and whether another Adventure Script is replacing the Story Cards list.');
  }
  if (command.operation==='MODE') {
    if (command.group !== 'GAME' && command.group !== 'POWER') return fail('Use #GAME MODE 1, 2, or STATUS.');
    var requested = String(args || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (requested === '1' || requested === '2') {
      sp.settings.gameMode = Number(requested);
      sp.settings.m2AlphaEnabled = requested === '2';
      // Mode 1's explicit-trigger rule is unchanged. Mode 2 uses AI-led
      // guidance, not blanket permission to exercise unrelated powers.
      sp.settings.fallback = requested === '1' ? 'DENY' : 'PERMIT';
      if (sp.mode1) { sp.mode1.pendingInput = null; sp.mode1.pendingGeneration = null; }
    } else if (requested !== 'STATUS' && requested !== '') {
      return fail('Usage: #GAME MODE 1 | 2 | STATUS.');
    }
    return fail('Mode ' + sp.settings.gameMode + (sp.settings.gameMode === 1
      ? ' (explicit #ability activation; no-trigger guard active).'
      : ' (Mode 2 natural language; advisory guidance; no #ability required).'));
  }
  if (command.operation==='CACHE') {
    if (command.group !== 'POWER') return fail('Use #POWER CACHE ON, OFF, or STATUS.');
    var setting = args.toUpperCase().trim();
    if (setting === 'ON') sp.settings.precedentHintsEnabled = true;
    else if (setting === 'OFF') sp.settings.precedentHintsEnabled = false;
    else if (setting !== 'STATUS' && setting !== '') return fail('Usage: #POWER CACHE ON | OFF | STATUS.');
    return fail('Precedent example cache: ' + (sp.settings.precedentHintsEnabled ? 'ON' : 'OFF') +
      '. Advisory story hints only; mechanical authorization and diagnostics unchanged.');
  }
  if (command.operation==='LIST') {
    var items = spRegistryList(registry, command.group.toLowerCase());
    return fail(items.length ? 'Registered '+command.group.toLowerCase()+'s:\n'+items.join('\n') : 'No '+command.group.toLowerCase()+'s registered. Use #'+command.group+' ADD ...');
  }
  if (command.operation==='CATALOG') {
    if(command.group==='POWER') {
      var single=args?spFindPreset(SP_POWER_PRESETS,args):null;
      if(single)return fail(spPowerLevelsText(single));
      return fail('Power catalog (25): '+SP_POWER_PRESETS.map(function(p){return p.name;}).join(', ')+
        '. Preview ten levels with #POWER LEVELS Telepathy (or #POWER CATALOG Telepathy). Register with #POWER ADD Name Level or #POWER CUSTOM Name Level | Description.');
    }
    var rankText = args.trim();
    if (rankText && !/^[0-9]$/.test(rankText)) return fail('Use #SPELL CATALOG or #SPELL CATALOG 0 through 9.');
    return fail(spSpellCatalogText(rankText ? Number(rankText) : null));
  }
  if (command.operation === 'LEVELS') {
    if(command.group!=='POWER')return fail('Use #POWER LEVELS Telepathy.');
    var preview=spFindPreset(SP_POWER_PRESETS,args);
    return fail(preview?spPowerLevelsText(preview):
      'Not a built-in power: '+args+'. Use #POWER CATALOG or #POWER CUSTOM.');
  }
  if (command.operation === 'DETAILS') {
    // A spell can be inspected from the index WITHOUT being registered.
    if (command.group === 'SPELL') {
      var spellInfo = spFindPreset(SP_SPELL_INDEX, args);
      if (!spellInfo) return fail('Spell not found in index: ' + args + '. Try #SPELL CATALOG 3.');
      return fail(spSpellDetailsText(spellInfo, registry[spellInfo.trigger]));
    }
    var match = spRegisteredId(registry, args);
    if (!match || registry[match].catalogType !== 'power') {
      var builtIn=spFindPreset(SP_POWER_PRESETS,args);
      return fail(builtIn?spPowerLevelsText(builtIn):'Not registered: '+args+'. Use #POWER LEVELS Name to preview built-in tiers.');
    }
    var p = registry[match];
    return fail('POWER — ' + p.name + '\nActivation: #' + p.id + '\nLevel: ' + p.level + '/10' +
      '\nEffect: ' + p.description + '\nLimits: ' + p.limits.join('; ') +
      '\nExclusions: ' + (p.exclusions || 'No unrelated powers.'));
  }
  if(command.operation==='REMOVE') {
    var match=spRegisteredId(registry,args);
    if (!match || registry[match].catalogType !== command.group.toLowerCase()) return fail('Not registered: '+args);
    var name=registry[match].name; delete registry[match];
    // Explicit removal discards remembered examples for this trigger.
    // An eventual re-registration starts from a clean precedent cache.
    if(sp.mode1 && sp.mode1.precedents) delete sp.mode1.precedents[match];
    return fail('Removed '+name+' (#'+match+'). This trigger is now unauthorized.');
  }
  if(command.operation==='LIMIT') {
    if(command.group!=='POWER') return fail('Use #POWER LIMIT trigger RANGE 30ft or MASS 100lb.');
    var lm=/^(\S+)\s+(RANGE|MASS)\s+(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(ft|feet|lb|lbs)$/i.exec(args);
    if(!lm) return fail('Format: #POWER LIMIT tk RANGE 30ft or #POWER LIMIT tk MASS 100lb.');
    var id=spRegisteredId(registry,lm[1]);
    if(!id || registry[id].catalogType!=='power') return fail('Power not registered: '+lm[1]);
    var prop=lm[2].toUpperCase()==='RANGE'?'rangeFt':'massLb';
    var number=Number(lm[3]);
    if(!isFinite(number) || number<=0 || number>1e18) return fail('Limit must be a positive reasonable number.');
    var unit=lm[4].toLowerCase();
    if(prop==='rangeFt' && unit!=='ft' && unit!=='feet' || prop==='massLb' && unit!=='lb' && unit!=='lbs') return fail('Range requires ft and mass requires lb.');
    var p=registry[id]; p.caps=p.caps || {}; p.caps[prop]=number;
    if(prop==='rangeFt') p.maxRangeFt=number; else p.maxWeightLb=number;
    p.limits=spCapStrings(p.caps).concat(['No unregistered effects; absent caps are NOT unlimited.']);
    return fail('Updated #'+id+' '+p.name+' '+lm[2].toLowerCase()+' to '+number+(prop==='rangeFt'?'ft':'lb')+'.');
  }
  if (command.operation==='CUSTOM') {
    if(command.group!=='POWER') return fail('Custom spells are not supported: use #SPELL ADD from the indexed SRD spell list.');
    var parsed=/^(.+?)\s+(10|[1-9])\s*\|\s*(.{3,360})$/.exec(args);
    if(!parsed) return fail('Format: #POWER CUSTOM Shadow Step 5 | Teleport between visible shadows within 30 feet.');
    var display=parsed[1].trim(),level=Number(parsed[2]),effect=parsed[3].trim();
    var trigger=spKey(display);
    if (!/^[a-z][a-z0-9]{0,23}$/.test(trigger)) return fail('Use a short power name starting with a letter (max 24 trigger characters).');
    if(registry[trigger] && registry[trigger].catalogId !== 'custom:'+trigger) return fail('Trigger #'+trigger+' is already registered to '+registry[trigger].name+'.');
    var extracted=spExtractCustomCaps(effect), caps=extracted.caps;
    var profile={id:trigger,name:display,level:level,kind:'CUSTOM',catalogType:'power',
      catalogId:'custom:'+trigger,catalogVersion:SP_CATALOG_VERSION,status:'CUSTOM_USER_DEFINED',
      trigger:trigger,description:effect,exclusions:'No abilities unrelated to the user-defined effect are granted.',
      limits:spCapStrings(caps).concat(['No other abilities are granted.']),caps:caps};
    if(typeof caps.rangeFt==='number') profile.maxRangeFt=caps.rangeFt;
    if(typeof caps.massLb==='number') profile.maxWeightLb=caps.massLb;
    registry[trigger]=profile;
    spLog('CUSTOM LIMITS','trigger='+trigger+' auto='+JSON.stringify(caps)+' warnings='+extracted.warnings.length);
    return fail('Registered CUSTOM #'+trigger+' '+display+' level '+level+'/10. '+
      (spCompactCaps(caps) ? 'Auto-limits: '+spCompactCaps(caps)+'. ' : 'No numeric hard limits detected. ')+
      (extracted.warnings.length ? extracted.warnings.join('; ')+'. ' : '')+
      'Ready: #'+trigger+' <action>. View #POWER DETAILS '+trigger+' for scope.');
  }
  if(command.operation==='ADD') {
    if(command.group==='POWER') {
      var m=/^(.+?)\s+(10|[1-9])$/.exec(args);
      if(!m) return fail('Format: #POWER ADD Telekinesis 4 (power name followed by level 1–10).');
      var preset=spFindPreset(SP_POWER_PRESETS,m[1]);
      if(!preset) return fail('Unknown built-in power '+m[1]+'. #POWER ADD accepts only names from #POWER CATALOG (presets). To register another ability, use #POWER CUSTOM '+m[1]+' '+m[2]+' | Describe its effects AND limitations. Reference-card names alone are not presets.');
      var level=Number(m[2]), p=spPowerFromPreset(preset,level);
      var taken=registry[p.id];
      if (taken && taken.catalogId!==preset.id) return fail('Trigger #'+p.id+' already belongs to '+taken.name+'. No changes made.');
      if (taken && (taken.catalogType!=='power' || taken.catalogId!==preset.id)) return fail('Cannot overwrite an unrelated power.');
      registry[p.id]=p;
      return fail((taken?'Updated':'Registered')+' #'+p.id+' '+p.name+' level '+level+'/10. '+(spCompactCaps(p.caps) || 'Qualitative limits')+'. Ready: #'+p.id+' <action>. Use #POWER DETAILS '+p.id+' for the full description.');
    }
    var sm=/^(.+?)(?:\s+(0|[1-9]))?$/.exec(args);
    if(!sm) return fail('Format: #SPELL ADD Fireball or #SPELL ADD Fireball 5.');
    var spell=spFindPreset(SP_SPELL_INDEX,sm[1]);
    if(!spell) return fail('Unknown SRD spell '+sm[1]+'.');
    if(spell.status!=='PILOT_RULES_PROFILE') return fail(spell.name+' has no rules profile (catalog integrity problem); no registration performed.');
    var sl=sm[2]!=null?Number(sm[2]):spell.baseLevel;
    if(sl<spell.baseLevel || sl>9 || spell.baseLevel===0 && sl!==0) return fail(spell.name+' must use spell rank '+(spell.baseLevel===0?'0':spell.baseLevel+' through 9')+'.');
    var pp=spSpellFromPreset(spell,sl);
    if (registry[pp.id] && (registry[pp.id].catalogId!==spell.id || registry[pp.id].catalogType!=='spell')) return fail('Trigger #'+pp.id+' already belongs to another power.');
    registry[pp.id]=pp;
    return fail('Registered #'+pp.id+' '+pp.name+' (D&D 2014 spell rank '+spell.baseLevel+', max cast rank '+sl+').\n'+
      'Activation: #'+pp.id+(sl > spell.baseLevel ? ' (upcast as #'+pp.id+':'+sl+')' : '')+'\n'+
      'Effect: '+pp.description+'\n'+
      'Note: spell slots, classes and components are NOT tracked by this prototype.');
  }
  return fail('Unknown command; use #POWER HELP.');
}

// M2-01: Dual-purpose power cards
// SURGE PROTECTOR — M2-01 DUAL-PURPOSE POWER/SPELL CARDS (EXPERIMENTAL)
// Pure reference module for AI Dungeon Library; no state access, hooks, registry mutation,
// player-tag parsing or narration rewriting. The adapter below adds read-only candidates.
// Common-card prose explains the effect; structured fields index it. Neither grants power.
// Only the player's separately REGISTERED definition can supply authority and hard caps.
// No require/import/module.exports needed in AI Dungeon; uses shared Library APIs.

var SP_M2_CARD_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;

// Common dimensions define the machinery for describing registered hard limits.
// Their presence in a reference card DOES NOT impose a default numeric cap.
var SP_M2_DIMENSIONS = {
  massLb: ['Object mass', 'lb'], rangeFt: ['Range', 'ft'],
  areaRadiusFt: ['Area radius', 'ft'], volumeGallons: ['Volume', 'gallons'],
  durationSeconds: ['Duration', 'seconds'], maxTargets: ['Simultaneous targets', ''],
  speedMph: ['Speed', 'mph'], altitudeFt: ['Altitude', 'ft'],
  maxForms: ['Forms', ''], lookaheadSeconds: ['Lookahead', 'seconds'],
  thicknessFt: ['Thickness', 'ft'], severityRank: ['Severity ceiling', '']
};

// BEGIN GENERATED REFERENCE CARDS — run `node scripts/build-m2-cards.cjs`
var SP_M2_REFERENCE_CARDS = {
  "telekinesis": {
    "schemaVersion": 1,
    "id": "telekinesis",
    "type": "power",
    "name": "Telekinesis",
    "aliases": [
      "telekinesis",
      "psychokinesis",
      "tk"
    ],
    "family": "psychokinesis",
    "reviewStatus": "curated_reference",
    "provenance": "Surge Protector original editorial reference; character rules are individually registered.",
    "narrative": {
      "definition": "Telekinesis exerts physical force on external objects through mental effort, without needing to touch them. It may lift, pull, push, suspend, or reposition objects at a distance when the character has sufficient control.",
      "ordinary": "Moving a small, nearby object can be routine if that scale is within the character’s established ability.",
      "boundary": "This ability does not inherently read minds, teleport objects, transform matter, manipulate internal organs, or make its user fly. Extremely heavy or distant objects require correspondingly greater stated capability."
    },
    "structured": {
      "effects": [
        "external_object_movement"
      ],

SP_M2_COMMON_POWER_REFERENCES.forEach(function(card){
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(SP_M2_REFERENCE_CARDS,card.id))
    SP_M2_REFERENCE_CARDS[card.id]=card;

// A qualitative-only protection power has no numeric dimensions; this
// dimension is a descriptive audit category, NOT a manufactured cap.
SP_M2_REFERENCE_CARDS.invulnerability.constraintDimensions=['severityRank'];
// Tighten a generic phrase that otherwise mistakes "become a better person"
// for an intentional body transformation.
SP_M2_REFERENCE_CARDS.shapeshifting.identification.supporting =
  SP_M2_REFERENCE_CARDS.shapeshifting.identification.supporting.filter(function(x){return x!=='person';});
