requires normal verbal/somatic/material components."},"structured":{"effects":["spell_effect"],"actions":["cast"],"mechanisms":["named_spell"],"nonImplied":["other_spells"]},"identification":{"strong":["fireball"],"supporting":["cast","conjure","invoke","spell"]},"constraintDimensions":["rangeFt","areaRadiusFt"],"spellMetadata":{"ruleset":"D&D 5e SRD 5.1 (2014)","baseRank":3,"rulesAudit":"needs_audit","mechanicDimensions":["castingTime","components","concentration","duration","range","upcasting"]}}};
Object.keys(SP_M2_COMMON_SPELL_REFERENCES).forEach(function(id){
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(SP_M2_REFERENCE_CARDS,id))
    SP_M2_REFERENCE_CARDS[id]=SP_M2_COMMON_SPELL_REFERENCES[id];
});
// Spell profiles from the whole indexed catalog share the same mechanical-to-AI
// translator. Names are recognized when the spell is actually registered.
// Cards never authorize an unregistered spell. Spell Telekinesis shares a title
// with the power and therefore has a unique reference id (spell-telekinesis).
SP_SPELL_INDEX.forEach(function(p){
  if(p.status!=='PILOT_RULES_PROFILE')return;
  var id='spell-'+p.id;
  if(SP_M2_REFERENCE_CARDS[id])return;
  var words=p.name.toLowerCase();
  SP_M2_REFERENCE_CARDS[id]={
    schemaVersion:1,id:id,type:'spell',name:p.name,aliases:[p.name],
    family:'dnd2014_spell',reviewStatus:'curated_reference',
    provenance:'SRD 5.1 (2014) rules-guidance; only registered spell authorizes casting.',
    narrative:{definition:p.description,ordinary:p.effect||p.description,boundary:p.exclusions},
    structured:{effects:['spell_effect'],actions:['cast'],mechanisms:['named_spell'],nonImplied:['other_spells']},
    identification:{strong:[words],supporting:['cast','casting','spell','conjure','invoke']},
    constraintDimensions:Object.keys(p.limits||{}),
    spellMetadata:{ruleset:'D&D 2014 SRD 5.1',baseRank:p.baseLevel,rulesAudit:'rules_guidance',
      mechanicDimensions:['castingTime','components','concentration','duration','range','upcasting']}
  };
});



function spM2IsPlainObject(o) {
  return !!o && typeof o === 'object' && !Array.isArray(o);
}
function spM2String(s) { return typeof s === 'string' && s.trim().length > 0; }
function spM2WordList(items, slug) {
  if (!Array.isArray(items) || !items.length) return false;
  var seen = {};
  for (var i=0; i<items.length; i++) {
    if (!spM2String(items[i])) return false;
    if (slug && !/^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/.test(items[i])) return false;
    var key=items[i].trim().toLowerCase();
    if (seen[key]) return false;
    seen[key]=true;
  }
  return true;
}

/** Structural validation only. This does NOT certify factual/rules completeness. */
function spM2ValidateCard(c) {
  var errors = [];
  if (!spM2IsPlainObject(c)) return {ok:false, errors:['Card must be an object']};
  if (c.schemaVersion !== SP_M2_CARD_SCHEMA_VERSION) errors.push('schemaVersion');
  if (!spM2String(c.id) || !/^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]*$/.test(c.id)) errors.push('id');
  if (c.type !== 'power' && c.type !== 'spell') errors.push('type');
  if (!spM2String(c.name) || !spM2String(c.family)) errors.push('identity');
  if (!spM2WordList(c.aliases,false)) errors.push('aliases');
  if (['curated_reference','pilot_requires_audit','index_only_needs_audit'].indexOf(c.reviewStatus) < 0) errors.push('reviewStatus');
  if (!spM2String(c.provenance)) errors.push('provenance');
  if (c.registrationCues !== undefined && !spM2WordList(c.registrationCues,false)) errors.push('registrationCues');
  if (!spM2IsPlainObject(c.narrative) || !spM2String(c.narrative.definition) ||
      !spM2String(c.narrative.ordinary) || !spM2String(c.narrative.boundary)) errors.push('narrative');
  if (!spM2IsPlainObject(c.structured) ||
      !spM2WordList(c.structured.effects,true) ||
      !spM2WordList(c.structured.actions,true) ||
      !spM2WordList(c.structured.mechanisms,true) ||
      !spM2WordList(c.structured.nonImplied,true)) errors.push('structured');
  if (!spM2IsPlainObject(c.identification) ||
      !spM2WordList(c.identification.strong,false) ||
      !spM2WordList(c.identification.supporting,false)) errors.push('identification');
  if (!Array.isArray(c.constraintDimensions) || (c.type!=='spell' && !c.constraintDimensions.length) ||
      c.constraintDimensions.some(function(d){return !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(SP_M2_DIMENSIONS,d);}) ||
      c.constraintDimensions.length !== c.constraintDimensions.filter(function(d,i,all){return all.indexOf(d)===i;}).length)
      errors.push('constraintDimensions');
  // Spell metadata is an audit TO-DO, not a second source of enforceable rules.
  if (c.type === 'spell') {
    var m=c.spellMetadata;
    if (!spM2IsPlainObject(m) || !spM2String(m.ruleset) ||
        !Number.isInteger(m.baseRank) || m.baseRank<0 || m.baseRank>9 ||
        (m.rulesAudit!=='needs_audit' && m.rulesAudit!=='rules_guidance') || !spM2WordList(m.mechanicDimensions,false))
      errors.push('spellMetadata');
  } else if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(c,'spellMetadata')) errors.push('spellMetadataPower');
  // A generic reference is never a registered character-specific limit profile.
  if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(c,'caps')) errors.push('referenceCapsForbidden');
  return {ok:errors.length===0, errors:errors};
}

/** Link a generic reference to an ALREADY registered character power; no inference or grants. */
function spM2ResolveCard(c, registered) {
  var validation=spM2ValidateCard(c);
  if (!validation.ok) return {ok:false, reason:'INVALID_CARD'};
  if (!spM2IsPlainObject(registered) || !spM2String(registered.description) ||
      !spM2String(registered.name)) return {ok:false,reason:'NOT_REGISTERED'};
  // Prefer explicit provenance. Never let an alias override a contradictory
  // registered catalog identity or mix spells and powers.
  if (registered.catalogType && registered.catalogType!==c.type)
    return {ok:false,reason:'REFERENCE_MISMATCH'};
  var name=(registered.trigger||registered.id||'').toLowerCase();
  var catalog=registered.catalogId;
  var matches=(catalog===c.id || (c.type==='spell' && catalog==='spell-'+c.id) ||
    (c.type==='spell' && c.id==='spell-'+catalog)) ||
    ((!catalog || /^custom:/.test(catalog)) && registered.referenceCardId===c.id) ||
    (!catalog && c.aliases.indexOf(name)!==-1);
  // Only for curated cards with registrationCues: an EXACT custom name +
  // matching capability description may provide a READ-ONLY reference link.
  // Never infer from an alias, loose family resemblance, or a title alone.
  // Does not add or alter any power, cap or exclusion; player prose is authority.
  if (!matches && typeof catalog==='string' && /^custom:[a-z][a-z0-9]*$/.test(catalog) &&
      c.type==='power' && c.reviewStatus==='curated_reference' &&
      (!registered.referenceCardId || registered.referenceCardId===c.id) &&
      Array.isArray(c.registrationCues) &&
      spM2Tokens(registered.name).join(' ')===spM2Tokens(c.name).join(' ')) {
    var registeredWords=spM2Tokens(registered.description);
    matches=c.registrationCues.some(function(clue){
      return spM2PhraseIndex(registeredWords,clue)>=0;
    });
  }
  if (!matches) return {ok:false,reason:'REFERENCE_MISMATCH'};
  var caps = registered.caps===undefined ? {} : registered.caps;
  if (!spM2IsPlainObject(caps)) return {ok:false,reason:'INVALID_REGISTERED_CAPS'};
  var keys=Object.keys(caps);
  for (var i=0;i<keys.length;i++) {
    var k=keys[i],v=caps[k];
    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(SP_M2_DIMENSIONS,k) ||
        typeof v!=='number' || !isFinite(v) || v<0 || (v===0 && ['maxTargets','maxForms','severityRank','massLb'].indexOf(k)>=0) ||
        (['maxTargets','maxForms','severityRank'].indexOf(k)>=0 && Math.floor(v)!==v))
      return {ok:false,reason:'INVALID_REGISTERED_CAPS'};
  }
  if (registered.limits!==undefined && (!Array.isArray(registered.limits) ||
      registered.limits.some(function(x){return !spM2String(x);})) )
      return {ok:false,reason:'INVALID_REGISTERED_LIMITS'};
  return {
    ok:true, card:c, registered:registered,
    caps:caps, capKeys:keys,
    originalDescription:registered.description,
    originalExclusions:spM2String(registered.exclusions)?registered.exclusions:'',
    originalMechanics:spM2String(registered.mechanics)?registered.mechanics:'',
    originalSpellRank:Number.isInteger(registered.baseLevel)?registered.baseLevel:null,
    originalLimits:registered.limits||[]
  };
}

/** Human-readable numeric *registered* caps; never fills missing dimensions from catalog tiers. */
function spM2RegisteredCapLines(resolved) {
  var lines=[];
  for(var i=0;i<resolved.capKeys.length;i++){
    var k=resolved.capKeys[i], unit=SP_M2_DIMENSIONS[k];
    lines.push(unit[0]+' max '+resolved.caps[k]+(unit[1]?' '+unit[1]:''));
  }
  return lines;
}

/** Creates a nonbinding compact guide. If essential constraints do not fit, SKIP entirely. */
function spM2BuildCardGuide(card, registered, maxChars) {
  var r=spM2ResolveCard(card,registered);
  if (!r.ok) return {text:'',skipped:true,reason:r.reason};
  var max=maxChars===undefined?1600:maxChars;
  if (typeof max!=='number' || !isFinite(max) || max<0) return {text:'',skipped:true,reason:'INVALID_BUDGET'};
  var lines=[
    '[SP MODE 2: NONBINDING REFERENCE]',
    'Candidate: '+r.registered.name+' ('+card.type+'; reference '+card.name+').',
    'Narrative meaning: '+card.narrative.definition,
    'Ordinary use: '+card.narrative.ordinary,
    'Not inherently granted: '+card.narrative.boundary,
    'PLAYER DEFINITION (authoritative): '+JSON.stringify(r.originalDescription),
    'Hard limits: '+(r.capKeys.length ? spM2RegisteredCapLines(r).join('; ') : 'none specified; unspecified is not unlimited.'),
    'Other registered limits: '+(r.originalLimits.length?JSON.stringify(r.originalLimits):'none.'),
    'Exclusions: '+(r.originalExclusions?JSON.stringify(r.originalExclusions):'none.'),
    'Player definition takes precedence. Reference is not a grant or numeric cap.',
    'AI: Interpret action in context; allow plausible creative uses naturally; resist overreach, do not invent limits or unrelated effects.'
  ];
  for(var bi=0;bi<SP_M2_BOUNDARY_GUIDANCE.length;bi++) lines.push(SP_M2_BOUNDARY_GUIDANCE[bi]);
  if (card.type==='spell') {
    // Only the registered spell's own mechanics and rank are authoritative.
    if(r.originalMechanics)lines.splice(2,0,'Registered spell mechanics: '+JSON.stringify(r.originalMechanics));
    if(r.originalSpellRank!==null)lines.splice(2,0,'Registered spell rank '+r.originalSpellRank+'.');
  }
  if (card.reviewStatus!=='curated_reference')
    lines.splice(2,0,'AUDIT REQUIRED: this reference requires a rules audit; do not treat it as a complete spell rule profile.');
  var s=lines.join('\n')+'\n[/SP MODE 2 REFERENCE]';
  // If space is tight, remove only redundant generic examples; NEVER player rules.
  if(s.length>max) {
    var ordinary=lines.filter(function(x){return x.indexOf('Ordinary use: ')===0;})[0];
    if(ordinary) {
      lines.splice(lines.indexOf(ordinary),1);
      s=lines.join('\n')+'\n[/SP MODE 2 REFERENCE]';
    }
  }
  if(s.length>max) return {text:'',skipped:true,reason:'CONTEXT_BUDGET'};
  return {text:s,skipped:false,reason:'OK'};
}

// M2-02: Read-only power candidate retrieval
// ===============================================================
// SURGE PROTECTOR — MODE 2 M2-02: CANDIDATE RETRIEVAL (PURE)
// Requires M2-01 power-card module above this section in Library.
// NONBINDING HINTS ONLY. NO ACTIVATION, AUTHORIZATION, OR CONTEXT INJECTION.
// The AI storyteller remains responsible for intent and meaning.
// ===============================================================

/** Normalize a short action or catalog phrase into word tokens (no regex from input). */
function spM2Tokens(s) {
  if (typeof s !== 'string') return [];
  var normalized=s.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ').trim();
  return normalized ? normalized.split(/\s+/) : [];
}

/**
 * AI Dungeon records player Do actions in second person ("> You ... your ...").
 * Reference-card clues are normally first-person ("... my ..."). Normalize only
 * a player-directed action's possessive, and ONLY for retrieval tokens.
 * Do not change the original narration, input, or authority registry.
 */
function spM2ActionClueTokens(input) {
  var words=spM2Tokens(input);
  if (/^\s*>?\s*you\b/i.test(input)) {
    for (var i=0;i<words.length;i++) {
      if (words[i]==='your') words[i]='my';
    }
  }
  return words;
}

/** Return first word index of a phrase, or -1; no substring/"movie" = "move" errors. */
function spM2PhraseIndex(words, phrase) {
  var seq=spM2Tokens(phrase);
  if (!seq.length || seq.length>words.length) return -1;
  for (var i=0;i<=words.length-seq.length;i++) {
    var matches=true;
    for(var j=0;j<seq.length;j++) if(words[i+j]!==seq[j]){matches=false;break;}
    if(matches)return i;
  }
  return -1;
}

/** Distances between clue phrases are heuristic; an unrelated verb isn't proof of a power. */
function spM2ClueNear(words, strong, supporting, maxGap) {
  var sa=spM2Tokens(strong), sb=spM2Tokens(supporting);
  if (!sa.length || !sb.length) return false;
  for(var i=0;i<=words.length-sa.length;i++) {
    var a=true;
    for(var j=0;j<sa.length;j++)if(words[i+j]!==sa[j]){a=false;break;}
    if(!a)continue;
    for(var k=0;k<=words.length-sb.length;k++) {
      if(k>i+sa.length+maxGap || i>k+sb.length+maxGap)continue;
      var b=true;
      for(var n=0;n<sb.length;n++)if(words[k+n]!==sb[n]){b=false;break;}
      if(b)return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/**
 * Small SHARED semantic index: concept tags used in the structured cards map to
 * ordinary ways players describe mechanisms/effects. This is advisory search,
 * not a parser for supernatural authorization and not a source of power limits.
 * We deliberately omit bare "mind", "thought", "concentrate", and generic verbs
 * as mechanisms: these alone describe many mundane actions.
 * Add future concepts here only after a failing TDD case for a relevant card.
 */
var SP_M2_CONCEPT_CUES = {
  mechanisms: {
    mental_force: ['mentally', 'with a thought', 'through sheer concentration',
      'through focused concentration', 'by mental effort', 'with my will',
      'through force of will', 'with my mind', 'using my mind', 'use my mind to'],
    restorative_magic: ['channel healing energy', 'healing energy',
      'healing magic', 'restorative magic', 'channel restorative energy',
      'magically', 'with a healing touch'],
    cold_magic: ['cold magic', 'ice magic', 'freezing magic',
      'icy magic', 'magical cold', 'magically freeze', 'magically chill',
      'cryokinetically'],
    fire_magic: ['fire magic', 'flame magic', 'magical flame',
      'conjure a flame', 'conjure a little fire', 'summon fire',
      'channel magical flame', 'pyrokinetically']
  },
  effects: {
    external_object_movement: ['move', 'lift', 'push', 'pull', 'slide', 'shift',
      'raise', 'lower', 'levitate', 'float', 'floating', 'hover', 'hovering',
      'rotate', 'reposition'],
    minor_injury_care: ['heal', 'mend', 'close', 'repair', 'knit', 'reduce',
      'bruise', 'bruises', 'bruised', 'swelling', 'cut', 'cuts',
      'wound', 'wounds', 'injury', 'injuries', 'ribs',
      'regenerate', 'restore'],
    cooling_freezing: ['cool', 'chill', 'freeze', 'freezing', 'frost',
      'lower', 'heat', 'ice', 'water', 'puddle', 'cup', 'shape'],
    fire_ignition_heating: ['ignite', 'kindle', 'light', 'warm',
      'heat', 'soften', 'melt', 'burn', 'engulf', 'scorch',
      'set ablaze', 'set alight']
  }
};

/** Clues used as mechanism and effect evidence must occupy DIFFERENT words.
 * UX-01B: Allow up to 18 descriptive intervening words within a single
 * independently scoped action; do not scan across then/semicolon clauses.
 * Otherwise "ice magic" falsely supplies both the magic and its ice effect.
 * This applies to supernatural evidence only, not mundane-method overrides. */
function spM2ClueNearDistinct(words, strong, supporting, maxGap) {
  var sa=spM2Tokens(strong),sb=spM2Tokens(supporting);
  if(!sa.length || !sb.length) return false;
  for(var i=0;i<=words.length-sa.length;i++) {
    var ok=true;
    for(var j=0;j<sa.length;j++) if(words[i+j]!==sa[j]) {ok=false;break;}
    if(!ok) continue;
    for(var k=0;k<=words.length-sb.length;k++) {
      if((i<k+sb.length && k<i+sa.length) ||
         k>i+sa.length+maxGap || i>k+sb.length+maxGap) continue;
      var other=true;
      for(var t=0;t<sb.length;t++) if(words[k+t]!==sb[t]) {other=false;break;}
      if(other) return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/** A nearby explicit manual method defeats incidental mental wording for THAT movement. */
function spM2ManualMovementNear(words, effectClue) {
  var manual=['with my hands','with my hand','using my hands',
    'using my hand','by hand','with both hands'];
  for (var i=0;i<manual.length;i++) {
    if (spM2ClueNear(words,manual[i],effectClue,4)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/** A clear mundane ignition/heat method defeats an incidental fire-magic clue. */
function spM2MundaneFireNear(words, effectClue) {
  var methods=['with a match','using a match','use a match','with matches',
    'with a lighter','using a lighter','with flint and steel',
    'using flint and steel','with a torch','using a torch'];
  for (var i=0;i<methods.length;i++) {
    if (spM2ClueNear(words,methods[i],effectClue,5)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/**
 * Find one nearby conceptual mechanism + compatible effect from a CARD'S
 * structured tags. Neither clue alone is enough. Explicitly manual movement
 * outweighs weak mental wording near that movement (e.g. "mentally count ...
 * then lift with my hands"). We do not alter the original player action.
 */
function spM2FindConceptPair(words, card) {
  if (!card || !card.structured) return null;
  var mechanisms=card.structured.mechanisms||[];
  var effects=card.structured.effects||[];
  for (var mi=0;mi<mechanisms.length;mi++) {
    var mech=mechanisms[mi];
    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(SP_M2_CONCEPT_CUES.mechanisms,mech)) continue;
    var mechanismClues=SP_M2_CONCEPT_CUES.mechanisms[mech];
    for (var ei=0;ei<effects.length;ei++) {
      var effect=effects[ei];
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(SP_M2_CONCEPT_CUES.effects,effect)) continue;
      var effectClues=SP_M2_CONCEPT_CUES.effects[effect];
      for (var a=0;a<mechanismClues.length;a++) {
        for (var b=0;b<effectClues.length;b++) {
          if (!spM2ClueNearDistinct(words,mechanismClues[a],effectClues[b],18)) continue;
          if ((mech==='mental_force' && spM2ManualMovementNear(words,effectClues[b])) ||
              (mech==='fire_magic' && spM2MundaneFireNear(words,effectClues[b]))) continue;
          return {mechanism:mech,effect,
            mechanismClue:mechanismClues[a],effectClue:effectClues[b]};
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * M2-11: Narrow, effect-led retrieval when the player does not explicitly name
 * a magical mechanism. Rules are keyed by shared STRUCTURED EFFECT concepts,
 * NOT power names. A specific intentional verb plus a nearby physical outcome
 * is required: a mere mention of water, frost, fire, ice or movement is not.
 *
 * This index is intentionally sparse. New effects must earn rules through
 * failing positive AND mundane-control tests; generic "move"/"heal"/"heat"
 * alone must never be considered evidence of magic.
 */
var SP_M2_EFFECT_ONLY_RULES = {
  // M2-15: minor tissue repair is a tentative effect, not proof of magic.
  // Mundane first aid and natural recovery must defeat this weak match.
  minor_injury_care: [
    {verbs:['heal','mend','close','knit'],
     targets:['cut','cuts','wound','wounds','bruise','bruises','injury','injuries','laceration'],gap:8}
  ],
  cooling_freezing: [
    {verbs:['freeze','flash freeze','flash frozen','freeze solid','frost','frost over'],
     targets:['water','bowl','cup','glass','puddle','pond','pool','surface','metal','brass',
       'scales','chamber','room','floor','wall','walls','door','object','block','blocks',
       'guard','enemy','person','creature','foe','body','arm','hand',
       'fortress','building','city','lake','ocean'],gap:9},
    {verbs:['spread','extend','coat','cover','creep','creeping','bloom','form','create','grow'],
     targets:['frost','rime','ice crystals','crystalline ice'],gap:8},
    {verbs:['form','create','shape','conjure','summon','mold','sculpt','hurl','throw'],
     targets:['ice','icicle','icicles','frost'],gap:9},
    {verbs:['cool','chill','lower the temperature','lower its temperature'],
     targets:['water','drink','cup','glass','bowl','metal','scales','stone','room','chamber',
       'object','surface'],gap:8}
  ],
  fire_ignition_heating: [
    {verbs:['ignite','kindle','light','set alight','set ablaze'],
     targets:['candle','wick','kindling','tinder','paper','wood','torch','fire','flame','flames'],gap:8},
    {verbs:['warm','heat','soften','melt'],
     targets:['wax','sealing wax','candle','metal','stone','water','cup','object',
       'ice','frost','snow','icicle','icicles'],gap:8},
    {verbs:['conjure','form','create','shape','summon','produce'],
     targets:['fire','flame','flames','ember','embers'],gap:8}
  ],
  external_object_movement: [
    // Levitation without physical support is distinctive; "move/lift" is not.
    {verbs:['levitate','levitating','levitates','float','make float','make hover'],
     targets:['stone','rock','crate','box','block','chair','object','orb','coin',
       'feather','book','table','statue','boulder'],gap:8}
  ]
};

/** Methods that explain the same temperature/fire effect without a superpower. */
function spM2ExplicitMundaneEffectNear(words, effectId, action, target) {
  var methods=[];
  if(effectId==='minor_injury_care') methods=[
    'with stitches','using stitches','with sutures','using sutures',
    'with medicine','using medicine','with ointment','using ointment',
    'with a bandage','using a bandage','with bandages',
    'with first aid','using first aid','with antiseptic','using antiseptic',
    'with salve','using salve','with a poultice','using a poultice',
    'naturally','over several weeks','over several days'];
  if(effectId==='cooling_freezing') methods=[
    'in the freezer','in a freezer','using a freezer','use a freezer','use the freezer','with a freezer','with the freezer',
    'in a refrigerator','in the refrigerator','with ice cubes','using ice cubes',
    'with an ice pack','with a bag of ice','with dry ice','with liquid nitrogen',
    'using liquid nitrogen','using a machine','with a cooling machine'];
  if(effectId==='fire_ignition_heating') methods=[
    'with a match','using a match','use a match','with matches','with a lighter',
    'using a lighter','with flint and steel','using flint and steel',
    'with a torch','using a torch','with my hands','with your hands',
    'by hand','on the stove','over the stove','with a heating pad',
    'using a heater','with a blowtorch','using a blowtorch',
    'with hot water','using hot water','in my hands','in my bare hands',
    'over a campfire','at the hearth','on a radiator'];
  for(var i=0;i<methods.length;i++) {
    if(spM2ClueNear(words,methods[i],action,10) ||
       spM2ClueNear(words,methods[i],target,8)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/**
 * An observation of a phenomenon is not intentional power use. Questions and
 * negations are handled by the candidate retriever's flags (outside here).
 */
function spM2EffectOnlyObservation(input) {
  return /^\s*>?\s*(?:you|i)\s+(?:watch|watches|notice|notices|observe|observes|see|sees|look\s+at|inspect|inspects|discover|discover\s+that|find|finds)\b/i.test(input);
}

/** Distinguish ordering someone to freeze from freezing their body by power. */
function spM2OrdinaryFreezeCommand(input) {
  if (/\b(?:magic|magically|spell|cryokinesis|cryokinetically)\b/i.test(input)) return false;
  return /\b(?:tell|order|ask|command|instruct)\s+(?:(?:the|a|my|that)\s+)?(?:guard|enemy|person|creature|foe|man|woman)\s+to\s+freeze\b/i.test(input) ||
    /\b(?:shout|yell|say|cry)\s+["'“”]?freeze["'“”]?\s+(?:at|to)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:guard|enemy|person|foe)\b/i.test(input);
}

/** Return narrow, read-only evidence of a plausible power effect, if any. */
function spM2FindEffectOnlyPair(words, card, input) {
  if (!card || !card.structured || spM2EffectOnlyObservation(input)) return null;
  var effects=card.structured.effects || [];
  for(var ei=0;ei<effects.length;ei++) {
    var effect=effects[ei];
    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(SP_M2_EFFECT_ONLY_RULES,effect)) continue;
    var rules=SP_M2_EFFECT_ONLY_RULES[effect];
    for(var r=0;r<rules.length;r++) {
      for(var a=0;a<rules[r].verbs.length;a++) {
        var verb=rules[r].verbs[a];
        for(var b=0;b<rules[r].targets.length;b++) {
          var target=rules[r].targets[b];
          if(!spM2ClueNear(words,verb,target,rules[r].gap)) continue;
          if(spM2ExplicitMundaneEffectNear(words,effect,verb,target)) continue;
          if(effect==='cooling_freezing' &&
             ['guard','enemy','person','creature','foe','man','woman'].indexOf(target)>=0 &&
             spM2OrdinaryFreezeCommand(input)) continue;
          // Freezing in fear, frozen in place: "freeze" is not an ice effect.
          if(effect==='cooling_freezing' && /\b(?:freeze|frozen)\s+in\s+(?:fear|place|terror|shock)\b/i.test(input)) continue;
          return {effect:effect,actionClue:verb,targetClue:target};
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return null;
}


/** M2-16: distinctive common-power phrases, registry-only, with player-intent
 * safeguards. Non-exclusive phrases are intentionally absent. */
function spM2CommonSignature(segment, card, input) {
  var phrases=card && SP_M2_COMMON_SIGNATURES[card.id];
  if(!phrases || spM2EffectOnlyObservation(input) && card.id!=='precognition') return null;
  // Don't treat explicit negatives or speculation as an activation.
  if(/\b(?:do not|don't|cannot|can't|never|maybe|perhaps|might|what if|wonder)\b|\?/i.test(input))return null;
  // A physical pipe/hose is not hydrokinesis even when it contains "water".
  if(card.id==='hydrokinesis' && /\b(?:water pipe|water hose|garden hose)\b/i.test(segment))return null;
  var words=spM2ActionClueTokens(segment);
  for(var i=0;i<phrases.length;i++) {
    if(spM2PhraseIndex(words,phrases[i])>=0)return phrases[i];
  }
  // v1.0: generalized, distinctive natural forms observed in mixed-character
  // actions. These are retrieval clues ONLY, never permission to use a power.
  // Separate explicit negatives/hypotheticals and observation safeguards above
  // continue to apply before any of these patterns can nominate a card.
  var ordinary=String(segment || '');
  var familyPattern={
    telepathy:/\b(?:say|speak|whisper|reply|send|tell)\b.{0,90}\b(?:into|inside|through)\b.{0,55}\b(?:mind|thoughts)\b/i,
    hydrokinesis:/\b(?:bend|shape|redirect)\s+(?:the|a|some|this)\s+(?:(?:flowing|running|nearby|moving|fresh)\s+){0,2}(?:water|stream)\b/i,
    aerokinesis:/\b(?:summon|conjure|raise|create)\b.{0,30}\bgust\b.{0,18}\bwind\b/i,
    invisibility:/\b(?:turn|make|render)\s+(?:myself|yourself|herself|himself|themselves)\s+invisible\b/i,
    precognition:/\b(?:look|see|glimpse|peer)\b.{0,42}\b(?:seconds?|moments?)\b.{0,26}\b(?:future|ahead)\b/i,
    energy_projection:/\b(?:fire|shoot|project|launch)\b.{0,32}\b(?:blast|beam|bolt)\b.{0,24}\benergy\b/i
  };
  var pattern=familyPattern[card.id];
  if(pattern && pattern.test(ordinary)) {
    // Keep purely physical references out of magical-effect detection.
    if(card.id==='hydrokinesis' && /\b(?:water pipe|water hose|garden hose)\b/i.test(ordinary))return null;
    if(card.id==='telepathy' && /\b(?:my|your|own)\s+mind\b/i.test(ordinary) &&
       !/\b(?:his|her|their|the|someone|[a-z]+['’]s)\s+mind\b/i.test(ordinary))return null;
    return 'family action pattern';
  }
  return null;
}

/** M2-13: Resolve action segments without treating "and" as a scope break.
 * "Use Cryokinesis to ignite and frost" retains a single named mechanism;
 * "Use Cryokinesis to frost, then ignite" gives the latter action a new scope.
 * Purely advisory; this function does not decide whether any effect is allowed. */
function spM2ActionSegments(input) {
  return input.split(/\s*(?:[.;]|,?\s+(?:and\s+)?then\s+)\s*/i)
    .filter(function(part){return part.trim().length>0;});
}

/** Strong, *exclusive* mechanism clues plus registered names anchor a segment.
 * Effect-suggesting phrases ("conjure a flame") are deliberately excluded,
 * because they might coexist with an independently attempted cold effect. */
var SP_M2_EXPLICIT_MECHANISMS = {
  cold_magic:['cold magic','ice magic','freezing magic','cryokinetically'],
  fire_magic:['fire magic','flame magic','pyrokinetically'],
  mental_force:['telekinesis','psychokinesis','telekinetically'],
  restorative_magic:['healing magic','restorative magic','healing energy','restorative energy']
};
// Sentinel is an INTERNAL detection guard, never a candidate or a registered power.
// The registry remains the only authority; catalog names and explicit invocations
// are recognized here solely to prevent effect-based power substitution.
var SP_M2_UNREGISTERED_METHOD='__sp_m2_unregistered_method__';
/** Ordinary language often uses power/spell names as nouns or verbs.
 * "raise your shield", "whisper a message", "take the flight of stairs"
 * are not named magical invocations. Require a casting/activation cue; natural
 * effect recognition is handled separately by the card's action patterns. */
function spM2NameActionEligible(name, power, segment) {
  var bare=spM2Tokens(name).join(' ');
  var ambiguous={flight:1,illusion:1,healing:1,regeneration:1,
    invisibility:1,shield:1,message:1,light:1,fly:1,command:1,aid:1,slow:1};
  if(!ambiguous[bare])return true;
  var norm=spM2Tokens(segment).join(' ');
  var clue='(?:cast|casting|use|using|invoke|invoking|activate|activating|channel|channeling|wield|wielding|project|projecting|conjure|conjuring)';
  var match=new RegExp('\\b'+clue+'\\s+(?:(?:my|the|a|an)\\s+)?'+bare+'\\b','i');
  return match.test(norm) || (new RegExp('\\b'+bare+'\\s+(?:spell|power|ability)\\b','i')).test(norm);
}

function spM2SegmentAnchors(segment, registry, cards) {
  var words=spM2ActionClueTokens(segment),found=[];
  var knownNames=[];
  var registeredMechanisms=[];
  Object.keys(registry).forEach(function(id) {
    var p=registry[id];
    if(!spM2IsPlainObject(p) || !spM2String(p.name) || !spM2String(p.description)) return;
    var card=null,cardIds=Object.keys(cards);
    for(var ci=0;ci<cardIds.length;ci++) {
      if(spM2ResolveCard(cards[cardIds[ci]],p).ok){card=cards[cardIds[ci]];break;}
    }
    var names=[p.name];
    if(spM2String(p.trigger)) names.push(p.trigger);
    if(card) names=names.concat(card.aliases||[]);
    knownNames=knownNames.concat(names);
    var anchored=names.some(function(name){
      return spM2PhraseIndex(words,name)>=0 &&
        spM2NameActionEligible(name,p,segment) &&
        (spM2Tokens(name).join('').length>=3 ||
          (card && card.aliases.indexOf(name)>=0));
    });
    if(card && card.structured && card.structured.mechanisms) {
      card.structured.mechanisms.forEach(function(mech){
        if(registeredMechanisms.indexOf(mech)<0) registeredMechanisms.push(mech);
        if(!anchored) (SP_M2_EXPLICIT_MECHANISMS[mech]||[]).forEach(function(clue){
          if(spM2PhraseIndex(words,clue)>=0) anchored=true;
        });
      });
    }
    if(anchored) found.push(id);
  });
  var unknown=false;
  // A reference title can be *named* before it is registered. Reference cards
  // are still NEVER permissions. Ignore short ambiguous aliases (e.g. 'tk').
  Object.keys(cards).forEach(function(id){
    var c=cards[id],aliases=[c.name].concat(c.aliases||[]);
    for(var i=0;i<aliases.length;i++) {
      var name=aliases[i];
      if(spM2Tokens(name).join('').length<5 || spM2PhraseIndex(words,name)<0) continue;
      // Common reference names overlap ordinary vocabulary (e.g. "shield",
      // "healing", "light"). Never mark them as unregistered on a bare word.
      // The explicit "use <unregistered power> to ..." parser below remains
      // responsible for blocking deliberate invocation by name.
      if((c.type==='spell' && /^spell-/.test(c.id)) ||
         Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(SP_M2_COMMON_SIGNATURES,c.id) ||
         Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(SP_M2_COMMON_SPELL_REFERENCES,c.id))continue;
      if(!knownNames.some(function(registered){return spM2Tokens(registered).join(' ')===spM2Tokens(name).join(' ');}))
        unknown=true;
    }
  });
  // Explicit *mechanisms* also remain recognizable when no matching power is
  // registered: 'ice magic' cannot quietly nominate Pyrokinesis, and vice versa.
  Object.keys(SP_M2_EXPLICIT_MECHANISMS).forEach(function(mech){
    if(registeredMechanisms.indexOf(mech)>=0) return;
    (SP_M2_EXPLICIT_MECHANISMS[mech]||[]).forEach(function(clue){
      if(spM2PhraseIndex(words,clue)>=0) unknown=true;
    });
  });
  // Custom power names do not necessarily have reference cards. An explicit
  // invocation ('use Embercraft to...') must not be treated as an unstated
  // invitation to select some other registered power by the desired effect.
  // This intentionally also prevents speculative magical inference when a
  // player explicitly describes a mundane method such as 'use a match to...'.
  var pattern=/\b(?:use|using|invoke|invoking|channel|channeling|cast|casting|activate|activating|wield|wielding)\s+(?:(?:my|your|the|a|an)\s+)?([a-z][a-z0-9'_-]*(?:\s+[a-z][a-z0-9'_-]*){0,2}?)\s+to\b/gi;
  var match;
  while((match=pattern.exec(segment))!==null) {
    var invoked=spM2Tokens(match[1]).join(' ');
    // Do not mistake a REGISTERED supernatural mechanism (e.g. 'channel
    // healing energy to ...') for a separately named, unregistered power.
    // A mechanism with no registered owner is still rejected above.
    var registeredPhrase=registeredMechanisms.some(function(mech){
      return (SP_M2_EXPLICIT_MECHANISMS[mech]||[]).some(function(clue){
        return spM2Tokens(clue).join(' ')===invoked;
      });
    });
    if(!registeredPhrase && !knownNames.some(function(name){
      return spM2Tokens(name).join(' ')===invoked;
    })) unknown=true;
  }
  if(unknown)found.push(SP_M2_UNREGISTERED_METHOD);
  return found;
}

/** Find advisory *possibilities* ONLY among actually registered character capabilities.
 *
 * Return values MUST NOT be used as authorization decisions. The storyteller can ignore
 * all hints, interpret hypotheticals, or identify a power that this retriever misses.
 * M2-03 can call spM2BuildCardGuide(card, registry[trigger]) for the authoritative text.
 */
function spM2FindCandidates(playerAction, registry, maxResults) {
  var out={candidates:[],flags:{speculative:false,negated:false}};
  if(typeof playerAction!=='string' || !playerAction.trim() ||
     !spM2IsPlainObject(registry))return out;
  var input=playerAction.slice(0,5000);
  var words=spM2ActionClueTokens(input);
  if(!words.length)return out;
  out.flags.speculative=/\b(?:wonder|consider|considering|imagine|imagining|perhaps|maybe)\b|\b(?:could|can|should)\s+i\b|\bwould\s+it\b|\bwhat\s+if\b|\bif\s+i\b|\?/i.test(input);
  out.flags.negated=/\b(?:do\s+not|don['’]?t|never|can['’]?t|cannot|won['’]?t)\b/i.test(input);
  // A purely hypothetical player statement is not an ability activation.
  // Scope this to the opening clause to avoid suppressing later real actions.
  if(/^\s*>?\s*(?:you|i)\s+(?:wonder|speculate|consider|imagine)\b/i.test(input) &&
     !/\b(?:and then|then)\s+(?:you|i)\s+(?:use|cast|activate|channel|project|try)\b/i.test(input))
    return out;
  var cards=SP_M2_REFERENCE_CARDS;
  // M2-15: a continuation after 'then' may omit 'You' but still belongs
  // to the player. Preserve second-person possessives for clue retrieval;
  // the raw text and actual narration are never rewritten.
  var playerDirected=/^\s*>?\s*you\b/i.test(input);
  var segments=spM2ActionSegments(input).map(function(part) {
    var cluePart=playerDirected && !/^\s*>?\s*(?:you|i)\b/i.test(part)?
      'You '+part:part;
    return {text:part,words:spM2ActionClueTokens(cluePart),
      anchors:spM2SegmentAnchors(cluePart,registry,cards)};
  });
  out.flags.unregisteredMethod=segments.some(function(segment){
    return segment.anchors.indexOf(SP_M2_UNREGISTERED_METHOD)>=0;
  });
  var entries=Object.keys(registry);
  for(var n=0;n<entries.length;n++){
    var trigger=entries[n], power=registry[trigger];
    if(!spM2IsPlainObject(power) || !spM2String(power.name) ||
       !spM2String(power.description))continue;
    var names=[power.name];
    if(spM2String(power.trigger)) names.push(power.trigger);
    var card=null;
    var cardIds=Object.keys(cards);
    for(var ci=0;ci<cardIds.length;ci++){
      var ref=cards[cardIds[ci]];
      if(spM2ResolveCard(ref,power).ok){card=ref;break;}
    }
    var named=null;
    if(card)names=names.concat(card.aliases);
    for(var ni=0;ni<names.length;ni++){
      if(spM2PhraseIndex(words,names[ni])>=0 &&
         spM2NameActionEligible(names[ni],power,input) &&
         // Don't use very short generated triggers as evidence without catalog alias.
         (spM2Tokens(names[ni]).join('').length>=3 ||
          (card && card.aliases.indexOf(names[ni])>=0))) {
        named=names[ni];break;
      }
    }
    // Reading or describing a magical phenomenon is not activating it.
    if(!named && /^\s*>?\s*(?:you|i)\s+(?:describe|discuss|study|read about|talk about|remember|recall)\b/i.test(input))continue;
    var evidence=[],score=0,source='registered_name';
    if(named){evidence.push('registered name/alias: '+named);score=12;}
    if(card){
      // Mechanism+effect pairs must be in the SAME independently scoped action.
      // A name or method absent from the registry also blocks weak attribution
      // to some other power in that segment. Explicit named matches still win.
      var strong=card.identification.strong, support=card.identification.supporting;
      for(var segi=0;segi<segments.length && !score;segi++) {
        var scope=segments[segi],scopeWords=scope.words;
        if(scope.anchors.length && scope.anchors.indexOf(trigger)<0) continue;
        for(var si=0;si<strong.length && !score;si++){
          if(spM2PhraseIndex(scopeWords,strong[si])<0)continue;
          for(var pi=0;pi<support.length;pi++){
            if(spM2PhraseIndex(scopeWords,support[pi])<0)continue;
            if(spM2ClueNearDistinct(scopeWords,strong[si],support[pi],18)){
              if((card.structured.mechanisms.indexOf('mental_force')>=0 &&
                 spM2ManualMovementNear(scopeWords,support[pi])) ||
                 (card.structured.mechanisms.indexOf('fire_magic')>=0 &&
                 spM2MundaneFireNear(scopeWords,support[pi]))) continue;
              evidence.push('mechanism: '+strong[si]);
              evidence.push('action: '+support[pi]);
              score=8;source='reference_card';break;
            }
          }
        }
      }
      if(!score){
        for(var csi=0;csi<segments.length;csi++) {
          var conceptSegment=segments[csi];
          if(conceptSegment.anchors.length && conceptSegment.anchors.indexOf(trigger)<0) continue;
          var concept=spM2FindConceptPair(conceptSegment.words,card);
          if(concept){
            evidence.push('mechanism concept: '+concept.mechanism+
              ' ('+concept.mechanismClue+')');
            evidence.push('effect concept: '+concept.effect+
              ' ('+concept.effectClue+')');
            score=6;source='concept_pair';break;
          }
        }
      }
      // M2-16: strong natural phrases may be sufficient even when no separate
      // generic mechanism word is present (e.g. 'turn into a wolf').
      if(!score && !out.flags.speculative && !out.flags.negated) {
        for(var cs=0;cs<segments.length;cs++) {
          var sigSegment=segments[cs];
          if(sigSegment.anchors.length && sigSegment.anchors.indexOf(trigger)<0)continue;
          var signature=spM2CommonSignature(sigSegment.text,card,sigSegment.text);
          if(signature){score=5;source='common_signature';
            evidence.push('distinctive registered action: '+signature);break;}
        }
      }
      // M2-11: lower-confidence, effect-only inference. Requires a registered
      // matching card plus an action/phenomenon pair, with mundane overrides.
      // Questions/negations remain flags but cannot generate this weak candidate.
      if(!score && !out.flags.speculative && !out.flags.negated) {
        var effectPair=null;
        for(var sp=0;sp<segments.length;sp++) {
          var segment=segments[sp];
          // Never silently substitute another registered power when the
          // player explicitly named a different power/mechanism for this act.
          if(segment.anchors.length && segment.anchors.indexOf(trigger)<0) continue;
          effectPair=spM2FindEffectOnlyPair(segment.words,card,segment.text);
          if(effectPair) break;
        }
        if(effectPair){
          evidence.push('possible intentional effect: '+effectPair.effect);
          evidence.push('action: '+effectPair.actionClue+' / '+effectPair.targetClue);
          score=4;source='effect_pair';
        }
      }
    }
    if(!score)continue;
    out.candidates.push({
      trigger:trigger,name:power.name,cardId:card?card.id:null,
      source:source,score:score,confidence:score>=12?'strong':'possible',
      suggestionOnly:true,auditRequired:!!card && card.reviewStatus!=='curated_reference',
      evidence:evidence
    });
  }
  // Repeatable order; prefer specific names when both named and mechanism-only cues match.
  out.candidates.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score || a.trigger.localeCompare(b.trigger);});
  var count=(typeof maxResults==='number' && isFinite(maxResults))?
    Math.max(0,Math.min(3,Math.floor(maxResults))):2;
  out.candidates=out.candidates.slice(0,count);
  return out;
}

/**
 * M2-12: Only explanatory, read-only storyteller guidance. Never infer
 * registration, enlarge a power or turn the heuristic into authorization.
 * These checks must apply equally to FULL and COMPACT candidate packets.
 */
var SP_M2_BOUNDARY_GUIDANCE = [
  'Same element does not grant new weapons, immunity or secondary powers; each effect must be supported by registration.',
  'Limit or fail excess size, targets or effects while preserving allowed parts.',
  'Range limits active magic, not later physical motion: a thrown object need not dissolve past range absent upkeep.',
  'Do not invent out-of-range distances for successful magic; effects beyond range must fail.'
];

// ================================================================
// MODE 2 M2-03: CONTEXT HINT ASSEMBLY (pure, advisory, budgeted)
// No candidate, generic reference, or cached example can grant a power.
// Never abbreviate a registered limitation to satisfy a token budget.
// ================================================================
function spM2CompactRegisteredGuide(power, candidate) {
  if (!spM2IsPlainObject(power) || !spM2String(power.description)) return '';
  var caps=spM2IsPlainObject(power.caps) ? power.caps : {};
  var capLines=[];
  Object.keys(caps).forEach(function(key){
    var value=caps[key];
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(SP_M2_DIMENSIONS,key) &&
        typeof value==='number' && isFinite(value)) {
      var detail=SP_M2_DIMENSIONS[key];
      capLines.push(detail[0]+' max '+value+(detail[1]?' '+detail[1]:''));
    } else {
      // Preserve an unfamiliar registered field exactly; don't assume it is a maximum.
      capLines.push(key+'='+JSON.stringify(value));
    }
  });
  var lines=[
    '[SP MODE 2: REGISTERED AUTHORITY]',
    'Possible power: '+power.name+' (suggestion, NOT confirmed activation).',
    'PLAYER DEFINITION (authoritative): '+JSON.stringify(power.description),
    'Hard limits: '+(capLines.length ? capLines.join('; ') : 'none explicitly numeric; unspecified is NOT unlimited.'),
    'Other registered limits: '+JSON.stringify(Array.isArray(power.limits)?power.limits:[]),
    'Exclusions: '+JSON.stringify(power.exclusions||'No unregistered abilities.')
  ];
  if (spM2String(power.mechanics)) lines.push('Registered mechanics: '+JSON.stringify(power.mechanics));
  if (Number.isInteger(power.baseLevel)) lines.push('Registered base spell level: '+power.baseLevel);
  lines.push('Player definition governs; do not invent limits or grant unrelated effects.',
    '[/SP MODE 2 REGISTERED AUTHORITY]');
  return lines.join('\n');
}

/**
 * M2-06.1: authoritative power information is mandatory *when it fits*;
 * generic narrative descriptions are optional. First attempt all full cards.
 * If that cannot fit, use complete registered-authority packets for ALL
 * candidates that fit, rather than spending the budget on one verbose card.
 * Never truncate or abbreviate the registered definition/constraints.
 */
function spM2BuildContextHints(actionText, registry, maxChars) {
  var max=maxChars===undefined ? 2400 : maxChars;
  if (typeof max!=='number' || !isFinite(max) || max<0 || max>10000 ||
      !spM2IsPlainObject(registry))
    return {text:'',skipped:true,reason:'INVALID_INPUT',used:0,candidates:0,omitted:0,coverage:'NONE'};
  var found=spM2FindCandidates(actionText,registry,3);
  var lines=[
    '[SURGE PROTECTOR / MODE 2 / STORYTELLER GUIDANCE]',
    'Candidates are tentative. Preserve attempted method, target and sequence; do not substitute other powers or opposite effects.',
    'Only registered descriptions authorize effects; enforce all numeric and qualitative limits.',
    'References grant no powers; creative effects must stay in scope.',
    'No script notices or rule text in prose.'
  ];
  if (Object.keys(registry).length>1 && found.candidates.length)
    lines.push('Assess each power separately; never borrow other powers or their limits.');
  // M2-12: each boundary rule is included ONCE for all candidates. Keep
  // per-power packets small so two full sets of registered limits can fit.
  if (found.candidates.length) {
    for(var bi=0;bi<SP_M2_BOUNDARY_GUIDANCE.length;bi++) lines.push(SP_M2_BOUNDARY_GUIDANCE[bi]);
  }
  // M2-13c: This warning must accompany MIXED actions as well as no-candidate actions.
  // An explicit unregistered method was previously diagnosed but not included in
  // Context when another, independent registered candidate was present.
  if (found.flags.unregisteredMethod)
    lines.push('UNREGISTERED METHOD: Any supernatural method explicitly invoked but absent from the CURRENT registry MUST FAIL completely; do not narrate new frost, sparks, residual or partial magical effects from it. Prior narrative success does not confer a removed power. Independently attempted registered effects may proceed. Mundane tools are unaffected.');
  if (found.candidates.some(function(c){return c.source==='effect_pair';}))
    lines.push('An effect-only match is tentative: no supernatural method was stated. Rule out ordinary physical means before treating it as a power attempt.');
  if (found.flags.speculative) lines.push('POSSIBLE QUESTION / HYPOTHETICAL: do not assume the player attempted or performed this effect.');
  if (found.flags.negated) lines.push('POSSIBLE NEGATION: do not assume this is an activation or an action taken.');
  if (!found.candidates.length) {
    lines.push('No confident registered candidate retrieved; this is not proof of no power use. Check the original action against ALL registered descriptions. Do not infer an unregistered effect.');
    lines.push('For an attempted supernatural effect unsupported by any registered power, do not narrate success: show a failed attempt or an ordinary alternative. Equipment, devices, and ordinary physical actions are outside power-registration enforcement.');
    if(Object.keys(registry).some(function(k){return registry[k].catalogId==='shapeshifting';}))
      lines.push('A form already established through registered Shapeshifting may use ordinary anatomy, senses, and movement of that creature as mundane actions; do not invent extra supernatural abilities.');
    var inventory=Object.keys(registry).map(function(k){
      var p=registry[k];return spM2IsPlainObject(p)&&spM2String(p.name)?p.name:null;
    }).filter(Boolean);
    var concise=inventory.join('; ');
    if (!inventory.length) lines.push('Registered powers: none. Do not grant character powers absent from the registry.');
    else if(concise.length<=320) lines.push('Registered powers: '+concise+'. Names alone do not expand their definitions.');
    else lines.push('Registered power inventory omitted (too large for concise Context); do not assume these are the only abilities.');
  }
  var close='[/SURGE PROTECTOR MODE 2]';
  var content=lines.join('\n');
  if (content.length+1+close.length>max)
    return {text:'',skipped:true,reason:'CONTEXT_BUDGET',used:0,flags:found.flags,
      candidates:found.candidates.length,omitted:found.candidates.length,coverage:'NONE'};
  var guides=[];
  var fullGuides=[];
  for(var i=0;i<found.candidates.length;i++) {
    var c=found.candidates[i], power=registry[c.trigger];
    if (!spM2IsPlainObject(power)) continue;
    var compact=spM2CompactRegisteredGuide(power,c);
    if (!compact) continue;
    var card=c.cardId && SP_M2_REFERENCE_CARDS[c.cardId];
    var full='';
    if(card) {
      var result=spM2BuildCardGuide(card,power,2400);
      if(!result.skipped) full=result.text;
    }
    guides.push(compact);
    fullGuides.push(full||compact);
  }
  function fitsAll(packets) {
    var total=content.length+1+close.length;
    for(var k=0;k<packets.length;k++) total+=2+packets[k].length;
    return total<=max;
  }
  var toSend=[];
  var coverage='NONE';
  if (guides.length && fitsAll(fullGuides)) {
    toSend=fullGuides;
    coverage='FULL';
  } else if (guides.length && fitsAll(guides)) {
    toSend=guides;
    coverage='COMPACT';
  } else if (guides.length) {
    // Budget insufficient for all. Include only complete authority packets.
    // Never choose a full card while other registered limitations are missing.
    // Reserve room to tell the narrator what was NOT provided; missing
    // restrictions must not be mistaken for permission.
    var warningReserve=Math.min(155,65+found.candidates.map(function(c){return c.name.length;})
      .reduce(function(a,b){return a+b;},0));
    var available=max-content.length-1-close.length-warningReserve;
    for(var j=0;j<guides.length;j++) {
      var needed=2+guides[j].length;
      if (needed<=available) {
        toSend.push(guides[j]);
        available-=needed;
      }
    }
    if(toSend.length) coverage='PARTIAL';
  }
  for(var n=0;n<toSend.length;n++) content+='\n\n'+toSend[n];
  if(toSend.length<found.candidates.length) {
    var withheld=found.candidates.slice(toSend.length).map(function(c){return c.name;});
    var caution='AUTHORITY WITHHELD for '+withheld.join(', ')+
      ': registered limits not supplied. Do NOT treat these candidate effects as authorized.';
    if(content.length+1+caution.length+1+close.length<=max) content+='\n'+caution;
    else if(content.length+64+close.length<=max)
      content+='\nSome registered authority omitted: never infer permission.';
  }
  content+='\n'+close;
  var omitted=found.candidates.length-toSend.length;
  return {text:content,skipped:false,reason:omitted?'AUTHORITY_OMITTED':
    (toSend.length?'OK':'GENERAL_ONLY'),used:toSend.length,
    flags:found.flags,candidates:found.candidates.length,omitted:omitted,coverage:coverage};
}
